# Verification Matrix — Acceptance Criteria by Component

This document translates the critical gating criteria from §11 of `ARCHITECTURE.md` into an actionable checklist for each component. A component is deemed **complete** only when ALL its criteria are validated.

---

## Global Security Invariants (Mandatory for every release)

- [x] No code path ever converts an error or failure into `PAM_SUCCESS` (checked by `crates/pam/src/lib.rs` and `crates/protocol`)
- [x] No camera device is opened by the PAM module (PAM strictly delegates via IPC; `quality_followups::test_pam_crate_never_opens_camera_devices`)
- [x] Daemon unavailable = standard password fallback works (`authenticate_returns_pam_ignore` in `crates/pam/src/lib.rs`, `ipc_tests::test_ipc_offline_daemon_returns_ignore`, `pam_bindings_tests::test_pam_hooks_authenticate_offline_daemon_returns_ignore`)
- [x] The `.so` never panics across FFI (enforced by `catch_unwind`; `ipc_tests::test_c_abi_all_entry_points_panic_safe`, `test_release_profile_unwinds_so_pam_catch_unwind_is_effective`, `test_pam_docker_suite_proves_release_panic_returns_pam_ignore`)
- [x] Each ONNX model is attested by manifest + SHA-256 checksum (`manifest_tests::test_parse_workspace_manifest_file`, `manifest_tests::test_verify_model_checksum_success_and_tamper_detection`, `registry_tests::test_registry_verify_integrity_missing_files_fails_closed`)
- [x] All biometric templates and evidence are located outside `$HOME` and inaccessible to non-root accounts (`test_install_script_creates_required_directories`, mode `0700` `root:root`)
- [x] Each target distribution integration is validated with a documented rollback procedure (`test_pam_config_ordering_matches_spec`, `test_uninstall_restores_pam_config`)

---

## Component: `protocol`

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | Types `Request`, `Response`, `Verdict` are serializable and deserializable | Round-trip tests (`crates/protocol/src/codec.rs` unit tests `request_roundtrip`, `response_roundtrip`, `event_roundtrip`; `property_tests::prop_request_roundtrip`, `property_tests::prop_response_roundtrip`) | ☑ Validated |
| P2 | Codec enforces maximum 4,096-byte message boundary | Oversized payload rejection test (`crates/protocol/src/codec.rs` unit test `rejects_declared_size_too_large`; `property_tests::prop_declared_size_bounds`) | ☑ Validated |
| P3 | `request_id` is exactly 256 bits (32 bytes) | Serialization test (`crates/protocol/src/codec.rs` unit test `request_id_is_256_bits`) | ☑ Validated |
| P4 | Protocol is versioned (`version` field) | v1 compatibility test (`crates/protocol/src/codec.rs` unit tests `rejects_unsupported_version`, `response_is_allow_only_with_current_version`) | ☑ Validated |
| P5 | Decoder fuzzing: zero panic on arbitrary inputs | Property tests and libFuzzer harness (`prop_decode_request_never_panics`, `prop_decode_response_never_panics`, `cargo-fuzz`) | ☑ Validated |
| P6 | `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` enabled | Invariant test (`test_business_crates_forbid_unsafe_code`) | ☑ Validated |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PO1 | `Allow` decision only if score >= threshold AND PAD positive AND valid UID | Parametric unit tests (`decision_tests::test_decision_allow_nominal`, `prop_decision_allow_invariant`) | ☑ Validated |
| PO2 | Per-UID rate-limiting is enforced | Request burst test (`rate_limit_tests::test_rate_limit_burst_same_uid`) | ☑ Validated |
| PO3 | Zero I/O inside crate | Dependency audit (`crates/policy/Cargo.toml` contains zero filesystem, network, or async deps) | ☑ Validated |
| PO4 | `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` enabled | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::test_business_crates_forbid_unsafe_code`) | ☑ Validated |
| POL1 | Decision engine explicitly rejects `f32::INFINITY` scores | Unit tests (`decision_tests::test_decision_deny_score_infinity`, `decision_tests::test_decision_deny_score_neg_infinity`) | ✅ Verified |
| POL2 | RateLimiter evicts stale UIDs and maintains capacity bounds | Unit tests (`rate_limit_tests::test_rate_limit_capacity_bounds_and_lru_eviction`, `rate_limit_tests::test_rate_limit_stale_uid_eviction_on_capacity`, `rate_limit_tests::test_rate_limit_default_capacity`, `rate_limit_tests::test_rate_limit_zero_capacity_fails_closed`) | ✅ Verified |

| PA14 | Telemetry `PasswordFailed` event payload includes target `uid` for evidence snapshot attribution | Unit & integration tests (`ipc_tests::test_password_failed_event_includes_uid`, `pipeline_integration_tests::test_12_4_password_failed_event_captures_evidence_snapshot`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `biometric-store`

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | Embeddings encrypted at rest | `encryption_tests::test_b1_*`, `proptest_suite::prop_encrypt_decrypt_roundtrip` | ☑ Validated |
| B2 | Files under `/var/lib/soos/biometrics/<uid>`, mode `0600`, owner `root:root` | `permissions_tests::test_b2_permissions_and_atomic_writes`, `test_b2_master_key_file_permissions` | ☑ Validated |
| B3 | `model_id` and version stored with each template | `metadata_tests::test_b3_metadata_tracking_and_model_migration_support` | ☑ Validated |
| B4 | Template deletion and re-enrollment operational | `crud_tests::test_b4_full_crud_lifecycle` | ☑ Validated |
| B5 | Master key created atomically with mode `0600` from inception (`O_CREAT \| O_EXCL`) | `permissions_tests::test_master_key_created_with_0600_from_inception` | ✅ Verified |
| B6 | Best-effort in-place overwrite on template deletion (3-pass CSPRNG overwrite + `fsync` before unlinking). Scope corrected by ADR 2026-09-30 "Biometric Template Erasure Model" (GitHub #179): the test proves the inode content is overwritten, not that the data is unrecoverable on CoW/journaled filesystems or flash media; the recoverability guarantee is B1 encryption plus master-key destruction | `crud_tests::test_delete_securely_overwrites_before_unlink` | ✅ Verified |
| B7 | Symlink traversal prevention on template and key paths | `crud_tests::test_biometric_store_rejects_symlink_template_path`, `permissions_tests::test_master_key_created_with_0600_from_inception` | ✅ Verified |
| B8 | `BiometricStore::new` never changes the mode of an existing directory; it refuses (`InvalidPath`) a symlink, a non-directory, a directory owned by neither root nor the effective UID, or one that is group- or world-writable (e.g. `/tmp` `1777` stays `1777`); only a directory it creates gets `0700` (GitHub #178, STO-04) | `directory_validation_tests::test_new_refuses_world_writable_sticky_dir_and_leaves_mode_unchanged`, `test_new_refuses_group_writable_dir_and_leaves_mode_unchanged`, `test_new_refuses_world_writable_dir_without_sticky_bit`, `test_new_accepts_safe_existing_dir_without_touching_its_mode`, `test_new_preserves_setgid_bit_on_safe_existing_dir`, `test_new_refuses_existing_non_directory`, `test_new_refuses_symlinked_store_dir`, `test_new_creates_missing_dir_with_0700`, `test_new_ownership_rule_against_effective_uid`; unit tests `store::tests::test_validate_store_dir_accepts_root_and_euid_owned_safe_dirs`, `test_validate_store_dir_refuses_foreign_owner`, `test_validate_store_dir_refuses_group_or_world_writable`, `test_validate_store_dir_refuses_symlink_and_non_directory` | ✅ Verified |
| B9 | Re-enrollment atomically commits the new template on a new inode, then overwrites the replaced inode in place (best effort, handle retained across the rename); no temporary file left (GitHub #179, STO-06) | `erasure_tests::test_reenroll_overwrites_previous_template_inode_before_release`, `erasure_tests::test_first_enroll_without_previous_template_succeeds` | ✅ Verified |
| B10 | `BiometricTemplate::to_cbor` returns the CBOR plaintext in a pre-reserved `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` and zeroizes its temporary embedding copy (GitHub #179) | `cbor_zeroize_tests::test_to_cbor_returns_zeroizing_buffer`, `proptest_suite` round trip | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `vision-pad` (Presentation Attack Detection)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PAD1 | **MiniFASNetV2** anti-spoofing model (`minifasnet_v2_pad`) attested in `models/manifest.toml` v2.0.0 with SHA-256 checksum, 80×80 BGR input shape, and `[1, 3]` output shape; live class index 1 (`DEFAULT_MINIFASNET_LIVE_CLASS_INDEX`) for `[PrintPhoto, Live, ScreenReplay]` class ordering (see ASG1, PLC1–PLC3) | Manifest parsing test (`manifest_tests::test_parse_workspace_manifest_file`, `manifest_tests::test_manifest_v2_model_count_and_checksum_attestation`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD2 | `PadDetector` trait, `OrtPadDetector`, and `MockPadDetector` with numerically stable softmax and fault injection | Unit tests (`pad_tests::test_mock_pad_detector_nominal_live`, `test_mock_pad_detector_spoof_*`, `test_softmax_numerical_stability`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD3 | Vision pipeline short-circuits on spoof detection, completely skipping embedding extraction | Pipeline unit tests (`pad_tests::test_pipeline_rejects_printed_photo_spoof`, `test_pipeline_rejects_screen_replay_spoof`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD4 | Genuine live face candidates pass PAD and extract biometric embeddings | Pipeline unit tests (`pad_tests::test_pipeline_accepts_live_face`, `test_pad_threshold_calibration`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD5 | Pipeline plumbing (NOT a model metric, review PAD-06 / GitHub #172): 100 scripted `MockPadDetector` verdicts (50 live, 25 print, 25 screen) are routed through the vision pipeline with every live verdict producing an embedding and every spoof verdict rejected. It measures the mock, so it provides no FAR/FRR/APCER/BPCER evidence (see PAD9) | Plumbing test (`soos-vision` `pad_tests::test_pad_pipeline_plumbing_routes_mock_verdicts_without_leaks`) *(renamed from `test_pad_far_frr_benchmark`, assertions unchanged)* | ✅ Verified (plumbing only) |
| PAD6 | PAD verification execution latency remains well within the 30ms budget allocated in updated `ARCHITECTURE.md` §7 for MiniFASNetV2 | Benchmark test (`pad_tests::test_pad_latency_budget_compliance`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD7 | Daemon integration: PAD presentation attack yields `Verdict::Deny` with `ReasonClass::PadFailed` | Integration test (`pipeline_integration_tests::test_15_pad_presentation_attack_spoof_returns_deny_pad_failed`) | ✅ Verified |
| PAD8 | Real MiniFASNetV2 model (GitHub #172): the installed `minifasnet_v2_80x80.onnx`, attested against the committed manifest SHA-256, exposes input `[N, 3, 80, 80]` and output `[N, 3]`; the production `OrtPadDetector` session is deterministic and finite; golden raw logits for three synthetic non-biometric inputs (tolerance `1e-3`, argmax pinned) catch preprocessing drift, including a red/blue channel swap (delta about 0.12); the detector score equals `softmax(logits)[1]` and synthetic non-face patterns are never accepted as live at threshold 0.85. Gated on model presence (`SOOS_MODELS_DIR`, default `/var/lib/soos/models`; `SKIPPED` when absent, hard failure with `SOOS_REQUIRE_REAL_MODELS=1`). Run locally on 2026-09-30 with the models present (walkthrough 87) | Real-model tests (`soos-inference-ort` `pad_real_model_tests::test_real_pad_model_io_shape_metadata_matches_manifest`, `test_real_pad_model_is_deterministic_and_finite_on_synthetic_input`, `test_real_pad_model_golden_logits_on_synthetic_inputs`, `test_real_pad_golden_logits_detect_channel_order_swap`, `test_real_ort_pad_detector_score_matches_live_class_softmax`) | ✅ Verified (local host with models; skipped on CI runners without models) |
| PAD9 | Corpus APCER / BPCER at the shipped threshold 0.85 (ISO/IEC 30107-3): >= 20 crops each of bona fide, print and screen replay from `SOOS_PAD_CORPUS_DIR` (outside the repository, never versioned); APCER <= 5 % per attack species, BPCER <= 10 %; empty classes count as 100 % error. Harness and metric math verified; **no corpus has been captured, so no APCER / BPCER has been measured yet** | Corpus test (`pad_real_model_tests::test_real_pad_corpus_apcer_bpcer_under_ceilings`), harness unit tests (`test_pad_error_counts_compute_apcer_bpcer_per_species`, `test_parse_ppm_accepts_valid_and_rejects_malformed`) | ⬜ Pending (corpus capture) |

## Component: `pam-release-panic-unwind` (Review PAM-01 / TCI-01, GitHub #148)

Complements PA3, PA12 and PFT1: those rows are proven under `[profile.test]` (always unwinding); the rows below prove the same contract on the release-built artifact that packaging ships.

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PRU1 | Root `Cargo.toml` `[profile.release]` declares `panic = "unwind"` exactly once, no workspace or `crates/pam` profile sets `panic = "abort"`, and every packaging/Docker build command (`scripts/build_*.sh`, `packaging/debian/rules`, `packaging/rpm/soos.spec`, `packaging/arch/PKGBUILD`, `tests/docker/*.sh`) uses `--release` without a custom `--profile` | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_release_profile_unwinds_so_pam_catch_unwind_is_effective`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRU2 | The `soos-pam` `fault-injection` feature is opt-in (`fault-injection = []`, never in `default`), its module and call site are `#[cfg(feature = "fault-injection")]`-gated, and no packaging, install, Dockerfile or CI build command references it | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_pam_fault_injection_feature_is_opt_in_and_never_packaged`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRU3 | With the feature, `fault_inject=panic` / `fault_inject=overflow` parse into `FaultInject`, and an injected panic returns `PAM_IGNORE` through every entry path (C ABI `pam_sm_authenticate`, `PamHooks::sm_authenticate`, `authenticate_with_config`), repeatedly in one process, never `PAM_SUCCESS`; without the feature the argument is inert and `PamConfig::fault_inject` stays `None` | Integration tests (`fault_injection_tests::with_feature::test_parse_cstrs_fault_inject_modes`, `test_fault_inject_panic_returns_pam_ignore_via_c_abi`, `test_fault_inject_overflow_returns_pam_ignore_via_c_abi`, `test_fault_inject_via_pam_hooks_returns_pam_ignore`, `test_fault_inject_never_yields_success`, `test_fault_inject_is_repeatable_in_same_process`; `fault_injection_tests::test_default_config_has_no_fault_injection`, `test_fault_inject_unknown_mode_is_ignored`, `without_feature::test_fault_inject_argument_without_feature_is_ignored`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRU4 | A panic inside the **release-built** `pam_soos.so` loaded by a real Linux-PAM host returns `PAM_IGNORE`: the host process is not killed (exit code never ≥ 128 / 134 SIGABRT), a valid password is accepted via `pam_unix` fallback and an invalid password is rejected, for both `fault_inject=panic` and `fault_inject=overflow` | Docker matrix T10 (`tests/docker/test_suite.sh`, run by `./run_tests.sh` and the `pam-integration` CI job) + invariant test pinning the case (`soos-invariants::tests::test_pam_docker_suite_proves_release_panic_returns_pam_ignore`). Red evidence before the fix: `pam_test_runner` aborted with exit 134 under `panic = "abort"` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pad-live-class-index-single-source` (Review finding PAD-01 / GitHub #146)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PLC1 | `soos-daemon` builds its PAD detector through `pipeline::build_pad_detector` with the crate default live class index (`DEFAULT_MINIFASNET_LIVE_CLASS_INDEX = 1`) and the configured `vision.pad_threshold`; no literal index on the PAM authentication path | Integration tests with an in-memory ONNX session (`pad_wiring_tests::test_pipeline_pad_detector_uses_default_live_class_index`, `pad_wiring_tests::test_pipeline_pad_detector_classifies_replay_as_spoof` in `crates/daemon`, fixture `tests/fixtures/mod.rs::onnx::minimal_identity_model`) | ✅ Verified |
| PLC2 | `soos-enroll` builds its PAD detector through `service::build_pad_detector` with the crate default live class index, so enrollment and `verify` diagnostics score liveness from the same class as the daemon and the GUI (class 2 = screen replay is a spoof, class 1 = live passes) | Integration tests (`pad_wiring_tests::test_enrollment_pad_detector_uses_default_live_class_index`, `pad_wiring_tests::test_enrollment_pad_detector_classifies_replay_as_spoof`) | ✅ Verified |

| PTF2 | `soos-daemon` refuses to start (`DaemonError::Config`) when `[pipeline.thresholds]` in `daemon.toml` sets `pad_threshold` to `0.0`, `0`, `-1.0`, `nan`, `inf`, `1.5` or `0.49`, or `match_threshold` to `0.0`, `0.39` or `nan`; accepted values reach both `pipeline.thresholds` and `pipeline.vision` | Integration tests (`threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_pad_threshold_zero`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_pad_threshold_integer_zero_typo`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_negative_pad_threshold`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_nan_pad_threshold`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_infinite_pad_threshold`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_pad_threshold_above_one`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_pad_threshold_below_floor`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_match_threshold_zero`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_match_threshold_below_floor`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_nan_match_threshold`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_accepts_floor_boundaries_and_propagates_to_vision`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_partial_thresholds_keep_validated_defaults`, `threshold_config_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejected_thresholds_fail_load_from_path`) | ✅ Verified |
| PTF3 | Repository invariant: `ThresholdConfig::new_raw(` never appears in production code outside `soos-policy`, and `crates/daemon/src/config.rs` validates with `build_with_security_floor()` | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::pad_contract::test_thresholds_never_built_unvalidated_outside_policy`) | ✅ Verified |
| PTF4 | Repository invariant: every living document (`AGENTS.md`, `AI/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `AI/DECISIONS.md`, `AI/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md`, `AI/BACKLOG.md`, `models/README.md`, `Docs/**`, `.agents/skills/**`) states the MiniFASNetV2 live class index exactly as the code constant `DEFAULT_MINIFASNET_LIVE_CLASS_INDEX` (`[PrintPhoto, Live, ScreenReplay]`, index 1); historical lines must carry `*(Superseded ...)*`; the claim extractor is self-tested against every known phrasing | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::pad_contract::test_minifasnet_class_contract_prose_matches_code_constant`, `soos-invariants::pad_contract::test_class_contract_claim_extractor_detects_every_phrasing`) | ✅ Verified |
| PTF5 | Repository invariant: the PAD verification-matrix rows (PAD1, NGM9, NGM10, ASG1, ASG2, PLC1–PLC3, PTF1) cite only `module::test_name` references that exist as `fn test_name(` in the workspace | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::pad_contract::test_pad_matrix_rows_cite_existing_tests`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `verification-matrix-citations` (Review finding TCI-04 / GitHub #187)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| MXC1 | Repository invariant: every citation of every `✅ Verified` / `☑ Validated` row and every checked global invariant of this matrix resolves: a bare test name, module::name, crate::module::name and soos-crate::module::name (name starting with test_) to a test function (`#[test]`, `#[tokio::test]`, `proptest!`, shell or Python test function) in the files selected by every qualifier; a prefix glob (test_prefix_*) to at least one such test; a module glob (module::*) to a file or module containing a test; repository paths (globs and `{a,b}` lists allowed) and bare `*.rs` / `*.sh` / `*.py` names to existing files; a production `.rs` source cited as evidence must contain a test. History inside `*( ... )*` is not a citation | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::matrix_citations::test_matrix_claimed_rows_cite_only_existing_evidence`, at least 900 citations checked). Red evidence: 21 of 1059 citations in 12 rows failed before the row corrections of walkthrough 104 | ✅ Verified |

| MXC3 | Claimed rows do not contradict the code or themselves: a row whose criterion opens with `**Superseded` carries a non-claimed status (GEPU3); every "decision budget" value equals `DECISION_BUDGET_MS` (D7 said 150 ms, the code says 900 ms); the removed landmark detector is only mentioned to record its removal (VZF3 vs NGM6). The MiniFASNetV2 live class index (1) is already enforced by PTF4 | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::matrix_citations::test_matrix_superseded_rows_do_not_claim_verification`, `test_matrix_decision_budget_matches_code_constant`, `test_matrix_claimed_rows_do_not_list_removed_landmark_detector`). ## Component: `pad-multiframe-consensus` (Review finding PAD-02 / GitHub #147)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |

| PMC2 | `Allow` requires `k` consecutive passing captures (live `>= pad_threshold`, match `>= match_threshold`); fewer than `k`, a single passing capture, or no capture never authorizes | Unit & property tests (`pad_consensus_tests::test_pad_aggregator_three_consecutive_live_frames_allow`, `test_pad_aggregator_fewer_than_k_live_frames_is_pending_timeout`, `test_pad_aggregator_first_passing_frame_does_not_allow`, `test_pad_aggregator_no_frames_is_unavailable_timeout`, `test_pad_aggregator_custom_required_count_is_honored`, `prop_allow_iff_trailing_k_frames_pass_without_spoof`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC3 | Any spoof-classified capture (PAD spoof, liveness score below threshold, or non-finite score) vetoes `Allow` for the whole request; the veto is sticky and survives any number of subsequent live captures | Unit & property tests (`pad_consensus_tests::test_pad_aggregator_alternating_spoof_live_never_allows`, `test_pad_aggregator_spoof_veto_is_sticky_for_the_request`, `test_pad_aggregator_spoof_after_consensus_reached_revokes_allow`, `test_pad_aggregator_pad_score_below_threshold_is_spoof_even_if_classified_live`, `test_pad_aggregator_non_finite_scores_fail_closed`, `prop_any_spoof_frame_prevents_allow`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC4 | Non-spoof interruptions (`NoFace`, `MultipleFaces`, live-but-not-matching) reset the consecutive run without vetoing; retained history is bounded by the window | Unit tests (`pad_consensus_tests::test_pad_aggregator_no_face_frame_resets_consecutive_run`, `test_pad_aggregator_multiple_faces_frame_resets_consecutive_run`, `test_pad_aggregator_low_match_score_breaks_run_without_veto`, `test_pad_aggregator_history_is_bounded_by_window`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC5 | Daemon dispatcher integration: alternating live/spoof captures, a spoof followed by live captures, and a live capture under the PAD threshold all return `Deny`/`PadFailed`; `k` consecutive live captures return `Allow`/`FaceMatch` after at least `k` PAD evaluations within `DECISION_BUDGET_MS`; existing single-spoof (`test_15_*`), stale-frame (`test_12_7_*`), rate-limit (`test_12_5_*`) and no-face recovery (`test_48_*`) contracts unchanged | Integration tests (`pipeline_integration_tests::test_147_alternating_live_spoof_pad_never_allows`, `test_147_spoof_frame_vetoes_subsequent_live_frames_for_whole_request`, `test_147_live_frame_below_pad_threshold_vetoes_request`, `test_147_k_consecutive_live_frames_allow_within_budget`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pad-ir-format-aware` (Review finding PAD-03 / GitHub #169)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PIR1 | `PadInputModality::from_pixel_format` maps `Grey` to `Monochrome` and `Rgb24`/`Yuyv`/`Nv12`/`Mjpeg` to `Color`; IR constants are ordered and bounded; `VisionPipelineConfig::default().ir_pad_threshold == DEFAULT_IR_PAD_THRESHOLD (0.95) > pad_threshold`; `effective_pad_threshold(Monochrome) = max(pad_threshold, ir_pad_threshold)` is never looser than the colour threshold | Unit tests (`ir_pad_policy_tests::test_grey_pixel_format_maps_to_monochrome_modality`, `test_colour_pixel_formats_map_to_colour_modality`, `test_ir_gate_constants_are_ordered_and_bounded`, `test_default_ir_pad_threshold_is_stricter_than_rgb_threshold`, `test_effective_pad_threshold_is_never_looser_for_monochrome`) | ✅ Verified |
| PIR2 | Fail-closed IR gate (`evaluate_ir_gate`, `ir_crop_statistics`): malformed buffers/zero dimensions are errors (`InvalidCrop`), and unlit, saturated, flat and texture-less crops are rejected (`Underexposed`, `Overexposed`, `LowContrast`, `LowTexture`) while a textured, well-exposed crop passes | Unit tests (`ir_pad_policy_tests::test_ir_crop_statistics_rejects_malformed_buffers`, `test_ir_crop_statistics_on_flat_crop`, `test_ir_gate_rejection_reasons`, `test_ir_gate_malformed_crop_fails_closed`) | ✅ Verified |
| PIR3 | `process_frame` on a `Grey` frame never takes the colour path: gate failures return `VisionError::IrLivenessGateFailed` without consulting the PAD model or extracting an embedding, even when the model would say live; a live score between `pad_threshold` and the IR threshold returns `PadFailed` with the IR threshold; NaN scores/thresholds and model spoofs reject; a textured crop scored above the IR threshold passes | Unit tests (`ir_pad_policy_tests::test_grey_flat_frame_is_rejected_by_ir_gate_even_if_model_says_live`, `test_grey_dark_frame_is_rejected_as_underexposed`, `test_grey_saturated_frame_is_rejected_as_overexposed`, `test_grey_smooth_frame_is_rejected_as_low_texture`, `test_grey_frame_uses_stricter_ir_threshold`, `test_grey_frame_threshold_never_looser_than_rgb_threshold`, `test_grey_frame_non_finite_thresholds_fail_closed`, `test_grey_frame_non_finite_score_fails_closed`, `test_grey_frame_model_spoof_is_rejected`, `test_grey_textured_frame_with_high_liveness_is_accepted`) | ✅ Verified |
| PIR4 | Colour frames keep the RGB PAD behavior (no IR gate, `pad_threshold`), and the GUI `analyze_frame` reports `is_live = false` for Grey gate failures and sub-IR-threshold scores while returning the raw model result for colour frames | Unit tests (`ir_pad_policy_tests::test_rgb_frame_keeps_rgb_threshold_and_skips_ir_gate`, `test_analyze_frame_marks_grey_gate_failure_as_not_live`, `test_analyze_frame_marks_grey_score_below_ir_threshold_as_not_live`, `test_analyze_frame_keeps_rgb_result_unchanged`) | ✅ Verified |
| PIR5 | Daemon integration: Grey captures scored live at 0.90 (above the vision 0.80 and policy 0.85 thresholds, below the IR threshold) end in `Deny`/`PadFailed`; Grey captures above the IR threshold still reach `Allow`/`FaceMatch`; `IrLivenessGateFailed` is mapped to a spoof capture (request veto). Real-hardware IR calibration is out of scope (GitHub #172) | Integration tests (`pipeline_integration_tests::test_169_grey_frames_use_stricter_ir_pad_threshold`, `pipeline_integration_tests::test_169_grey_frames_above_ir_threshold_allow`). Red evidence before the fix: the first test returned `Allow`/`FaceMatch` | ✅ Verified |
| PIR6 | The IR policy keys on the sensor type as well as the pixel format: `Frame` carries a `SensorType` (default `Unknown`, set by `with_sensor_type`), the V4L2 capture classifies the opened node with `classify_sensor` (`plan_capture`), prefers `Grey` on `Infrared` nodes under auto negotiation, and stamps every frame; `PadInputModality::for_source`/`for_frame` return `Monochrome` for any `Infrared` frame, so an IR node streaming YUYV passes the IR gate and the IR threshold, never the RGB path (candid review finding 2) | Unit & integration tests (`soos-vision::ir_sensor_policy_tests::test_frame_new_defaults_to_unknown_sensor_and_tag_is_applied`, `test_infrared_sensor_is_monochrome_modality_for_every_pixel_format`, `test_rgb_and_unknown_sensors_keep_format_based_modality`, `test_for_frame_uses_the_frame_sensor_tag`, `test_ir_sensor_streaming_yuyv_flat_frame_is_rejected_by_ir_gate`, `test_ir_sensor_streaming_yuyv_uses_stricter_ir_threshold`, `test_ir_sensor_streaming_yuyv_textured_high_liveness_is_accepted`, `test_analyze_frame_ir_sensor_yuyv_below_ir_threshold_is_not_live`; `soos-camera-v4l::ir_capture_plan_tests::*`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `preview-frame-authorization` (Review finding CAM-01 / DMN-02 — GitHub #143)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PFA1 | Default configuration denies `RequestKind::PreviewFrame` to every unprivileged peer (`[preview]` absent or `enabled = false`, empty `allowed_uids`); a root peer is always authorized; the allow-list is bounded to `MAX_PREVIEW_ALLOWED_UIDS` (64) and larger configurations fail closed | Unit tests (`preview_authorization_tests::test_preview_config_defaults_deny_unprivileged_peers`, `test_authorize_preview_root_peer_always_allowed`, `test_authorize_preview_default_denies_unprivileged_peer`, `test_authorize_preview_listed_but_disabled_is_denied`, `test_preview_config_rejects_oversized_allow_list`, `test_daemon_config_default_preview_is_disabled`, `test_daemon_config_parses_preview_section`, `test_daemon_config_rejects_oversized_preview_allow_list`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFA2 | Dispatcher serves preview frames only after kernel peer verification: a peer that is disabled, not allow-listed, or declares a foreign `uid_hint` receives a standard `Response` (`ProtocolError` / `UidMismatch`, at most 59 bytes) and zero pixel bytes | Integration tests (`preview_authorization_tests::test_preview_frame_rejected_when_disabled`, `test_preview_frame_rejected_when_enabled_but_uid_not_listed`, `test_preview_frame_uid_mismatch_rejected`, `test_authorize_preview_uid_mismatch_is_denied_even_when_listed`, `test_authorize_preview_enabled_but_not_listed_is_denied`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFA3 | Unprivileged preview peers must own an active logind session (`SessionValidator`); an authorized, allow-listed peer with an active session receives real `PreviewResponse` frames from the daemon camera | Integration tests (`preview_authorization_tests::test_preview_frame_rejected_without_active_session`, `test_preview_frame_allowed_for_configured_uid_serves_frames`, migrated `dispatcher_tests::test_dispatcher_preview_frame_roundtrip`, `dispatcher_tests::test_dispatcher_persistent_stream_multiple_requests`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFA4 | Preview requests are rate limited per peer UID (`[preview] max_requests_per_sec`, default 40, 1 s window, 64 tracked UIDs) with `ProtocolError` / `RateLimited` beyond the quota | Integration test (`preview_authorization_tests::test_preview_frame_rate_limited_per_peer_uid`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFA5 | A refused preview request never wakes the camera (`notify_activity()` not called) nor enters the readiness wait loop; `PreviewResponse` and every encoded daemon response are zeroized on drop | Integration & unit tests (`preview_authorization_tests::test_preview_frame_denied_peer_does_not_wake_camera`, `preview_tests::test_preview_response_zeroize_erases_pixel_data`, `logging_audit_test::test_daemon_source_code_has_zero_sensitive_data_in_logs`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFA6 | `soos-gui` `IpcCameraManager` sends a fresh random nonce and its real UID, maps a nonce-bound refusal to `IpcPreviewError` (`Unauthorized` stops polling without reconnecting, `RateLimited` keeps polling), and `probe_preview` reports authorization before the GUI commits to the daemon proxy | Integration tests (`ipc_camera_tests::test_ipc_camera_manager_stops_on_unauthorized_response`, `test_ipc_camera_manager_keeps_polling_after_rate_limit`, `test_probe_preview_reports_unauthorized`, `test_probe_preview_accepts_authorized_reply`, `test_probe_preview_reports_io_error_when_socket_absent`, `test_ipc_preview_error_display_is_english_and_specific`, `layout_tests::test_ipc_camera_manager_receives_persistent_frames`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `package-master-key-isolation` (GitHub #144 / review finding ONB-01)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PMK1 | `scripts/install.sh --destdir <stage>` never generates key material inside the staging tree: no `*.key` file anywhere under the stage, `var/lib/soos/master.key` absent, and the `usr/libexec/soos/provision-master-key` helper staged with mode `0755` | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::tests::test_install_script_destdir_stages_no_key_material`, `soos-invariants::tests::test_install_script_creates_required_directories`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMK2 | The shared helper `scripts/provision_master_key.sh` (`/usr/libexec/soos/provision-master-key`) generates a 32-byte non-zero key with mode `0600` from inception (`umask 077`, private temporary file, atomic hard-link publish), is idempotent (an existing key is never overwritten, its mode is tightened to `0600`) and leaves no temporary file | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_provision_master_key_helper_generates_0600_key_once`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMK3 | The helper fails closed on a symlink or a non-regular file at the key path and never writes through the symlink target | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_provision_master_key_helper_refuses_symlink_and_non_regular`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMK4 | Every native package generates the key on the target host at first install: `packaging/debian/postinst` (`configure`), `packaging/arch/soos.install` (`post_install`) and `packaging/rpm/soos.spec` (`%post`) call the shipped helper; the helper is installed by `install.sh`, `PKGBUILD` and the RPM `%install`/`%files` (`master.key` is never listed, not even `%ghost`, walkthrough 106) and removed by `scripts/uninstall.sh`; no installer or scriptlet keeps an inline key generator | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::tests::test_package_scriptlets_provision_key_via_shared_helper`, `soos-invariants::tests::test_uninstall_restores_pam_config`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMK5 | Package builders (`scripts/build_deb.sh`, `scripts/build_arch.sh`, `packaging/debian/rules`) run the fail-closed guard `scripts/check_no_key_material.sh` on the staged tree; the guard rejects any `*.key` file at any depth and a missing tree, and accepts a clean tree | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_package_builders_refuse_staged_key_material`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMK6 | Built artifacts contain no key entry (`dpkg-deb -c`, `rpm -qlp --noghost`, `bsdtar -tf`), the installed key is `0600 root:root` and 32 bytes, it survives package removal, and two fresh installs of the same artifact yield distinct keys | Docker package test (`tests/docker/test_packages.sh`: `verify_package_has_no_key_material`, `verify_key_survives_removal`, `verify_fresh_install_generates_distinct_key`); executed manually in `ubuntu:24.04` and `archlinux:base` containers (walkthrough 83 §8) | ✅ Verified |

|---|---|---|---|
| FAP1 | The Fedora `authselect` custom profile (`packaging/pam/fedora/soos/`) is complete: `README` (declared features), `REQUIREMENTS`, `system-auth`, `password-auth`, `nsswitch.conf`, `fingerprint-auth`, `smartcard-auth`, `postlogin`, `dconf-db`, `dconf-locks`; no template uses the unsupported `{?feature:...}` syntax; every feature referenced by a template is declared in `README` (`with-faillock::` included); `nsswitch.conf` defines `passwd`, `shadow`, `group`, `hosts`, `services` | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_fedora_authselect_profile_is_complete`); Docker validation A1/A2/A4 (`tests/docker/authselect_profile_test.sh`: `authselect select custom/soos with-faillock --force` rc=0, `authselect check` rc=0, `/etc/nsswitch.conf` identical to the `local` baseline) | ✅ Verified |
| FAP2 | Generated `system-auth` and `password-auth` keep `pam_faillock.so preauth` before the primary `pam_soos.so` rule (module default timeout since #185), `pam_soos.so` before `pam_unix.so` (`sufficient`), `pam_faillock.so authfail` (`required`, never `[default=die]`) after `pam_unix.so`, the optional `pam_soos.so event=password-failed timeout_ms=20` after `authfail`, `pam_deny.so` last, and the account-phase `pam_faillock.so`; all three `pam_faillock` lines are gated by `{include if "with-faillock"}` | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_fedora_authselect_profile_preserves_faillock_ordering`); Docker validation A3 (line-number ordering of the generated files, exactly 3 `pam_faillock` lines) and A6 (no `pam_faillock` line without the feature) | ✅ Verified |
| FAP3 | Password fallback through the generated stacks keeps working when `pam_soos.so` is absent or returns `PAM_IGNORE`: correct password accepted, wrong password rejected on `system-auth` and `password-auth` (`pamtester`), with and without `with-faillock` | Docker validation A5/A6 (`tests/docker/authselect_profile_test.sh`, `fedora:40`) | ✅ Verified |
| FAP4 | Rollback path: `scripts/install.sh` and the RPM `%post` record `authselect current --raw` in `/etc/soos/authselect.previous` (never overwritten by `custom/soos`); `scripts/uninstall.sh` and the RPM `%preun` re-select the recorded profile (fallback `local`, `minimal`, `sssd`) **before** removing `/etc/authselect/custom/soos`, and keep the profile when no restoration succeeds | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_fedora_authselect_activation_and_rollback_are_scripted`); Docker validation A7 (`scripts/uninstall.sh --keep-data --skip-systemd` restores `local with-faillock`, removes the profile and the record, `authselect check` rc=0, password login works) | ✅ Verified |
| FAP5 | Activation is documented with the command `authselect` accepts (`authselect select custom/soos with-faillock --force`, then `authselect check`) in `Docs/DISTRIBUTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` and `Docs/PACKAGING_AND_PROVISIONING.md`; `tests/distro/fedora_rhel_test.sh` activates the profile for real (no `authselect check \|\| true`) and verifies `/etc/nsswitch.conf`; the Docker validation is exposed by `./run_tests.sh authselect` and runs in CI (`authselect-profile` job) | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_fedora_authselect_activation_and_rollback_are_scripted`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `camera-single-resolver` (Review findings CAM-02 / CAM-04 — GitHub #150, #152)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| CSR1 | A single shared resolver (`soos_camera_v4l::resolve_camera_device`) handles the auto sentinels identically (`""`, `auto`, `default`, `/dev/v4l/by-id/default-camera`, case-insensitive), honors an explicit path verbatim, auto-selects the capture node matching `sensor_preference` (default IR) through its stable by-id alias, never selects a by-id alias of a metadata node (`...-video-index1`), and falls back to the sentinel without capture nodes; the by-id scan is bounded (64 entries) and skips dangling aliases | Unit tests (`resolver_tests::test_default_sentinel_handled_like_auto`, `test_no_config_prefers_ir_by_id_alias`, `test_prefer_rgb_selects_rgb_by_id_alias`, `test_by_id_metadata_index1_never_selected`, `test_selected_node_without_alias_is_returned_as_is`, `test_explicit_path_honored_verbatim`, `test_no_capture_device_falls_back_to_sentinel`, `test_parse_sensor_preference_shared_vocabulary`, `test_camera_config_explicit_device_ignores_sentinel`, `test_system_enumerator_canonicalizes_by_id_aliases`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSR2 | `soos-enroll` and `soos-gui` direct mode resolve exactly like the daemon: no `daemon.toml`, no `camera_device` key, `auto`, `default` and `""` select the daemon's device (IR first), `sensor_preference` is read with the shared vocabulary, explicit precedence is CLI flag then `camera_device` | Integration tests (`camera_resolver_parity_tests::test_no_config_prefers_ir_for_daemon_cli_and_gui`, `test_cli_default_sentinel_handled_like_auto`, `test_cli_sensor_preference_from_config_matches_daemon`, `test_cli_explicit_precedence_is_cli_then_config`; existing `device_resolution_tests::*`, `import_tests::test_resolve_camera_device_from_daemon_config`, `model_id_tests::test_camera_device_path_uses_stable_by_id` unchanged and green) | ✅ Verified |
| CSR3 | The daemon keeps the auto sentinel for a missing key, `auto`, `default` and `""`, resolves through the shared resolver (`pipeline::resolve_pipeline_camera`), honors an explicit `camera_device` verbatim, and never enumerates with the mock camera | Integration tests (`camera_resolution_tests::test_daemon_default_sentinel_handled_like_auto`, `test_daemon_resolution_matches_shared_resolver`, `test_daemon_explicit_device_honored_verbatim`, `test_daemon_mock_camera_skips_enumeration`; existing `config_tests::test_pipeline_config_camera_device_auto_resolution`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSR4 | `soos-gui` never opens the camera directly while the daemon owns it: direct V4L2 only when `connect(2)` fails with `ENOENT`/`ECONNREFUSED` and `soos-daemon.service` is not active; `EACCES`/`EPERM` is classified as missing `soos` group membership; a reachable daemon whose preview is refused or unavailable, or an active daemon with an unreachable socket, yields a blocked state with an actionable English message and an `UnavailableCameraManager` that never serves frames | Integration tests (`camera_mode_tests::test_probe_classifies_eacces_as_permission_denied`, `test_probe_classifies_missing_and_stale_socket_as_not_running`, `test_probe_classifies_listening_socket_as_reachable`, `test_direct_mode_refused_while_daemon_active`, `test_ipc_and_direct_modes_selected_only_when_safe`, `test_blocked_reasons_are_actionable`, `test_unavailable_camera_manager_never_serves_frames`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSR5 | `EBUSY` from `VIDIOC_S_FMT` / `VIDIOC_REQBUFS` / `VIDIOC_DQBUF` maps to `CameraError::DeviceBusy` and `ENODEV` to `DeviceNotFound` (raw OS error kept); other ioctl errors keep their contextual variant; the supervisor logs a dedicated busy-device warning | Unit tests (`error_recovery_tests::test_set_format_ebusy_maps_to_device_busy`, `test_ioctl_enodev_maps_to_device_not_found`, `test_ioctl_other_errno_uses_contextual_fallback`, `test_device_busy_is_reported_as_busy`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSR6 | One tested path, one scanner: `initialize_pipeline` resolves the camera through `resolve_pipeline_camera` (system enumerator), itself built on `plan_camera_device`, so the #152 parity tests cover the shipped path; `stable_device_path` delegates to `SystemCameraEnumerator::by_id_aliases`, the single bounded by-id scanner (`MAX_BY_ID_ENTRIES` in `resolver.rs`) (candid review findings 3 and 4) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::tests::test_daemon_production_camera_resolution_uses_tested_resolver`, `test_single_bounded_by_id_scanner`) + existing `camera_resolution_tests::*`, `camera_health_tests::*`, `supervision_tests::test_stable_device_path_prefers_by_id_link`, `test_stable_device_path_falls_back_to_node` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-camera-health` (Review findings CAM-03 / CAM-05 / DMN-07 — GitHub #151, #153)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| CSH1 | The V4L2 capture supervisor re-resolves the device through a `DevicePathResolver` after every `DeviceNotFound` / `UnsupportedCapability` backoff and reopens the new node (`/dev/videoN` re-enumeration after suspend, replug or boot race) without a daemon restart; a resolver returning `None` keeps the current path | Unit test (`soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_supervisor_reresolves_device_after_enodev`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSH2 | Re-resolution is paced by the bounded exponential backoff (at most one resolver call per backoff period, never a busy loop) | Unit test (`soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_supervisor_reresolution_is_bounded_by_backoff`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSH3 | An explicitly configured `device_path` (e.g. a by-id link not yet created by udev) is kept and retried as-is, never silently replaced by a different camera; only the auto-selection sentinel `/dev/v4l/by-id/default-camera` enumerates and gets a re-resolving supervisor | Unit tests (`soos-daemon::camera_health_tests::test_pipeline_init_keeps_configured_by_id_when_missing_and_retries`, `test_pipeline_auto_select_uses_sentinel_and_resolver`; `soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_supervisor_without_resolver_keeps_fixed_path`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSH4 | Auto-selected devices are addressed by their persistent `/dev/v4l/by-id/...` link when one resolves to the enumerated node (deterministic choice, bounded directory scan, dangling links ignored), falling back to the node path | Unit tests (`soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_stable_device_path_prefers_by_id_link`, `test_stable_device_path_falls_back_to_node`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSH5 | `HealthState.camera_ready` (and therefore `StatusResponse.camera_ready`, `is_healthy`, `soos-admin status`) is derived live from `CameraManager::health()`: `false` while the device is missing, busy or starting, `true` while streaming **and** during idle auto-standby; startup no longer forces it to `true` | Unit tests (`soos-daemon::camera_health_tests::test_camera_ready_false_when_device_missing`, `test_camera_ready_true_when_mock_streaming`, `test_camera_ready_follows_failure_and_recovery`, `test_camera_ready_stays_true_during_auto_standby`, `test_camera_ready_without_attached_camera_uses_flag`; `soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_v4l_health_recovering_when_device_missing`, `test_mock_health_tracks_streaming_failure_and_standby`, `test_camera_health_operational_states`) | ✅ Verified |
| CSH6 | A panic inside the `soos-v4l-capture` supervisor (possible under `panic = "unwind"`) is caught with `catch_unwind`: the camera is reported `Dead` (`camera_ready = false`), readiness and the published frame are withdrawn, the thread is never restarted (fail-closed) and dropping the manager does not hang or propagate the panic | Unit test (`soos-camera-v4l::supervision_tests::test_supervisor_panic_marks_camera_dead`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `gui-responsiveness-and-errors` (Review findings CAM-06 / CAM-07, GitHub #154 / #155)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| GRE1 | `soos-daemon.service` state is polled by the background `DaemonMonitor` at most once per `DAEMON_POLL_INTERVAL` (2 s, `PollThrottle` with injectable clock), published in an atomic read lock-free by the header, and re-polled promptly after `request_refresh()`; reading the state never invokes the probe or blocks on a slow probe (supersedes the synchronous `SoosApp::is_daemon_active` evidence of GEPU3) | Integration tests (`responsiveness_tests::test_poll_interval_is_two_seconds`, `test_poll_throttle_first_poll_is_due_then_waits_for_interval`, `test_poll_throttle_bounds_polls_under_repaint_storm`, `test_poll_throttle_force_makes_next_poll_due`, `test_daemon_monitor_state_reads_never_call_probe`, `test_daemon_monitor_refresh_picks_up_state_change`, `test_daemon_monitor_state_does_not_block_on_slow_probe`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE2 | Every `pkexec` operation (pause/resume daemon, list/import/delete templates) runs on a `TaskRunner` worker thread: `submit` returns while the executor blocks, the outcome is delivered through a channel drained by `poll` and wakes the UI, a concurrent privileged action is rejected with `TaskRunnerError::Busy`, and `app.rs` spawns no process | Integration tests (`responsiveness_tests::test_task_runner_submit_returns_while_executor_blocks`, `test_task_runner_rejects_concurrent_privileged_action`, `test_app_ui_code_spawns_no_process`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE3 | `PrivilegedAction` never exposes embedding values through `Debug`; the import file is created `0600` with `O_EXCL` under a random name and removed on every path | Integration test (`responsiveness_tests::test_import_action_debug_never_prints_embedding`) and code review of `privileged::write_private_file` / `TempFileGuard` | ✅ Verified |
| GRE4 | The GUI binary installs a stderr `tracing` subscriber honoring `RUST_LOG` before the first log (invalid or empty directives fall back to `info`, idempotent init) | Integration tests (`camera_status_tests::test_log_filter_defaults_and_rejects_invalid_directive`, `test_logging_init_is_idempotent`, `test_gui_main_installs_tracing_subscriber_first`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE5 | `CameraError::kind()` classifies failures into distinct `CameraErrorKind`s (`EBUSY`, `EACCES`/`EPERM` even inside `Io`, `ENOENT`/`ENODEV`, unsupported format, starvation, simulated codes); `CameraStatusCell` counts consecutive failures per kind (saturating); `CameraManager::status()` defaults from `is_ready()` and `MockCameraManager` reports injected faults and recovery | Integration tests (`camera_status_tests::test_camera_error_kind_classifies_os_errors`, `test_camera_error_kind_classifies_domain_variants`, `test_camera_error_kind_classifies_simulated_codes`, `test_status_cell_counts_consecutive_failures_per_kind`, `test_status_cell_failure_count_saturates`, `test_default_status_is_derived_from_readiness`, `test_mock_camera_status_reports_injected_error_and_recovery`, `test_mock_camera_status_reports_starvation`, `test_error_kind_descriptions_are_distinct_and_english`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE6 | The GUI renders a distinct, actionable banner per camera state instead of a generic spinner (unique title per `CameraErrorKind`, failure count shown, busy device shown as busy, unreachable daemon names `soos-daemon`), and `IpcCameraManager::status()` reports an absent daemon socket as `SourceUnreachable` | Integration tests (`camera_status_tests::test_status_banner_shows_camera_error`, `test_status_banner_titles_are_distinct_per_error_kind`, `test_status_banner_distinguishes_non_error_states`, `test_status_banner_reports_retry_count`, `test_status_banner_unreachable_daemon_mentions_daemon`, `test_render_status_banner_draws_in_egui_context`, `test_ipc_camera_status_reports_unreachable_daemon`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE7 | The GUI camera source is re-decided at runtime by the pure `CameraSourcePlanner` from the `DaemonMonitor` state: an unknown state never opens the device; daemon paused => direct V4L2 (shared resolver); daemon active => IPC preview or a blocked notice; a direct source is left as soon as the daemon is active; Pause switches IPC to direct instead of a blank feed; steady sources are never re-probed | Integration tests (`camera_source_tests::test_planner_unknown_daemon_state_never_opens_direct`, `test_planner_initial_selection_follows_daemon_state`, `test_planner_releases_direct_when_daemon_becomes_active`, `test_planner_switches_ipc_to_direct_after_pause`, `test_planner_keeps_steady_sources_without_probing`, `test_switchable_camera_delegates_to_current_source_and_bumps_generation`, `test_supervisor_switches_sources_on_daemon_state_changes`, `test_gui_main_selects_camera_source_at_runtime`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE8 | Resume handover: `HandoverExecutor` stops and drops the direct V4L2 manager (device closed) on the privileged worker thread before `pkexec systemctl start` runs, keeps direct mode disabled while the Resume is pending and until the daemon is seen active (bounded grace), and re-enables it immediately when the Resume fails; other privileged actions pass through | Integration tests (`camera_source_tests::test_release_manager_stops_and_drops_the_last_reference`, `test_planner_handover_never_selects_direct`, `test_handover_releases_direct_camera_before_resume_executes`, `test_failed_resume_lets_the_gui_reopen_the_direct_camera`, `test_handover_executor_passes_other_actions_through_without_release`, `test_handover_executor_without_camera_source_still_resumes`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE9 | Accurate retry behavior: transient blocks (rate limit, I/O, unavailable, daemon unreachable or starting, direct open failure) are re-probed at most `MAX_TRANSIENT_PROBE_RETRIES` times, permanent ones (not authorized, permission denied, protocol) only on a daemon state change; block messages say whether soos-gui retries; the camera banner no longer claims "retrying automatically" for `SourceUnauthorized` (candid review finding 5) | Integration tests (`camera_source_tests::test_planner_retries_transient_preview_failure_until_ipc`, `test_planner_transient_retries_are_bounded`, `test_planner_permanent_block_not_retried_until_state_change`, `test_block_reason_transience_classification`, `test_block_messages_state_whether_retry_happens`; `camera_status_retry_tests::*`) | ✅ Verified |
| GRE10 | The `pkexec soos-enroll list` stdout is bounded while it is read (`read_bounded`, `Read::take(MAX_PROFILE_LIST_BYTES + 1)`), never buffered whole by `Command::output()`; an oversized or failed read kills the helper (candid review finding 7) | Integration tests (`bounded_output_tests::test_read_bounded_accepts_output_up_to_the_limit`, `test_read_bounded_rejects_output_over_the_limit`, `test_read_bounded_never_consumes_more_than_limit_plus_one`, `test_list_profiles_reads_helper_stdout_through_bounded_reader`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-deadline-and-messages` (Review findings PAM-02 / PAM-03 / PAM-05 — GitHub #173, #174, #176)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PDM1 | The PAM IPC client applies ONE cumulative deadline per exchange: `SO_RCVTIMEO` / `SO_SNDTIMEO` are re-armed with the remaining budget before every `read()` / `write()` (`read_exact_before_deadline`, `write_all_before_deadline`, also used by `notify_event`), and a verdict completed after the deadline is discarded; a daemon drip-feeding 1 body byte per 70 ms with `timeout_ms=100` yields `IpcError::Timeout` / `PAM_IGNORE` in < 250 ms, while a fragmented response completed inside the budget is still accepted. Red evidence: `Allow` honored after ~2.6 s | Integration tests (`ipc_tests::test_ipc_drip_fed_body_respects_cumulative_deadline`, `ipc_tests::test_ipc_direct_drip_fed_body_returns_timeout`, `ipc_tests::test_ipc_fragmented_body_within_budget_is_accepted`) | ✅ Verified |
| PDM2 | Without a `service=` argument the module adopts the `PAM_SERVICE` item (UTF-8, trimmed, bounded to `MAX_SERVICE_LEN` on a char boundary; explicit `service=` wins), forwards it to the daemon, and `is_disabled()` honors `<flag_dir>/disabled`, `<flag_dir>/gdm.disable` for every service containing `gdm`, and `<flag_dir>/<service>.disable` for `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` names not starting with `.` (no path traversal); a disabled service returns `PAM_IGNORE` without contacting the daemon | Unit tests (`config_tests::test_default_flag_dir_is_etc_soos`, `config_tests::test_service_argument_marks_service_as_explicit`, `config_tests::test_pam_service_item_used_when_no_service_argument`, `config_tests::test_explicit_service_argument_wins_over_pam_service_item`, `config_tests::test_pam_service_item_is_bounded_and_validated`, `config_tests::test_gdm_disable_flag_in_flag_dir_disables_every_gdm_service`, `config_tests::test_global_disabled_flag_in_flag_dir_disables_everything`, `config_tests::test_per_service_disable_flag_in_flag_dir`, `config_tests::test_per_service_flag_rejects_path_traversal_service_names`); integration tests through a real `pam_start` handle (`pam_handle_tests::test_pam_service_item_forwarded_to_daemon_when_no_service_argument`, `pam_handle_tests::test_gdm_disable_flag_honored_through_pam_service_item`, `pam_handle_tests::test_per_service_disable_flag_honored_through_pam_service_item`, `pam_handle_tests::test_explicit_service_argument_overrides_pam_service_item`); Docker matrix T12 (`gdm-password` line without `service=`, `/etc/soos/gdm.disable` → password prompt reached) | ✅ Verified |
| PDM3 | The `PAM_TEXT_INFO` feedback shown to the unauthenticated user never depends on `ReasonClass`: every `Verdict::Deny` (all 13 reasons, including `PadFailed`) reads `[soos] Face not recognized.`, every `Unavailable` / `ProtocolError` / IPC error reads `[soos] Face verification unavailable.` (no "spoof", no "camera"), at most two failure texts exist, and every such pathway returns `PAM_IGNORE` | Integration tests with a capturing conversation on a real `pam_start` handle (`pam_handle_tests::test_deny_feedback_is_identical_for_every_reason_class`, `pam_handle_tests::test_unavailable_feedback_is_a_single_generic_text`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-local-session-binding` (Review finding DMN-09 — GitHub #160)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| LSB1 | logind session records are parsed into `SessionRecord` (`UID`, `ACTIVE`/`STATE`, explicit `REMOTE=0/1`, `SEAT`, `CLASS`); malformed `REMOTE` stays unknown and empty `SEAT`/`CLASS` mean absent | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_session_record_parses_logind_fields`, `test_session_record_parses_ssh_session_as_remote_and_seatless`, `test_session_record_unknown_remote_value_is_none`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB2 | The peer PID maps to a logind session only through a single, well-formed `session-<id>.scope` cgroup component (cgroup v2 and v1 `name=systemd`); service, `user@.service`, empty, ambiguous and malformed/traversal IDs are unresolved | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_cgroup_v2_session_scope_is_resolved`, `test_cgroup_v1_named_systemd_hierarchy_is_resolved`, `test_cgroup_outside_session_scope_is_unresolved`, `test_cgroup_ambiguous_or_malformed_session_is_unresolved`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB3 | A root peer is tied to its own session: allowed only if that session belongs to the target UID and is active, `REMOTE=0`, seat-attached and `CLASS=user`; `sudo`/`su` from an SSH session, `su <target>` from another user's local or SSH session, `sshd` (no session), a missing/zero/negative PID, a vanished session, an inactive/seatless/greeter/unknown-remote session and a session without UID are denied | Integration tests with `MockLogind` (`session_policy_tests::test_root_peer_sudo_in_local_terminal_is_allowed`, `test_root_peer_from_ssh_session_is_denied_even_if_target_is_at_the_desk`, `test_root_peer_su_from_another_users_local_session_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_su_from_another_users_ssh_session_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_outside_any_session_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_without_kernel_pid_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_session_that_vanished_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_session_must_be_active_seated_local_user_class`, `test_root_peer_session_without_uid_is_foreign`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB4 | An unprivileged peer (`peer_uid == uid_hint`) needs an active session of its UID not flagged `REMOTE=1`; a remote-only or inactive user, and a peer for another UID, are denied; the preview `SessionValidator` also ignores `REMOTE=1` sessions | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_same_uid_peer_with_local_active_session_is_allowed`, `test_same_uid_peer_with_only_remote_sessions_is_denied`, `test_same_uid_peer_without_active_session_is_denied`, `test_unprivileged_peer_for_another_uid_is_denied`, `test_session_validator_ignores_remote_sessions`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB5 | Fail closed: every logind/procfs error (source failure, missing sessions directory, oversized cgroup file) denies; the disabled policy is reported as not enforced; denial reason codes are distinct and non-sensitive | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_root_peer_logind_unavailable_fails_closed`, `test_same_uid_peer_logind_unavailable_fails_closed`, `test_system_logind_missing_sessions_dir_fails_closed`, `test_system_logind_oversized_cgroup_file_fails_closed`, `test_disabled_policy_permits_and_reports_not_enforced`, `test_denial_reason_codes_are_distinct_and_non_empty`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB6 | `SystemLogind` reads fake `/run/systemd/sessions` and `/proc` trees end to end, skips `*.ref` entries, never follows unsafe session IDs out of the sessions directory, and `LocalSessionPolicy::from_validator` follows the configured directory and enforcement flag | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_system_logind_ties_root_peer_to_its_cgroup_session`, `test_system_logind_rejects_unsafe_session_ids`, `test_policy_from_validator_follows_enforcement_and_directory`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB7 | The dispatcher applies the policy at Step 6b of every `Auth` request with the kernel `SO_PEERCRED` PID and answers `ProtocolError`/`UidMismatch` on denial; a local seat session reaches the pipeline | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_dispatcher_denies_auth_when_target_has_only_remote_session`, `test_dispatcher_applies_injected_session_policy`; unchanged `policy_concurrency_tests::test_auth_rejected_for_uid_without_active_session`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB8 | Real-host behavior of GDM lock-screen reauthentication, `swaylock`/KDE lockers and `sudo` in a graphical terminal under the new binding | Manual check on a systemd-logind host | ⬜ Pending (hardware) |
| LSB9 | The user-manager UID is parsed strictly from the real desktop layouts (GNOME Terminal `vte-spawn-*.scope`, `org.gnome.Shell@wayland.service`, Konsole, cgroup v1 `name=systemd`, agreeing hybrid lines); non-user-manager paths (session scope, system services, `user-runtime-dir@`) are `None`; signed, suffixed, zero-padded, empty, overflowing or mismatched slice/service UIDs, a bare `user@<uid>.service` and disagreeing hierarchies are `UserManagerCgroupMalformed`; a `session-<id>.scope` nested in a user manager or service is not a session (`sd_pid_get_session` first-unit rule) | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_user_manager_cgroup_real_desktop_layouts_are_resolved`, `test_user_manager_cgroup_outside_user_manager_is_none`, `test_user_manager_cgroup_malformed_uid_fails_closed`, `test_cgroup_session_scope_nested_in_user_manager_is_not_a_session`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB10 | A root peer under the target's user manager is allowed when the target owns a local seat session and no remote session (`sudo` in a GNOME/KDE terminal, gnome-shell polkit agent); denied when the target also owns a remote session (active, closing or unknown-remote), for `user@<other>.service` (`su` from another user's terminal), for malformed UIDs and when the target has no local seat session; a resolved session scope keeps the session-scope rules | Integration tests with `MockLogind` (`session_policy_tests::test_root_peer_sudo_in_gnome_terminal_user_manager_is_allowed`, `test_root_peer_polkit_from_gnome_shell_user_manager_is_allowed`, `test_root_peer_user_manager_denied_when_target_has_remote_session`, `test_root_peer_su_other_user_from_user_manager_terminal_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_user_manager_malformed_uid_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_user_manager_without_local_seat_session_is_denied`, `test_root_peer_session_scope_takes_precedence_over_user_manager`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB11 | User-manager path fails closed: unreadable logind state and oversized cgroup files deny; the four user-manager denial codes are distinct from the existing ones | Integration tests (`session_policy_tests::test_root_peer_user_manager_logind_unavailable_fails_closed`, `test_system_logind_user_manager_oversized_cgroup_fails_closed`, `test_denial_reason_codes_cover_user_manager_variants`) | ✅ Verified |
| LSB12 | `SystemLogind` resolves the user-manager caller end to end over fake `/proc` and sessions trees (terminal and gnome-shell allowed, other user's terminal and malformed UID denied, vanished PID unresolved) and refuses it once the target also has an SSH session | Integration test (`session_policy_tests::test_system_logind_user_manager_caller_real_layout`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-inference-budget` (Review findings DMN-05 / DMN-06 — GitHub #158, #159)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DIB1 | Vision inference never runs on a Tokio worker: `InferenceGate::run` executes `VisionPipeline::process_frame` through `spawn_blocking`, so a Status request is answered while an inference is blocked, even on a single-threaded runtime | Integration test (`inference_budget_tests::test_158_status_request_served_while_inference_is_blocked`) | ✅ Verified |
| DIB2 | At most `MAX_CONCURRENT_INFERENCES` (1) inferences run at once; a request whose deadline cannot be met while the slot is busy gets a decoded `Unavailable`/`Timeout` response without running or queuing an inference; the permit moves into the blocking job and is released once the job ends (no background work left behind); a zero-sized gate is clamped to one slot | Integration tests (`inference_budget_tests::test_158_second_auth_never_queues_behind_busy_inference_gate`, `test_158_inference_gate_is_bounded_and_never_zero_sized`) | ✅ Verified |
| DIB3 | A panicking inference job fails closed with `Unavailable`/`InternalError` (never EOF, never `Allow`), releases its slot, and no panic payload is logged (`InferenceJobError` carries none) | Integration test (`inference_budget_tests::test_158_panicking_inference_returns_internal_error_not_eof`) | ✅ Verified |
| DIB4 | One `RequestDeadline` per request = min(client deadline − `RESPONSE_WRITE_MARGIN_MS`, request start + `connection_timeout` − `RESPONSE_WRITE_MARGIN_MS`); no client deadline (`0` / `u64::MAX`) falls back to `DECISION_BUDGET_MS`; time spent before the consensus loop (reads, checks, camera wake) is charged against the outer bound; an expired or margin-only client deadline leaves no budget | Unit tests (`inference_budget_tests::test_159_client_deadline_reserves_write_margin`, `test_159_time_spent_before_the_loop_counts_against_outer_timeout`, `test_159_missing_client_deadline_uses_decision_budget`, `test_159_expired_or_margin_only_client_deadline_has_no_budget`, `test_159_write_margin_and_estimate_constants`) | ✅ Verified |
| DIB5 | An inference starts only when the per-inference estimate fits the remaining budget (never once it is exhausted); the estimate is a bounded EMA of measured durations (`DEFAULT_INFERENCE_ESTIMATE_MS` 80, clamped to `MAX_INFERENCE_ESTIMATE_MS` 1000) | Unit tests (`inference_budget_tests::test_159_inference_starts_only_when_estimate_fits_remaining_budget`, `test_159_estimator_tracks_measured_latency_with_bounded_ema`) | ✅ Verified |
| DIB6 | When no inference fits (short client deadline or estimate above the budget) the dispatcher runs zero inferences, returns a decoded `Unavailable`/`Timeout` (PAM falls back to the password) and still records the rate-limit attempt | Integration tests (`inference_budget_tests::test_159_client_deadline_shorter_than_estimate_skips_inference`, `test_159_estimate_exceeding_budget_times_out_and_records_attempt`); existing `pipeline_integration_tests::*` and `policy_concurrency_tests::*` unchanged and green | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-peer-limits` (Review findings DMN-04 / GitHub #157, PAM-04 / DMN-03 / GitHub #175)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DPL1 | `[peer_limits]` defaults are bounded (2 connections per unprivileged UID, 2 permits reserved for root, 1024 requests and 30 s per connection, 5 `PasswordFailed` events per 10 s per peer) and valid for the default capacity; zero bounds and a root reservation not strictly below `max_concurrent_connections` are rejected at load time | Unit tests (`peer_limits_tests::test_peer_limits_defaults_are_bounded_and_valid`, `test_peer_limits_validate_rejects_unsafe_values`, `test_daemon_config_parses_peer_limits_section`, `test_daemon_config_rejects_reservation_not_below_capacity`) | ✅ Verified |
| DPL2 | Admission is keyed on the `SO_PEERCRED` UID: an unprivileged UID holds at most `max_connections_per_uid` permits, unprivileged peers can never take the root-reserved permits (8 unprivileged attempts leave a 9th root connection served), root is bounded only by the capacity, the reservation is clamped to leave one unprivileged permit, and the tracking table is bounded and released on drop | Unit tests (`peer_limits_tests::test_limiter_caps_connections_per_unprivileged_uid`, `test_limiter_reserves_permits_for_root_peers`, `test_limiter_root_is_not_subject_to_the_per_uid_cap`, `test_limiter_tracking_is_bounded_and_released`, `test_limiter_clamps_reservation_to_leave_one_unprivileged_permit`) | ✅ Verified |
| DPL3 | A connection over the per-UID cap is closed within 500 ms without any payload and without consuming a permit, held connections keep working, and one idle-polling UID leaves the remaining capacity to other peers; the PAM client maps the immediate close to `PAM_IGNORE` | Integration tests (`peer_limits_tests::test_same_uid_connection_over_cap_is_closed_without_blocking_others`, `test_idle_polling_peer_cannot_exhaust_global_capacity`; PAM fallback: `crates/pam/tests/ipc_tests.rs::test_ipc_daemon_crash_immediate_disconnect_returns_ignore`) | ✅ Verified |
| DPL4 | A connection is closed after `max_requests_per_connection` requests, after `max_connection_lifetime` even while the peer keeps polling, and right after an `Auth` response (one-shot) | Integration tests (`peer_limits_tests::test_connection_closed_after_max_requests`, `test_connection_closed_after_max_lifetime`, `test_auth_request_is_one_shot_per_connection`) | ✅ Verified |
| DPL5 | `PasswordFailed` events are rate limited per peer UID (root included): events beyond the quota are dropped and trigger no evidence snapshot | Integration test (`peer_limits_tests::test_password_failed_events_are_rate_limited_per_peer_uid`) | ✅ Verified |
| DPL6 | `PasswordFailed` target UID trust model (GitHub #175): an unprivileged peer may report only for itself (`event.uid` `None` or its own `SO_PEERCRED` UID); events claiming another UID (root included) are dropped with no snapshot; a root peer may report for any UID | Integration tests (`peer_limits_tests::test_unprivileged_peer_cannot_report_event_for_foreign_uid`, migrated `pipeline_integration_tests::test_12_4_password_failed_event_captures_evidence_snapshot`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `installer-preflight-and-deps` (Review findings ONB-06 / ONB-07 / ONB-09 — GitHub #164, #165, #167)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| INS1 | `scripts/install.sh` fails closed: an empty artifact directory or a single missing artifact (`soos-daemon`, `soos-admin`, `soos-enroll`, `soos-gui`, `libpam_soos.so`) exits non-zero, names every missing artifact, never reports success and leaves the destination untouched (read-only preflight before any write); `--allow-missing` is the only, explicit way to install a partial set and ends with an `INCOMPLETE` banner | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_fails_closed_when_artifact_dir_is_empty`, `test_install_fails_closed_when_one_artifact_is_missing`); Docker live run in `ubuntu:24.04` (walkthrough 93 §6) | ✅ Verified |
| INS2 | Artifacts come from `${CARGO_TARGET_DIR:-target}/release` or `--artifact-dir` only; `target/debug` is never searched and a cargo `debug` profile directory is refused unless `--allow-debug-artifacts` is passed; `--build` runs `scripts/check_build_deps.sh` then `cargo build --release --locked --workspace` (as `$SUDO_USER` under sudo) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_refuses_debug_profile_artifacts`, `test_install_orders_models_before_unit_enable_and_builds_release`) | ✅ Verified |
| INS3 | Every artifact is staged under its documented name and mode (`0755` binaries, `0644` `pam_soos.so`) with its exact content, through an atomic temp-file + rename, leaving no temporary file behind | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_stages_every_artifact_with_expected_modes`); Docker live run (`stat` of every installed path, `ubuntu:24.04` and `archlinux`) | ✅ Verified |
| INS4 | A live install (no `--destdir`) is refused for a non-root user before any provisioning step; pre-existing system directories (`usr/bin`, the PAM module directory) keep their mode while soos-owned directories are enforced (`biometrics` tightened to `0700`) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_live_mode_requires_root`, `test_install_never_chmods_preexisting_system_dirs`) | ✅ Verified |
| INS5 | Transactional install: when a step fails after files were written (model SHA-256 mismatch), every created file and directory is removed, every overwritten file gets its previous content and mode back, a `soos` group created by the run is deleted, a master key created by the run is removed, the unit is never enabled, and the run exits non-zero (`60`) reporting the rollback | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_rolls_back_every_change_on_failure`); Docker live run as root in `ubuntu:24.04` and `archlinux` (nothing named `*soos*` left, group removed) | ✅ Verified |
| INS6 | `--dry-run` runs the same read-only preflight (missing artifacts fail it) and never writes; models are deployed and verified before `systemctl enable`, which is the last mutating step; `soos-daemon.service` bounds restarts (`StartLimitIntervalSec`, `StartLimitBurst`) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_install_dry_run_runs_preflight_without_mutation`, `test_install_orders_models_before_unit_enable_and_builds_release`) | ✅ Verified |
| INS7 | `scripts/download_models.sh` has no Python dependency: the fixed manifest schema is parsed in bash, deployment, `--check-only` and tamper detection work with a PATH that exposes neither `python3` nor `curl`, and `--dry-run` on `models/manifest.toml` reports all three attested filenames and SHA-256 digests | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_download_models_deploys_and_verifies_without_python`, `test_download_models_parses_repository_manifest_without_python`); `ubuntu:24.04` and `archlinux` base images (no python) | ✅ Verified |
| INS8 | The manifest is validated before any write: bare file names only (no `/`, no leading `.`, no `../` traversal), 64-hex SHA-256, non-empty `https://` or `file://` source, at least one model; `curl` (and `sha256sum`) are preflighted, including by `--preflight`, before the target directory is created; downloads are `https`-only (`--proto '=https'`) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_download_models_rejects_malformed_or_unsafe_entries`, `test_download_models_preflight_requires_curl_for_remote_sources`) | ✅ Verified |
| INS9 | Dependency lists are complete and single-sourced: `scripts/check_build_deps.sh --print-packages <build\|gui\|models>` lists OpenSSL headers, libclang/clang, PAM headers, pkg-config and the C/C++ toolchain per distribution, `curl`/`ca-certificates` (never python) for models and the dlopen'ed GUI libraries; the preflight names a missing `cargo`; `debian/control` Build-Depends, `soos.spec` BuildRequires and `PKGBUILD` makedepends declare the OpenSSL/pkg-config/clang packages; the `.deb` recommends every GUI runtime library; `Docs/PACKAGING_AND_PROVISIONING.md` lists every package and documents `ORT_LIB_LOCATION`, and `README.md` documents the installation | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::installer_contract::test_check_build_deps_lists_required_packages`, `test_packaging_and_docs_declare_complete_dependencies`); `cargo build --release --locked --workspace` succeeded in bare `ubuntu:24.04`, `fedora:40` and `archlinux` containers with only the documented build lists plus rustup (walkthrough 93 §6) | ✅ Verified |
| INS10 | `install.sh --destdir` without `--pam-dir` never derives the PAM directory from the build host: an existing directory inside the stage is used, otherwise `/usr/lib/security` with a warning naming `--pam-dir`; nothing is staged under `lib64` | Invariant test (`installer_contract::test_install_destdir_never_guesses_pam_dir_from_build_host`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-packaging-rollback` (Review findings ONB-03 / GitHub #161, ONB-08 / GitHub #166)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DNP1 | The Debian `soos-notify` profile is `Auth-Type: Primary`, Priority `12` (after `unix` 256 and the other standard primary methods), control `[default=ignore]` on `Auth` and `Auth-Initial` (never `success=`, `optional` or `sufficient`); `soos` stays Primary 260 with `[success=done default=ignore]`; docs no longer describe the former `Additional`/128 profile | Invariant test (`soos-invariants::tests::test_debian_notify_profile_sits_between_pam_unix_and_pam_deny`) | ✅ Verified |
| DNP2 | The `common-auth` generated by `pam-auth-update --package --enable soos soos-notify` orders the primary `pam_soos.so` rule (default timeout since #185) < `pam_unix.so` < `pam_soos.so event=password-failed` < `pam_deny.so` < `pam_permit.so`, and the `pam_unix` `success=N` jump lands exactly on `pam_permit.so`; a `pam_exec` probe at the hook position is reached on a wrong password only, the outcome being unchanged | Docker validation D2–D4 (`tests/docker/pam_rollback_test.sh`, `ubuntu:24.04`) | ✅ Verified |
| DNP3 | With the real release `pam_soos.so` and a recording mock daemon (`Verdict::Deny`), a wrong password through the generated `common-auth` is rejected and emits exactly one `PasswordFailed` event; a correct password is accepted and emits none | Docker PAM matrix T11 (`tests/docker/test_suite.sh`, `tests/docker/mock_daemon.py --record`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRB1 | `scripts/pam_snapshot.sh snapshot` records dereferenced copies of every `/etc/pam.d` entry and `/etc/nsswitch.conf` with a `SHA256SUMS` manifest (plus `authselect current --raw` on live hosts) in `/var/lib/soos/state/pam-backup` (`0700`, nothing group/world-writable), never overwrites an existing snapshot and refuses symlinked state paths; `verify` exits 0/1/2 (identical / differences listed / no snapshot) using bash only and fails closed; `scripts/install.sh` snapshots before installing any PAM template (live installs only) | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::tests::test_pam_snapshot_helper_records_and_verifies_pre_install_state`, `test_pam_rollback_is_wired_and_documented`); Docker D1/D2/F1 (verification reports every activated file) | ✅ Verified |
| PRB2 | `soos-admin gdm enable` keeps a byte-exact `gdm-password.soos-backup` (original mode, group/world write stripped, never overwritten), replaces the file atomically (exclusive temp file, `fchown`, `fsync`, `rename`, directory `fsync`), leaves no temporary file, is idempotent and refuses symlinked or non-regular PAM files without modification | Integration tests (`soos-admin-cli` `gdm_tests`: `test_gdm_enable_creates_byte_exact_backup_with_original_mode`, `test_gdm_enable_never_overwrites_an_existing_backup`, `test_gdm_enable_is_atomic_and_idempotent`, `test_gdm_enable_refuses_a_symlinked_pam_file`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRB3 | `scripts/uninstall.sh` restores `*.soos-backup` files atomically, then removes residual `pam_soos.so` lines only when safe (stripped file equals its snapshot copy, or no `success=N` jump), verifies the result against the snapshot and discards it only when identical; otherwise the file is untouched, `pam_soos.so` is kept (degrades to `PAM_IGNORE`), the snapshot is kept and the script exits 1 | Invariant tests (`soos-invariants::tests::test_uninstall_restores_pre_install_pam_state_byte_for_byte`, `test_uninstall_keeps_module_when_pam_rollback_is_unsafe`, `test_uninstall_restores_pam_config`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRB4 | End to end, activation followed by `scripts/uninstall.sh --keep-data --skip-systemd` returns every `/etc/pam.d` entry (type, link target, sha256), `/etc/nsswitch.conf`, the `pam-auth-update` profile list and `authselect current --raw` to their pre-install values; no file references `pam_soos.so`; password login works (correct accepted, wrong rejected); exposed as `./run_tests.sh rollback` and the CI job `pam-rollback` | Docker validation D5 (`ubuntu:24.04`, incl. a `gdm-password` edited like `soos-admin gdm enable`) and F2 (`fedora:40`, `custom/soos with-faillock` restored to `local with-faillock`) in `tests/docker/pam_rollback_test.sh` | ✅ Verified |
| PRB5 | `scripts/uninstall.sh --purge-data` keeps `/var/lib/soos/state/pam-backup` (and says so) when the PAM rollback is incomplete or unverified, while still purging the key, templates and evidence; after a verified rollback the whole state directory is purged | Invariant tests (`test_uninstall_purge_keeps_snapshot_when_pam_rollback_is_incomplete`, `test_uninstall_purge_removes_state_after_verified_rollback`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRB6 | A PAM snapshot that fails part-way leaves no `state/.pam-backup.*` temp directory; `install.sh` block 6a journals a snapshot it creates before invoking the helper, so a failing live install (after 6a, or inside the helper) rolls back to a byte-identical PAM state with no `/var/lib/soos`, binaries, module or `soos` group | Invariant tests (`test_pam_snapshot_failure_leaves_no_temporary_directory`, `test_install_journals_pam_snapshot_before_invoking_helper`); Docker `tests/docker/pam_rollback_test.sh` D0a/D0b (CI job `pam-rollback`) | ✅ Verified |
