Red evidence: `/workspace/tests/distro/${distro}_test.sh` | ✅ Verified |
| DVM5 | `--dry-run` executes nothing privileged; without Docker the runner fails and executes nothing (no live fallback); `--skip-docker` and direct live runs of `debian_ubuntu_test.sh`, `fedora_rhel_test.sh`, `arch_linux_test.sh` require `--allow-host-changes` (exit 2 otherwise), which the runner passes only inside the disposable container | Behavioral invariant tests with recording shims for 30 privileged commands first in `PATH` (`test_distro_runner_dry_run_executes_nothing_privileged`, `test_distro_runner_without_docker_refuses_live_host_run`, `test_distro_scripts_refuse_host_mutation_without_consent`, `test_distro_runner_grants_consent_only_inside_container`). Red evidence: the runner invoked `docker build` with Docker declared absent | ✅ Verified |
| DVM6 | After a native package install the distro scripts record the mode actually used (`deb`, `rpm`, `pkgbuild`) so rollback goes through `dpkg -r` / `rpm -e` / `pacman -R` | Invariant test (`test_distro_scripts_record_the_install_mode_actually_used`) | ✅ Verified |

| DVM8 | Distro tests enroll through `soos-enroll --mock enroll --username testuser --yes` (mock camera, synthetic inference, real encrypted store), assert `/var/lib/soos/biometrics/<uid>.cbor.enc` is `600 root:root`, and `soos-enroll --mock verify` accepts it; no fabricated `/dev/urandom` `.bin` template | Invariant test (`test_distro_tests_enroll_through_soos_enroll_mock`); Docker: `run_distro_validation.sh fedora` (RPM path, fedora:40), `debian_ubuntu_test.sh --install-sh` (ubuntu:24.04) and `arch_linux_test.sh --install-sh` (archlinux) pass end to end (walkthrough 92 §8) | ✅ Verified |
| DVM9 | The Ubuntu packaging and deployment path runs in CI on every pull request and gates the merge: job `package-deploy` (after `lint`, SHA-pinned checkout, no persisted credentials, no event interpolation) runs `./tests/distro/run_distro_validation.sh ubuntu` and `tests/docker/test_packages.sh`, and is listed in the `CI Success` `needs` | Invariant test (`distro_matrix::test_ci_runs_ubuntu_package_deployment_on_pull_requests`); both commands passed locally in Docker (walkthrough 92 §8.2); first green CI run on `main` (push `e2f602c`, 2026-09-30): https://github.com/Mysticaly622/soos/actions/runs/36745249037/job/109989794002 | ✅ Verified |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| GSO1 | Ubuntu 24.04 `gdm-password`: the managed block (`GDM_BLOCK_BEGIN`, `GDM_PAM_LINE`, `GDM_BLOCK_END`) lands after `pam_nologin.so` and `pam_succeed_if.so`, immediately before `@include common-auth`; every original line is kept verbatim and in order; no guard is added when the delegated stack has no gate | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_ubuntu_inserts_after_nologin_and_succeed_if_before_common_auth`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO2 | Gates of a delegated stack that run before its first credential module (`pam_faillock.so preauth`, `pam_nologin.so`, `pam_shells.so`, `pam_succeed_if.so`, `pam_access.so`, `pam_listfile.so`, `pam_securetty.so` with a plain `required`/`requisite` control) are copied, in order, in front of `pam_soos.so`, following `include`/`substack`/`@include` up to `MAX_PAM_INCLUDE_DEPTH` (4) levels; rules after the credential module (`authfail`, `authsucc`) are never copied; conditional (jump) gates before it are refused (GSO11) | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_ubuntu_faillock_profile_adds_preauth_guard_before_soos`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_fedora_never_inserts_at_top_and_guards_faillock_preauth`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_arch_guards_shells_nologin_and_faillock_from_nested_includes`); unit tests (`pam_stack::tests::test_guard_requires_plain_control_and_preauth`, `pam_stack::tests::test_parse_include_forms`, `pam_stack::tests::test_parse_bracket_control_and_optional_type`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO3 | `pam_soos.so` is never inserted at index 0: on Fedora 40 it follows `pam_selinux_permit.so` and precedes `substack password-auth`; in a self-contained stack it follows an in-file `pam_faillock.so preauth` (not duplicated) and precedes `pam_unix.so`; the flattened auth stack is asserted gate-before-soos-before-`pam_unix` on every fixture | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_fedora_never_inserts_at_top_and_guards_faillock_preauth`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_inline_faillock_inserts_after_preauth_before_pam_unix`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO4 | Fail closed without modification or backup: no credential/shared-stack anchor, an unclassified rule before the anchor (GSO11), a `[...=N]` jump that would change target, non-UTF-8 content, line continuations, files over `MAX_PAM_FILE_BYTES` (64 KiB), include chains deeper than 4 levels, unbalanced managed blocks | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_a_stack_without_a_credential_anchor`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_when_a_jump_crosses_the_insertion_point`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_non_utf8_pam_file`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO5 | Migration and idempotency: a misplaced `LEGACY_GDM_PAM_LINE` (`auth  sufficient  pam_soos.so timeout_ms=2500`, pre-#177 releases) is removed and re-inserted at the safe position, the new backup holds the stack without any soos rule; a second enable is a no-op; a `pam_soos.so` rule written by the administrator is left untouched | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_migrates_a_misplaced_legacy_line`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_is_idempotent_on_distribution_stacks`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_leaves_an_admin_managed_soos_rule_untouched`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO6 | `soos-admin gdm restore` atomically puts back the `.soos-backup` bytes and mode, removes the backup and leaves no temporary file; it fails without any change when there is no backup, the backup is a symlink or exceeds `MAX_PAM_FILE_BYTES`; the backup is checked and read through one `O_NOFOLLOW` descriptor | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_restore_puts_back_the_pristine_file_and_removes_the_backup`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_restore_without_backup_fails_and_changes_nothing`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_restore_refuses_a_symlinked_backup`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_restore_refuses_an_oversized_backup`, `gdm_tests::test_gdm_restore_and_module_dir_parsing`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO7 | `GDM_PAM_LINE` = `auth  [success=done default=ignore]  pam_soos.so timeout_ms=2500` (explicit control shared with every packaged soos rule, no `service=`); `enable` edits the PAM file before removing `gdm.disable`, so a refused stack keeps the flag | Unit tests (`gdm_tests::test_gdm_pam_line_includes_timeout_ms_2500`, `gdm_tests::test_gdm_status_configured_enabled`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO8 | The `soos-admin gdm enable` binary refuses to edit the PAM file unless `pam_soos.so` is a regular file in `--pam-module-dir` or one of `DEFAULT_PAM_MODULE_DIRS` | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_soos_admin_gdm_enable_refuses_when_module_is_not_installed`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_find_pam_module_locates_the_installed_module`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_find_pam_module_ignores_directories_named_like_the_module`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO9 | Traceability: every `gdm_tests` / `gdm_stack_order_tests` test reference in this matrix resolves to an existing test function and `GDM_PAM_LINE` exists | Invariant test (`test_matrix_gdm_references_resolve_to_real_tests`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO11 | Anchor classification: the insertion anchor is only a delegation (`include`/`substack`/`@include`) or one of `CREDENTIAL_MODULES` (`pam_unix`, `pam_sss`, `pam_ldap`, `pam_krb5`, `pam_winbind`, `pam_systemd_home`, `pam_fprintd`); any unclassified auth rule before it, in the edited file (`pam_tally2.so` before `@include common-auth`; `pam_group.so` between `pam_nologin.so` and an `@include` whose stack runs `pam_faillock.so preauth`; an unknown module before `pam_unix.so`) or in a delegated stack before its credential module (unknown module, conditional gate), and an unresolvable (absolute) include target make `enable` refuse with an error naming the rule, leaving the file byte-for-byte unchanged, no backup, no temporary file and the `gdm.disable` flag in place | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_unclassified_lockout_module_before_include`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_unclassified_module_before_include_with_faillock`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_unclassified_module_before_inline_credential`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_unclassified_module_inside_delegated_stack`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_conditional_gate_inside_delegated_stack`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_an_unresolvable_include_target`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_accepts_documented_credential_modules_as_anchor`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO12 | A delegated stack that ends without a credential module does not end the scan: the rules after the delegation in the edited file are classified the same way (gates copied in front of `pam_soos.so`, unclassified rule refused, credential module ends the scan) | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_scans_past_a_gate_only_include`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO13 | PAM type and control keywords are case-insensitive (`pam.conf(5)`) in gate classification, delegation detection and jump detection: a capitalized delegated `Auth Requisite pam_faillock.so preauth` is copied before `pam_soos.so` (normalized to lowercase), a capitalized unclassified `Auth required pam_tally2.so` in a delegated stack refuses, a capitalized `Auth [success=1 ...]` jump in the edited file is detected, and `auth Include` delegates | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_copies_a_capitalized_delegated_gate`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_a_capitalized_unclassified_delegated_rule`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_detects_a_capitalized_jump_before_the_insertion_point`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_follows_a_capitalized_include_control`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO14 | Gate de-duplication: a delegated gate is skipped only when an earlier rule of the edited file runs it with a plain `required`/`requisite` control; an earlier `optional pam_nologin.so` or `optional pam_faillock.so preauth` does not stop the enforcing delegated gate from being copied | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_copies_requisite_nologin_despite_an_earlier_optional_one`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_copies_required_preauth_despite_an_earlier_optional_one`, `gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_does_not_duplicate_an_earlier_enforcing_gate`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO15 | A delegated stack missing from the PAM directory (possibly resolved by libpam from a vendor directory such as `/usr/lib/pam.d`, which is never searched) or failing to read makes `enable` refuse with an error naming the target: file byte-for-byte unchanged, no backup, no temporary file, `gdm.disable` kept | Integration tests (`gdm_stack_order_tests::test_gdm_enable_refuses_a_missing_include_target`, `gdm_tests::test_gdm_enable_is_atomic_and_idempotent`) | ✅ Verified |
| GSO10 | Real GDM login and lock-screen unlock with the managed block on Ubuntu 24.04, Fedora 40 and Arch (face accepted; locked `pam_faillock` account refused even with a matching face; `gdm restore` returns to password-only) | Manual check on real hosts | ⬜ Pending (hardware) |

## Component: `gui-import-stdin` (Review findings CAM-08 / STO-12 — GitHub #156)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| ISE1 | GUI import finalize pipes the fused embedding (JSON, zeroizing buffer) to the stdin of `soos-enroll import --uid <uid> --file -`; no file is created under `temp_dir()` while the helper runs or afterwards | `crates/gui/tests/import_privacy_tests.rs` (`test_import_helper_args_use_stdin`, `test_import_finalize_pipes_embedding_without_temp_file` with a recording `/bin/sh` helper that lists `temp_dir()` during the call). Red evidence: `unresolved imports soos_gui::privileged::import_helper_args, import_template_with` | ✅ Verified |
| ISE2 | A failing helper, or one exiting without reading its stdin, is reported as an error and never hangs the privileged worker | `test_import_reports_helper_failure`, `test_import_does_not_hang_when_helper_ignores_stdin` | ✅ Verified |
| ISE3 | No silent `/tmp` fallback: `privileged.rs`, `main.rs` and `store_mode.rs` never use `temp_dir`, `soos-gui-master.key` or `soos-gui-biometrics`; an inaccessible system store selects `GuiStore::Polkit` (no local store) and creates nothing under `temp_dir()` | `test_import_and_store_code_never_use_temp_dir`, `test_accessible_system_store_is_used_directly`, `test_inaccessible_system_store_selects_polkit_without_tmp_fallback`. Red evidence: `unresolved import soos_gui::store_mode` | ✅ Verified |
| ISE4 | A local store outside the system store exists only through the explicit `--dev-store <DIR>` (absolute) developer mode, which never imports into the system store and carries a banner naming the directory and stating it is not used by PAM | `test_dev_store_is_explicit_and_announced`, `test_gui_args_accept_dev_store`. Red evidence: `no field dev_store on type GuiArgs` | ✅ Verified |
| ISE5 | `soos-enroll import --file -` reads the embedding from stdin; every input is capped at `MAX_IMPORT_INPUT_BYTES` (64 KiB) while reading (endless reader consumes at most cap + 1 bytes) | `crates/enrollment-cli/tests/import_stdin_tests.rs` (`test_import_stdin_marker_is_accepted_by_the_cli`, `test_import_reads_embedding_from_stdin`, `test_import_stdin_rejects_oversized_input_while_reading`). Red evidence: `no method named import_from_reader found for struct EnrollmentService` | ✅ Verified |
| ISE6 | Import refuses malformed JSON, wrong dimension and non-finite values (`1e39` overflowing to infinity); nothing is stored on refusal | `test_import_rejects_malformed_or_non_finite_embeddings`; existing `import_tests.rs` unchanged and green | ✅ Verified |
| ISE7 | A file input is opened `O_NOFOLLOW`, must be a regular file within 64 KiB and, under pkexec, owned by `PKEXEC_UID` (checked on the open descriptor); `PKEXEC_UID` must be a decimal UID | `test_import_file_rejects_oversized_file`, `test_import_file_owner_must_match_pkexec_uid`, `test_import_file_refuses_symlinks_and_non_regular_files`, `test_parse_pkexec_uid` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `enroll-fusion-target` (Review findings STO-09 / STO-10 / STO-11 — GitHub #182, #183, #184)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| GEF1 | Guided enrollment rejects non-finite, zero-norm, oversized (> `MAX_GUIDED_EMBEDDING_DIM`) and dimension-inconsistent embeddings (`InvalidEmbedding`) and non-finite poses; rejected samples are never recorded | Integration tests (`guided_enrollment_consistency_tests`: `test_non_finite_embeddings_are_rejected`, `test_zero_norm_embedding_is_rejected`, `test_dimension_mismatch_and_oversized_embeddings_are_rejected`, `test_non_finite_pose_is_rejected`) | ✅ Verified |
| GEF2 | A sample is fused only if its cosine similarity is >= `MIN_SAMPLE_CONSISTENCY_COSINE` (0.5) with every accepted frontal sample and, off-axis, with the frontal mean direction (`IdentityMismatch` otherwise); a second identity during any step does not contaminate the template, whose composite equals the frontal direction; fusion is the mean direction and re-validates every sample | Integration tests (`test_inconsistent_frontal_sample_is_rejected`, `test_second_identity_during_off_axis_steps_does_not_contaminate_template`, `test_consistent_off_axis_sample_above_threshold_is_accepted`); existing `guided_enrollment_tests::test_guided_enrollment_full_workflow` unchanged and green | ✅ Verified |
| GEF3 | Documented pose ranges are enforced: turn yaw in `[10, 25]` degrees, tilt pitch in `[-25, -8]`, secondary rotation <= 15, roll <= 10 (`PoseOutOfRange` beyond the upper bound, e.g. yaw -80 or pitch -60); at most `MAX_SAMPLES_PER_STEP` (20) samples per step | Integration tests (`test_extreme_turn_left_is_out_of_range`, `test_extreme_turn_right_is_out_of_range`, `test_tilt_up_enforces_documented_range`, `test_off_axis_steps_reject_combined_extreme_rotations`, `test_sample_count_is_bounded_per_step`, `test_consistency_threshold_and_bounds_are_documented_constants`) | ✅ Verified |
| ETU1 | Without `--uid`/`--username`, the enrollment target is the invoking user: the real UID when non-root, otherwise `SUDO_UID` then `PKEXEC_UID` when non-root; root is never an implicit target (`TargetUserRequired`), a malformed invoker UID fails closed (`InvalidInvokerUid`), `--uid 0` enrolls root explicitly; the target UID is printed before capture | Integration tests (`soos-enrollment-cli` `enroll_target_tests`: `test_default_target_under_sudo_uses_sudo_uid`, `test_default_target_under_pkexec_uses_pkexec_uid`, `test_default_target_prefers_sudo_uid_over_pkexec_uid`, `test_default_target_never_silently_root`, `test_default_target_sudo_uid_zero_is_not_an_implicit_root_target`, `test_default_target_malformed_sudo_uid_fails_closed`, `test_default_target_unprivileged_caller_is_real_uid`, `test_explicit_root_target_is_allowed`). Red evidence: unresolved `resolve_default_target_uid` | ✅ Verified |
| EMP1 | `soos-enroll enroll` records the loaded embedding model by default: `--model-id` defaults to `MODEL_ID_EMBEDDING` (`arcface_w600k_mbf`) and `--model-version` to `EMBEDDING_MODEL_VERSION`, pinned to the `models/manifest.toml` version (`2.0.0`); the retired `mobilefacenet`/`1.0.0` default is gone and the stored template carries the loaded model | Integration tests (`soos-enrollment-cli` `enroll_model_provenance_tests`: `test_embedding_model_constants_match_attested_manifest`, `test_enroll_cli_defaults_record_loaded_embedding_model`, `test_enroll_default_args_persist_loaded_embedding_model_id`); `scaffold_tests::test_cli_parse_enroll_subcommand_with_uid` (default assertions migrated with user approval 2026-09-30). Red evidence: unresolved `EMBEDDING_MODEL_VERSION` | ✅ Verified |
| EMP2 | An explicit `--model-id`/`--model-version` differing from the loaded model is flagged (`EnrollmentSummary::model_overridden`) and warned about before capture | Integration test (`test_enroll_explicit_model_override_is_flagged_in_summary`); existing `enroll_tests` (explicit override recorded verbatim) unchanged and green | ✅ Verified |
| EMP3 | `soos-daemon` refuses an enrolled template whose `model_id` differs from the loaded embedding extractor (`pipeline::EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID`, also used to load the session): `Verdict::Unavailable` / `ReasonClass::ModelUnavailable` without matching (PAM falls back); a matching template is evaluated normally; `main.rs` binds the dispatcher with `with_expected_embedding_model(EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID)` | Integration tests (`soos-daemon` `template_model_binding_tests`: `test_template_with_foreign_model_id_is_refused`, `test_template_with_matching_model_id_is_evaluated`, `test_embedding_model_id_is_attested_by_manifest`, `test_production_wiring_binds_templates_to_loaded_embedding_model`). Red evidence: no method `with_expected_embedding_model` | ✅ Verified |
| EMP4 | Legacy alias removed (owner decision 2026-10-01, GitHub #278): a template recording `mobilefacenet` / `1.0.0` (any version, case or whitespace variant) or an unrelated id (e.g. `facenet_512`, the retired `arcface_w600k_mbf`) is classified `Foreign` by `pipeline::classify_template_model` and refused with `Unavailable` / `ModelUnavailable` | Integration tests (`template_model_binding_tests`: `test_legacy_alias_template_is_refused`, `test_legacy_alias_with_other_version_is_refused`, `test_unrelated_foreign_model_id_is_refused`, `test_classify_template_model_accepts_only_the_loaded_model`, `test_retired_cli_default_alias_is_foreign`) *(the 2026-09-30 alias tests, which accepted the alias, were migrated by GitHub #278)* | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `vision-mjpeg-bounds` (Review finding VIS-02 — GitHub #190)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| MJB1 | A compressed MJPEG buffer larger than `MAX_MJPEG_COMPRESSED_BYTES` (16 MiB) is rejected before decoding with `VisionError::MjpegInputTooLarge { max, actual }`; a buffer of exactly the bound reaches the decoder | Unit tests (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::test_mjpeg_compressed_input_above_limit_rejected`, `test_mjpeg_compressed_input_at_limit_is_not_rejected_for_size`, `test_mjpeg_limits_are_sane`) | ✅ Verified |
| MJB2 | Headers are parsed before any pixel decoding and the SOF width/height must equal the negotiated frame: a transposed RGB or greyscale frame with the same byte count fails with `MjpegFrameMismatch` | Unit tests (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::test_mjpeg_dimension_mismatch_fails_closed`, `test_mjpeg_grey_dimension_mismatch_fails_closed`); property `prop_mjpeg_header_geometry_must_match` | ✅ Verified |
| MJB3 | A 65535×65535 SOF header is rejected from the header alone (no allocation from the header, under 1 s), and a negotiated frame above `MAX_MJPEG_DIMENSION` (4096) is rejected with `InvalidDimensions` | Unit tests (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::test_mjpeg_oversized_header_rejected`, `test_mjpeg_frame_dimensions_above_limit_rejected`) | ✅ Verified |
| MJB4 | Only 8-bit YCbCr/RGB and 8-bit greyscale JPEGs are accepted; CMYK (and 16-bit) frames fail closed with `ColorConversionFailed` instead of returning a buffer of arbitrary size | Unit test (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::test_mjpeg_cmyk_frame_rejected`) | ✅ Verified |
| MJB5 | Well-formed RGB and greyscale frames still decode to exactly `width * height * 3` bytes (greyscale replicated to three channels); empty and truncated frames fail closed | Unit tests (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::test_mjpeg_valid_rgb_frame_decodes_to_exact_rgb_len`, `test_mjpeg_valid_grey_frame_expands_to_rgb`, `test_mjpeg_empty_and_truncated_input_fail_closed`); existing `color_tests::test_mjpeg_corrupt_data_fails_closed` | ✅ Verified |
| MJB6 | The decoder entry point never panics: arbitrary bytes (with or without SOI) and mutated real frames with arbitrary SOF dimensions return a typed error or exactly `width * height * 3` bytes, within the header-rejection deadline | Properties (`mjpeg_bounds_tests::prop_mjpeg_arbitrary_bytes_never_panic`, `prop_mjpeg_mutated_frames_fail_closed`, 512 cases each) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `embedding-model-facts` (Review finding VIS-03 — GitHub #191)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| EMR1 | `input_shape` is the logical `[N, C, H, W]` shape and the optional `input_layout` (`"NCHW"` default, `"NHWC"`) gives the physical layout; `expected_input_dims` permutes NHWC to `[N, H, W, C]`, requires rank 4 for NHWC, and an unknown layout fails manifest parsing | Unit tests (`manifest_shape_tests::test_input_layout_defaults_to_nchw`, `test_input_layout_nhwc_permutes_logical_shape`, `test_input_layout_nhwc_requires_rank_four`, `test_unknown_input_layout_is_rejected_at_parse_time`) | ✅ Verified |
| EMR2 | `ModelMetadata::validate_session_shapes` requires exactly one input and, when declared, the same number of outputs; ranks and every concrete dim (including zero) must match; negative (symbolic) session dims are wildcards; any disagreement is `InferenceError::ModelShapeMismatch` | Unit tests (`manifest_shape_tests::test_validate_exact_static_shapes_ok`, `test_validate_dynamic_dims_are_wildcards`, `test_validate_layout_mismatch_fails_closed`, `test_validate_concrete_dim_mismatch_fails_closed`, `test_validate_rank_mismatch_fails_closed`, `test_validate_input_and_output_counts_fail_closed`, `test_validate_without_declared_outputs_checks_input_only`, `test_validate_zero_session_dim_is_not_a_wildcard`) | ✅ Verified |
| EMR3 | `ModelRegistry::get_or_load_session` validates the session shapes after the SHA-256 check and before caching: the real embedding model (SFace, NCHW) under an NHWC manifest is rejected with `ModelShapeMismatch`, and all three shipped models load under the committed manifest | Real-model tests, skipped without models (`embedding_real_model_tests::test_registry_rejects_real_embedding_model_under_nhwc_manifest`, `test_real_models_all_pass_committed_manifest_shape_validation`) *(the ArcFace-era test under an NCHW manifest was migrated by GitHub #278)* | ✅ Verified |
| EMR4 | The attested embedding model metadata is pinned: 136,619,444 bytes, one input `input_1` `[-1, 112, 112, 3]` (NHWC), one output `embedding` `[-1, 512]`, and the committed manifest validates against the session | Real-model tests (`embedding_real_model_tests::test_real_embedding_model_io_metadata_is_pinned`, `test_real_embedding_committed_manifest_matches_session`) | ✅ Verified |
| EMR5 | The committed manifest describes the shipped network truthfully (SFace, NCHW, 128D, RGB; never the retired ArcFace / ResNet34) and declares `input_layout = "NCHW"` for the embedding model | Unit tests (`manifest_shape_tests::test_workspace_manifest_embedding_description_is_truthful`, `test_workspace_manifest_declares_embedding_layout_nchw`, `test_workspace_manifest_nchw_models_keep_default_layout`) *(the NHWC ArcFace pins were migrated by GitHub #278)* | ✅ Verified |
| EMR6 | The production extractor accepts the NCHW layout of the real SFace model and emits a finite, deterministic, L2-normalized 128D embedding on a synthetic crop; the embedding latency is measured (p50 9.8 ms / p95 11.0 ms on one intra-op thread; the retired ArcFace measured p50 127.5 ms / p95 170.9 ms) and p95 stays below `MAX_INFERENCE_ESTIMATE_MS` (1000 ms) | Real-model tests (`embedding_real_model_tests::test_real_embedding_extractor_uses_nchw_and_emits_normalized_128d`, `test_real_embedding_latency_report`) | ✅ Verified |
| EMR7 | Backward compatibility: a manifest entry without `input_layout` (earlier installed release) leaves the layout unspecified (`input_layout_declared = false`): the logical shape is accepted in NCHW or NHWC order, while rank, dims, input count and outputs are still enforced and a one-time warning is logged; an explicit layout is always enforced (wrong value fails closed); the real NHWC embedding model loads under such a legacy manifest; the committed manifest declares the embedding layout explicitly | Unit tests (`manifest_shape_tests::test_input_layout_declared_flag_tracks_the_manifest_field`, `test_absent_layout_accepts_either_layout_of_the_logical_shape`, `test_absent_layout_still_rejects_wrong_dims`, `test_declared_layout_is_enforced_both_ways`, `test_layout_declaration_round_trips_through_serde`, `test_workspace_manifest_declares_embedding_layout_explicitly`); real-model test (`embedding_real_model_tests::test_real_embedding_model_loads_under_manifest_without_input_layout`) | ✅ Verified |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |

| PSC4 | The physical PAD session of `adversarial_test.sh` verifies against the provisioned store (no temporary empty store; `-b`/`-k` overrides), refuses to measure when the target UID has no template (`list --format json` pre-flight), and aborts with exit 2 when `verify` fails without printing a verdict, so an operational error is never counted as a rejected attack | Architectural invariant test; container runs confirmed the pre-flight refusal (exit 1) and the no-verdict abort (exit 2) | ✅ Verified (`test_adversarial_physical_session_uses_an_enrolled_store`) |

## Component: `camera-capture-format-fps` (Review findings CAM-10 / CAM-11 / CAM-12 — GitHub #192, #193, #194)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| CFP1 | The V4L2 capture path honours the driver-returned `VIDIOC_S_FMT` result: a substituted fourcc is adopted and stamped on frames only when its layout is known (`RGB3`, `YUYV`, `NV12`, `MJPG`, `GREY`/`Y800`/`Y8  `); `BGR3`, `RGB4` and unknown fourccs, zero or odd-NV12 dimensions and a stride below one row fail as `CameraError::SetFormat`; `bytesperline == 0` means tight rows (GitHub #192) | Integration tests (`capture_validation_tests::test_validate_negotiated_format_accepts_exact_fourcc`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_labels_known_substitution`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_rejects_unknown_substitution`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_rejects_bgr_and_rgb32_substitution`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_accepts_grey_aliases`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_rejects_stride_smaller_than_row`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_zero_stride_means_tight_rows`, `test_validate_negotiated_format_rejects_invalid_dimensions`) | ✅ Verified |
| CFP2 | Every published frame is size-validated: `V4L2_BUF_FLAG_ERROR` buffers are skipped, `bytesused` beyond the mapping is rejected, short uncompressed buffers are rejected, padded rows are de-strided so the payload equals `PixelFormat::expected_buffer_size`, MJPEG keeps exactly `bytesused` bytes and an empty payload is rejected (GitHub #192; also exercises the `bytesused` slice of GARP2) | Integration & unit tests (`capture_validation_tests::test_validate_captured_buffer_rejects_short_yuyv`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_accepts_exact_yuyv`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_rejects_error_flag`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_destrides_padded_rows`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_destrides_nv12_planes`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_short_padded_frame_rejected`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_bytesused_handling`, `test_validate_captured_buffer_mjpeg_keeps_payload_and_rejects_empty`; `capture::test_error_flag_frame_skipped`, `capture::test_short_frame_never_published`, `capture::test_padded_frame_published_tightly_packed`) | ✅ Verified |
| CFP3 | A run of `MAX_CONSECUTIVE_REJECTED_FRAMES` (30) invalid buffers ends the stream with `BufferDequeue` so the supervisor backs off and reopens the device instead of dropping frames silently forever | Unit tests (`capture::test_consecutive_invalid_frames_reopen_device`; `capture_validation_tests::test_rejected_frame_streak_is_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |
| CFP4 | `fps` is requested from the hardware with `VIDIOC_S_PARM` through `capture::apply_frame_rate` (`requested_frame_interval`, zero rate falls back to 30 fps); the granted interval, possibly adjusted by the driver, is logged and drives the poll timeout (`FrameRateOutcome::granted_fps`), and a refusal is a non-fatal warning (`FrameRateOutcome::Refused`) (GitHub #193) | Integration tests (`capture_validation_tests::test_requested_frame_interval_uses_configured_fps`, `test_granted_fps_from_driver_interval`, `test_apply_frame_rate_accepted_request`, `test_apply_frame_rate_adjusted_request`, `test_apply_frame_rate_unusable_granted_interval`, `test_apply_frame_rate_refusal_is_non_fatal`, `test_apply_frame_rate_zero_fps_requests_default`); the ioctl is injected as a closure, `open_and_stream` passes `Capture::set_params` | ✅ Verified |
| CFP5 | `idle_fps` has one meaning in the mock and the V4L2 manager: `CameraConfig::publish_fps` returns `idle_fps` once more than half of `idle_timeout` elapsed without activity (never when `idle_timeout` is zero), and the V4L2 loop throttles publication with `publish_due` before any copy (GitHub #193) | Integration & unit tests (`capture_validation_tests::test_publish_fps_idle_throttle_parity`, `test_publish_due_throttles_only_below_full_rate`; `capture::test_idle_fps_throttles_publication`, `capture::test_idle_timeout_suspends_stream`, `capture::test_warmup_frames_are_discarded_before_validation`) | ✅ Verified |
| CFP6 | The DQBUF wait is bounded: `dqbuf_poll_timeout(fps)` is three frame intervals clamped to 150-250 ms for every rate, the loop re-checks `running` after every poll and exits right after the first poll following a stop request, so `Drop` waits at most one poll (< `C9_SHUTDOWN_BUDGET`, 500 ms, Criterion C9) (GitHub #194) | Integration & unit tests (`capture_validation_tests::test_dqbuf_poll_timeout_bounded_for_every_fps`, `test_dqbuf_poll_timeout_is_three_frame_intervals_when_in_range`; `capture::test_shutdown_observed_after_one_bounded_poll`); manual, non-CI evidence: `capture_validation_tests::test_v4l_streaming_drop_completes_within_budget_on_hardware` (`#[ignore]`, gated by `SOOS_HW_TESTS=1`, run by hand on an Integrated Camera on 2026-09-30; not reproducible from the repository) | ✅ Verified |
| CFP7 | Consecutive poll timeouts covering `MAX_STREAM_STALL` (2 s) report `CameraError::Starved`; a delivered frame resets the budget; after a timeout or an `EINTR` inside `v4l`'s `next()` one buffer is dequeued and discarded (`resync`) before `next()` is called again, so no buffer is queued twice (GitHub #194) | Unit & integration tests (`capture::test_poll_timeouts_escalate_to_starved`, `capture::test_frame_between_timeouts_resets_stall_budget`, `capture::test_dequeue_timeout_resyncs_without_requeue`, `capture::test_dequeue_interrupted_resyncs_instead_of_reopening`; `capture_validation_tests::test_stall_budget_preserves_two_second_tolerance`) | ✅ Verified |

| CHT5 | GUI preview client: an oversized length prefix is refused from the header alone, a zero-length reply is a protocol error, a truncated reply is a transport error, and after a protocol violation the worker reconnects and recovers | Hermetic tests (`ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_probe_preview_rejects_oversized_declared_length`, `ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_probe_preview_rejects_zero_length_reply`, `ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_probe_preview_reports_io_on_truncated_reply`, `ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_ipc_camera_manager_reconnects_after_oversized_reply`) | ✅ Verified |
| CHT6 | GUI failure states: daemon without camera (`Verdict::Unavailable`) shows "Daemon camera unavailable" and keeps polling until frames resume; an `EACCES` socket is `SourceUnreachable`; direct-mode `EACCES` / `EBUSY` reach distinct, retrying banner titles | Hermetic tests (`ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_ipc_camera_manager_reports_unavailable_and_keeps_polling`, `ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_probe_preview_reports_io_when_socket_permission_denied`, `ipc_camera_failure_tests::test_direct_mode_permission_and_busy_errors_reach_the_banner`) | ✅ Verified |
| CHT7 | `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md` defaults equal `CameraConfig::default()`, its test citations resolve, its C5 row states the `daemon.toml` warmup default of 0, and `Docs/ENROLLMENT_CLI.md` documents the shared resolver and the `/dev/videoN` fallback | Invariant tests (`camera_docs_contract::test_camera_doc_defaults_match_camera_config_default`, `camera_docs_contract::test_camera_doc_test_citations_resolve`, `camera_docs_contract::test_camera_doc_states_daemon_toml_warmup_default`, `camera_docs_contract::test_enrollment_doc_describes_shared_camera_resolver`, `camera_docs_contract::test_camera_doc_parsers_self_test`) | ✅ Verified |
| CHT8 | Real-hardware smoke tests (scan, resolve, stream one frame) exist but never run in CI: `#[ignore]` plus `SOOS_HW_TESTS=1` gate | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/hardware_smoke_tests.rs` compiles under clippy; both tests passed on a physical RGB-only camera (Azurewave 13d3:54bf, `uvcvideo`) *(walkthroughs 152 §6 and 159 §8)*; an RGB+IR run is a hardware-validation follow-up (walkthrough 159 §9) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-deadline-replay-uid` (Review findings PAM-06 / PAM-10 / PAM-11 — GitHub #219, #222, #223)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PDR1 | Replay protection of a `Response` is the single-use 256-bit `request_id` plus the client deadline; the timestamps are never documented as replay protection *(they were informational until GitHub #287; they are now enforced as a staleness guard, rows PRE1–PRE7)*, and a captured `Allow` replayed on a later exchange is rejected | Invariant tests (`pam_response_freshness_contract::test_response_timestamps_are_not_documented_as_replay_protection`, `pam_response_freshness_contract::test_ipc_protocol_documents_the_real_replay_protection`); integration test (`deadline_uid_tests::test_replayed_allow_response_is_rejected_by_request_id_binding`) | ✅ Verified |
| PDR2 | The CLOCK_MONOTONIC deadline sent to the daemon is fixed when the client budget starts (before connect and before UID resolution) and never exceeds `start + timeout_ms` | Integration tests (`deadline_uid_tests::test_exchange_deadline_is_fixed_when_the_budget_starts`, `deadline_uid_tests::test_request_deadline_is_captured_before_connect`, `deadline_uid_tests::test_authenticate_request_deadline_never_exceeds_the_client_budget`) | ✅ Verified |
| PDR3 | A sub-millisecond `poll()` budget is rounded up to 1 ms (never truncated to an immediate `poll(.., 0)` timeout); oversized budgets saturate | Integration test (`deadline_uid_tests::test_poll_timeout_rounds_sub_millisecond_budget_up`) | ✅ Verified |
| PDR4 | Username -> UID resolution is charged to the authentication budget: a lookup that spends `timeout_ms` returns `PAM_IGNORE` without contacting the daemon; the password-failed event is still sent within `EVENT_TIMEOUT_MS` after the lookup; the lookup always runs and `uid=` is honoured only when the resolved UID matches it (GitHub #302, owner decision 2026-10-02; row reworded from "`uid=` skips the lookup") | Integration tests (`deadline_uid_tests::test_slow_uid_resolution_exhausting_the_budget_returns_ignore`, `deadline_uid_tests::test_fast_uid_resolution_sends_resolved_uid`, `deadline_uid_tests::test_uid_argument_requires_matching_resolved_uid` *(formerly `test_uid_argument_bypasses_the_resolver`)*, `deadline_uid_tests::test_slow_uid_resolution_event_path_stays_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |
| PDR5 | Client-side enforcement of `expires_monotonic_ns` / `issued_monotonic_ns` | Delivered by GitHub #287 (owner decision 2026-10-01, ADR 2026-10-01 "PAM Client Enforces Response Expiry"): rows PRE1–PRE7 (`response_expiry_tests::test_pre_expired_allow_response_returns_ignore`, `response_freshness_tests::prop_pre_freshness_accepts_only_consistent_unexpired_windows`) *(was Pending on the fixture migration, now done setup-only)* | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-feedback-silent` (Review findings PAM-07 / PAM-08 / PAM-15 — GitHub #220, #221, #227)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PHS1 | The authentication flow reaches the PAM conversation, `pam_get_user` and the `PAM_SERVICE` item only through the `PamFeedback` trait (`SoosPam::authenticate_with_feedback`); message selection is asserted with a recorder, without any libpam handle | Integration tests (`pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_allow_emits_lookup_then_recognized`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_deny_emits_lookup_then_not_recognized`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_unavailable_and_protocol_error_emit_generic_text`); invariant test (`pam_feedback_contract::test_pam_flow_reaches_libpam_only_through_feedback_adapter`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS2 | The user lookup and the service item are exercised under test: a known user resolves to its uid, `uid=` is honoured only when it equals that uid and otherwise falls back without daemon contact, an unknown or missing user falls back without daemon contact (never to the process uid), the service item is forwarded and a `gdm.disable` flag keeps the module silent with no daemon contact and no user lookup (reworded for GitHub #300 / #302, owner decision 2026-10-02) | Integration tests (`pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_user_lookup_resolves_uid_when_no_uid_argument`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_uid_argument_mismatching_pam_user_returns_ignore` *(formerly `test_feedback_user_lookup_skipped_when_uid_argument_present`)*, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_uid_argument_matching_pam_user_sends_request`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_unknown_user_returns_ignore_without_daemon_contact` *(formerly `test_feedback_unknown_user_falls_back_to_process_uid`)*, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_service_item_forwarded_and_gdm_disable_honored`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS3 | *(Superseded by PHS4 on 2026-09-30, commit `4f15282`: the pointer-address heuristic (`addr >= 0x10000`) that this row confined to one `PamHandle` adapter helper was removed entirely; `pam_feedback_contract::test_pam_handle_address_guard_is_confined_to_one_helper` now holds vacuously and PHS4 asserts the absence)* | — | ⏹ Superseded |
| PHS4 | The pointer-address heuristic is removed entirely: `is_libpam_handle` and `0x10000` appear nowhere in `crates/pam/src`, the `PamHandle` adapter trusts the libpam handle, and the PAM hooks still return `PAM_IGNORE` on a real `pam_start` handle when the daemon is offline or on `event=password-failed` | Integration tests on a real `pam_start` handle (`pam_bindings_tests::test_pam_hooks_authenticate_offline_daemon_returns_ignore`, `pam_bindings_tests::test_pam_hooks_authenticate_password_failed_event_returns_ignore`); invariant test (`pam_handle_guard_removal_contract::test_pam_handle_address_heuristic_is_absent_from_pam_sources`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS5 | `PAM_SILENT` (alone or combined with other flag bits) suppresses every `PAM_TEXT_INFO` through the C ABI entry point and `PamHooks::sm_authenticate`, for every verdict and when the daemon is absent, without changing the verdict or the `PAM_IGNORE` fallback | Integration tests on a real `pam_start` handle (`pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_pam_silent_allow_emits_no_conversation`, `pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_pam_silent_deny_emits_no_conversation`, `pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_pam_silent_daemon_absent_emits_no_conversation`, `pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_pam_silent_combined_with_other_flags_is_silent`, `pam_silent_tests::test_pamhooks_sm_authenticate_pam_silent_emits_no_conversation`); recorder (`pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_pam_silent_suppresses_every_message_for_every_verdict`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_pam_silent_daemon_absent_emits_nothing`); invariant test (`pam_feedback_contract::test_pam_entry_points_thread_flags_and_honor_pam_silent`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS6 | An absent (`ENOENT`) or stopped (`ECONNREFUSED`) daemon never produces "Looking for face..." and at most one line; a connected daemon without `PAM_SILENT` keeps both messages | Integration tests (`pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_daemon_absent_never_announces_face_lookup`, `pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_daemon_stopped_never_announces_face_lookup`, `pam_silent_tests::test_c_entry_without_silent_keeps_lookup_and_verdict_messages`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_daemon_absent_never_announces_face_lookup`, `pam_feedback_tests::test_feedback_connection_refused_never_announces_face_lookup`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS7 | No message at all when the daemon connection fails (`IpcError::Connect`: not installed or stopped), still `PAM_IGNORE` (fail quiet, user decision 2026-10-01; the offline case of `pam_handle_tests::test_unavailable_feedback_is_a_single_generic_text` changed with user approval) | Integration tests (`pam_fail_quiet_tests::test_221_daemon_not_installed_sends_no_message`, `pam_fail_quiet_tests::test_221_daemon_stopped_sends_no_message`, `pam_handle_tests::test_unavailable_feedback_is_a_single_generic_text`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS8 | Preview channel properties: every `RequestKind` including `PreviewFrame` round-trips, `PreviewResponse` round-trips through `encode_preview` / `decode_preview`, `decode_preview` never panics on input up to `MAX_PREVIEW_MESSAGE_SIZE + 4` bytes and refuses a declared size above the limit; exact-limit boundary | Property tests in `crates/protocol/tests/preview_property_tests.rs` (bounded case counts); unit test (`preview_property_tests::test_preview_codec_boundary_at_max_preview_message_size`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHS9 | libFuzzer: the harness builds (standalone `[workspace]`, committed `Cargo.lock`), a `decode_preview` target exists, and the nightly `.github/workflows/fuzz.yml` runs every target with `-max_total_time`, SHA-pinned actions, read-only token and crash upload | Invariant tests (`pam_feedback_contract::test_fuzz_harness_is_standalone_and_covers_preview_decoder`, `pam_feedback_contract::test_nightly_fuzz_workflow_runs_every_target`); local run of all four targets (walkthrough 122 §5); no scheduled run yet | ✅ Verified (definition and local runs; first scheduled run pending) |
| PHS10 | Docker matrix case asserting the `PAM_TEXT_INFO` count with `PAM_SILENT` and with the socket absent | Needs a `pam_test_runner` flags option and a new T-number coordinated with the parallel matrix work | ⬜ Pending (follow-up) |
| PHS11 | `fault_injection::trigger` runs before any libpam access in `authenticate_flow` (`with_pam_service`, UID resolution, conversation), and the ordering is documented next to the trigger; this is what keeps the pre-existing synthetic-handle case `fault_injection_tests::test_fault_inject_via_pam_hooks_returns_pam_ignore` safe | Invariant test (`pam_handle_guard_removal_contract::test_fault_injection_trigger_runs_before_any_libpam_call`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `protocol-codec-strict-zeroize-docs` (Review findings PAM-12, PAM-13, PAM-14 — GitHub #224, #225, #226)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PCZ1 | `decode`, `decode_preview` and `decode_with_limit` reject a payload that leaves bytes inside the declared length with `CodecError::TrailingBytes { unconsumed }`; bytes after the declared frame are ignored; valid messages still round-trip | Unit/property tests (`strict_codec_tests::decode_rejects_trailing_bytes`, `strict_codec_tests::decode_trailing_bytes_error_reports_unconsumed_count`, `strict_codec_tests::decode_preview_rejects_trailing_bytes`, `strict_codec_tests::decode_ignores_bytes_after_the_declared_frame`, `strict_codec_tests::prop_response_with_trailing_bytes_never_decodes`, `strict_codec_tests::prop_request_strict_roundtrip`); invariant `protocol_codec_contract::test_codec_decode_is_strict_about_trailing_bytes` | ✅ Verified |
| PCZ2 | The PAM client and the daemon share one strict decoder (`codec::decode_payload`); no crate outside `soos-protocol` calls `postcard` in production code; an `Allow` frame with trailing bytes inside the declared length returns `PAM_IGNORE`, never `PAM_SUCCESS` | Tests (`strict_codec_tests::decode_payload_is_strict_and_exact`, `strict_decode_tests::test_allow_with_trailing_bytes_inside_frame_returns_ignore`, `strict_decode_tests::test_direct_authenticate_reports_trailing_bytes_codec_error`); invariant `protocol_codec_contract::test_no_crate_decodes_wire_payloads_with_postcard_directly` | ✅ Verified |
| PCZ3 | `encode_with_limit` sizes the payload first, rejects oversize before allocating, allocates the frame once at its final size and serializes in place (no intermediate `to_allocvec` buffer holding the request nonce); wire bytes are unchanged | Tests (`strict_codec_tests::encode_is_length_prefix_followed_by_postcard_payload`, `strict_codec_tests::encode_allocates_the_frame_exactly_once_at_its_final_size`); invariant `protocol_codec_contract::test_codec_encode_serializes_without_intermediate_buffer` | ✅ Verified |
| PCZ4 | `Docs/IPC_PROTOCOL.md`, `Docs/PAM_MODULE.md`, the protocol crate frame diagram and the PAM crate docs name only existing identifiers, document postcard varint widths, both size limits, `StatusResponse` / `PreviewResponse`, the enum wire indices and no fixed PAM deadline figure | Invariants (`protocol_codec_contract::test_ipc_protocol_doc_names_only_existing_identifiers`, `protocol_codec_contract::test_ipc_protocol_doc_enum_references_resolve`, `protocol_codec_contract::test_ipc_protocol_doc_wire_indices_match_code`, `protocol_codec_contract::test_protocol_crate_frame_diagram_uses_postcard_widths`, `protocol_codec_contract::test_pam_crate_docs_state_no_fixed_deadline_figure`) | ✅ Verified |
| PCZ5 | `tests/docker/mock_daemon.py` uses the protocol wire indices: `REASON_SCORE_BELOW_THRESHOLD = 3` (`ReasonClass::ScoreBelowThreshold`, never `NoFace` = 1) and every `REASON_*` / `VERDICT_*` constant matches its enum (GitHub #226) *(split 2026-09-30: the tagged envelope moved to PCZ6)* | Invariant test (`protocol_codec_contract::test_mock_daemon_wire_indices_match_protocol_enums`) | ✅ Verified |
| PCZ6 | Tagged `Message` envelope (wire discriminator) replaces the daemon's Request/Event tie-break (GitHub #224) | *(Deferred envelope never built: the one-byte client message tag trailer of GitHub #204 removed the tie-break without a protocol version bump; GitHub #285)* | ⏹ Superseded (DRW3, DRW4, DRW6, BBX1–BBX4) |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DCS1 | `DaemonConfig::validate()` runs at the end of `from_toml_str` (hence `load_from_path` / `load_or_default`) and the built-in default passes it; bounds are code constants (`ALLOWED_SOCKET_MODES` = 0o660/0o600, `MIN_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS` = 100, `MAX_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT_MS` = 10 000, `MAX_LOG_LEVEL_LEN` = 256) | `config_validation_tests::test_default_daemon_config_passes_validation`, `config_validation_tests::test_validation_bounds_constants_match_spec`, `config_validation_tests::test_invalid_socket_mode_fails_load_from_path_and_load_or_default` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS2 | `socket_mode` outside the allowlist (0o666 = `438`, any "other" bit, setuid/setgid/sticky, 0o640, 0o700, 0) is refused with `DaemonError::Config` naming `socket_mode`, both from TOML and programmatically; 0o660 and 0o600 are accepted | `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_world_writable_socket_mode_438`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_socket_modes_outside_allowlist`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_accepts_allowlisted_socket_modes`, `config_validation_tests::test_programmatic_world_writable_socket_mode_fails_validate` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS3 | `bind_socket` validates `socket_mode` before touching the filesystem: a refused mode leaves no socket node; 0o600 binds | `socket_hardening_tests::test_bind_socket_refuses_world_writable_mode_before_bind`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_bind_socket_accepts_0600_mode` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS4 | `connection_timeout_ms` outside 100..=10 000 ms and `max_concurrent_connections = 0` are refused | `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_connection_timeout_out_of_bounds`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_accepts_connection_timeout_bounds`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_zero_max_concurrent_connections`, `config_validation_tests::test_programmatic_zero_timeout_and_permits_fail_validate` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS5 | `[pipeline.rate_limit]` `max_attempts = 0`, `window_duration_secs = 0` and `max_tracked_uids = 0`, and `[pipeline.evidence] retention_days = 0` are refused; the minimal valid values are accepted | `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_zero_rate_limit_max_attempts`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_zero_rate_limit_window`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_zero_rate_limit_tracked_uids`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_accepts_minimal_rate_limit`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_zero_evidence_retention_days` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS6 | `log_level` must be non-blank, at most 256 bytes and accepted by `EnvFilter::try_new` (an invalid directive used to be dropped silently) | `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_rejects_unparseable_log_level`, `config_validation_tests::test_daemon_toml_accepts_valid_log_directives` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS7 | `validate()` re-checks programmatically built thresholds against the policy security floor (0.0, NaN refused) and refuses any drift between the policy thresholds and the vision mirror | `config_validation_tests::test_programmatic_unchecked_thresholds_fail_validate`, `config_validation_tests::test_programmatic_vision_threshold_drift_fails_validate` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS8 | The `EOPNOTSUPP` permission fallback pins the node with `O_PATH \| O_NOFOLLOW` relative to the validated directory descriptor, refuses symlinks, non-sockets and multi-component names, and changes the mode through `/proc/self/fd/<n>`; no path-based `set_permissions(&config.socket_path` remains | `socket_hardening_tests::test_chmod_fallback_sets_mode_on_socket_node`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_chmod_fallback_never_follows_symlink`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_chmod_fallback_refuses_non_socket_node`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_chmod_fallback_refuses_path_with_directory_component`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_socket_module_never_spawns_subprocess_or_path_chmod` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS9 | The daemon never spawns `groupadd` / `addgroup` or any subprocess from `socket.rs`; a missing group is a `Group '<name>' not found` error | `socket_hardening_tests::test_missing_group_is_an_error`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_daemon_crate_never_spawns_groupadd`, `socket_hardening_tests::test_socket_module_never_spawns_subprocess_or_path_chmod` | ✅ Verified |
| DCS10 | `soos-daemon.service` bounds capabilities to exactly `CAP_IPC_LOCK CAP_CHOWN CAP_FOWNER CAP_DAC_OVERRIDE`, grants no ambient capability and none of `CAP_SYS_ADMIN`, `CAP_SYS_MODULE`, `CAP_SYS_PTRACE`, `CAP_NET_*`, `CAP_SETUID`, `CAP_SETGID`, `CAP_SYS_RAWIO`, `CAP_MKNOD`, `CAP_BPF` | `systemd_hardening_tests::test_unit_bounds_capabilities_to_daemon_needs`, `systemd_hardening_tests::test_unit_never_grants_dangerous_capabilities` | ✅ Verified |

| DCS12 | Peer `/proc/<pid>/cgroup` and `/dev/video*` stay reachable: no `ProtectProc=` / `ProcSubset=`, no `PrivateDevices=yes`, and `DeviceAllow=char-video4linux rw` (systemd does not expand globs in `DeviceAllow=` node paths); the unit is ordered `Before=display-manager.service` | `systemd_hardening_tests::test_unit_keeps_peer_proc_and_camera_access`, `systemd_hardening_tests::test_unit_starts_before_display_manager` | ✅ Verified (unit content; camera capture under the sandbox needs hardware) |

| DRW3 | Tagged client frames are classified by their trailer alone (`MESSAGE_TAG_REQUEST` / `MESSAGE_TAG_EVENT`, both `>= 0x80`); no complete legacy v1 frame ends on a tag byte; unknown tags, tag/body mismatches and trailing bytes are rejected | `client_message_tests::prop_tagged_request_always_classified_as_request`, `client_message_tests::prop_tagged_event_always_classified_as_event`, `client_message_tests::prop_legacy_frames_never_end_on_a_tag_byte`, `client_message_tests::test_unknown_tag_is_rejected`, `client_message_tests::test_tag_mismatching_body_is_rejected`, `client_message_tests::test_tagged_body_with_trailing_bytes_is_rejected` | ✅ Verified |
| DRW4 | A legacy untagged frame is accepted only when it decodes as exactly one type; a frame decoding as both `Request` and `Event` is rejected and the daemon closes the connection without running either handler | `client_message_tests::test_legacy_frame_decoding_as_both_types_is_rejected`, `client_message_tests::prop_arbitrary_bytes_never_panic`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_ambiguous_legacy_frame_is_rejected_and_connection_closed`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_unknown_message_tag_is_rejected` *(a libFuzzer target named decode_client_message is also registered in the protocol fuzz crate; it is not run in CI)* | ✅ Verified |
| DRW5 | Backward compatibility: the daemon still serves untagged v1 frames, the in-tree strict v1 decoders accept a tagged frame's matching trailer, and `CURRENT_VERSION` stays `1` *(a daemon built before #204 rejects tagged frames as malformed; PAM and daemon upgrade together, `Docs/IPC_PROTOCOL.md` §12, GitHub #285)* | `client_message_tests::test_legacy_untagged_request_still_accepted`, `client_message_tests::test_legacy_untagged_event_still_accepted`, `client_message_tests::test_tagged_frame_stays_readable_by_v1_decoders`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_legacy_untagged_status_request_still_served` | ✅ Verified |
| DRW6 | Every first-party client tags its frames: PAM `Auth` and `PasswordFailed`, `soos-admin status` / `test-pam`, `soos-gui` preview; the daemon serves tagged requests and consumes tagged events without a reply | `soos-pam` `wire_tag_tests::test_204_pam_auth_request_frame_is_tagged`, `wire_tag_tests::test_204_pam_event_frame_is_tagged`, `soos-admin-cli` `wire_tag_tests::test_204_admin_status_request_frame_is_tagged`, `wire_tag_tests::test_204_admin_test_pam_request_frame_is_tagged`, `soos-gui` `wire_tag_tests::test_204_gui_preview_request_frame_is_tagged`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_tagged_status_request_is_served`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_tagged_auth_request_is_served_as_request`, `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_tagged_event_is_consumed_without_response` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `storage-redaction-keys-evidence-cap` (Review findings STO-13 / STO-14 / STO-18 — GitHub #229, #230, #234)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| SRK1 | `soos-admin` log redaction searches an ASCII-lowercased copy with the exact byte layout of the line (`to_ascii_lowercase`, never `to_lowercase`), so a character whose lowercase form changes UTF-8 length (`İ`, KELVIN SIGN, `ẞ`, OHM SIGN) before a secret neither disables redaction nor shifts the cut (bearer tokens had an off-by-one leak); the key boundary check looks at the previous full character, so a multi-byte non-alphanumeric character is a boundary | Integration tests (`redact_utf8_offset_tests::test_229_kv_redaction_survives_dotted_capital_i_prefix`, `test_229_kv_redaction_survives_kelvin_sign_prefix`, `test_229_bracket_redaction_survives_length_changing_prefix`, `test_229_bearer_redaction_has_no_off_by_one_after_length_changing_prefix`, `test_229_every_length_changing_char_before_every_secret_kind`, `test_229_multibyte_non_alphanumeric_char_is_a_key_boundary`, `test_229_multibyte_alphanumeric_char_is_not_a_key_boundary`, `test_229_uppercase_ascii_keys_still_match`); the pre-existing `redact_tests` suite is unchanged and green. Red: 7 of 8 failed before the fix | ✅ Verified |
| SRK2 | An existing biometric or evidence master key is opened with `O_NOFOLLOW \| O_NONBLOCK` and validated on the open descriptor: regular file, owner root or the effective UID, `mode & 0o077 == 0`, exactly 32 bytes, read bounded to 33 bytes; any violation is `KeyError` (symlink: `InvalidPath`/`Io`) and the file is not modified; a FIFO never blocks | Integration tests (`master_key_hardening_tests::test_230_world_readable_key_is_refused_with_key_error`, `test_230_group_accessible_keys_are_refused`, `test_230_owner_only_key_of_exact_length_is_accepted`, `test_230_wrong_length_keys_are_refused`, `test_230_non_regular_key_paths_are_refused_without_blocking`, `test_230_foreign_owned_key_is_refused` (root only), and the same names in `key_hardening_tests` plus `key_hardening_tests::test_230_symlinked_evidence_key_is_refused_and_target_untouched`); unit tests `crypto::key_open_tests::test_230_key_open_does_not_follow_symlinks` in both crates (mutation: removing `O_NOFOLLOW` from the evidence open fails it) | ✅ Verified |
| SRK3 | Creating a key never forces `0700` on a parent: missing parents are created with `KEY_PARENT_DIR_MODE` = `0755` (the `/var/lib/soos` contract, `fchmod` through an `O_NOFOLLOW \| O_DIRECTORY` descriptor of the directory just created), existing parents are never chmod-ed | Integration tests (`master_key_hardening_tests::test_230_missing_parent_is_created_0755_not_0700`, `test_230_missing_nested_parents_are_all_created_0755`, `test_230_existing_parent_mode_is_never_modified`, same names in `key_hardening_tests`) | ✅ Verified |
| SRK4 | The per-UID evidence daily cap is persisted in the date partition (`YYYY-MM-DD/.daily_count.<uid>`, `DAILY_COUNT_FILE_PREFIX`, mode `0600`, atomic write) and holds across a store restart; the counter is never listed as a snapshot | Integration tests (`daily_cap_persistence_tests::test_234_daily_cap_survives_store_restart`, `test_234_counter_files_are_not_listed_as_snapshots_and_are_private`); `daily_cap_tests` unchanged and green | ✅ Verified |
| SRK5 | No per-(UID, day) state grows in memory: retention pruning a partition also forgets its count; a slot is consumed only after the snapshot rename succeeds (a failed write leaves the counter unchanged; a counter that cannot be persisted rolls the snapshot back); concurrent writers never exceed the cap | Integration tests (`daily_cap_persistence_tests::test_234_retention_forgets_counts_of_pruned_dates`, `test_234_failed_write_does_not_consume_a_slot` (non-root only), `test_234_concurrent_writers_never_exceed_the_cap`) | ✅ Verified |
| SRK6 | A symlinked, oversized or unparseable counter file fails closed (`Io` / `CorruptPayload`), writes nothing and never touches a symlink target | Integration tests (`daily_cap_persistence_tests::test_234_symlinked_counter_file_is_refused_and_target_untouched`, `test_234_corrupt_counter_file_fails_closed`) | ✅ Verified |

| OPM6 | The RPM spec `License:`, the PKGBUILD `license=()` and the Arch `.PKGINFO` license equal the `Cargo.toml` license; no build script hardcodes `Apache-2.0` / `MIT` | Invariant test | ✅ Verified (`onboarding_packaging_contract::test_package_license_matches_cargo_workspace`) |
| OPM7 | The spec `Version:` and PKGBUILD `pkgver=` equal the `Cargo.toml` version; `build_deb.sh`, `build_rpm.sh` and `build_arch.sh` source `pkg_meta.sh`, hardcode no `VERSION`, and their `--dry-run` prints the `Cargo.toml` version | Invariant test (runs each script with `--dry-run`) | ✅ Verified (`onboarding_packaging_contract::test_package_versions_derive_from_cargo_workspace`) |
| OPM8 | The RPM (`%install` + `%files`) and the PKGBUILD `package()` ship `soos-gui`; its `dlopen()`ed libraries are RPM `Recommends:` and PKGBUILD `optdepends` (lists from `check_build_deps.sh --print-packages gui`) | Invariant test | ✅ Verified (`onboarding_packaging_contract::test_rpm_and_arch_recipes_ship_soos_gui`) |
| OPM9 | Every `cargo build` / `cargo test` line of `packaging/debian/rules`, `soos.spec`, `PKGBUILD` and `scripts/build_*.sh` uses `--locked` | Invariant test | ✅ Verified (`onboarding_packaging_contract::test_packaging_cargo_invocations_are_locked`) |
| OPM10 | The PKGBUILD `build()` and `package()` `cd` into an explicit source tree (`SOOS_SRC_DIR`, default the checkout) because `source=()` leaves `$srcdir` empty | Invariant test; building from a published release tarball is a follow-up (no release tag exists) | ✅ Verified (`onboarding_packaging_contract::test_pkgbuild_builds_from_an_explicit_source_tree`) |

## Component: `installer-distro-templates-and-first-start` (Review findings ONB-12, ONB-13, ONB-15 — GitHub #208, #209, #211)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| IDT1 | `install.sh --distro debian\|fedora\|arch\|none` stages exactly that family's PAM template (Debian `pam-auth-update` profiles, authselect profile, or `/usr/share/soos/pam/system-auth.snippet` mode `0644`) and never creates a file under `etc/pam.d`, `etc/authselect` or `usr/share/pam-configs` for another family | Invariant test (`installer_templates_contract::test_install_stages_only_the_selected_distro_template`) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT2 | Without `--distro`, the family comes from the staged `os-release` `ID` then `ID_LIKE` (ubuntu, mint, fedora, rocky, arch, manjaro); openSUSE or a stage without `os-release` installs no template and prints a `--distro` hint; an explicit `--distro` wins; an unknown value fails the preflight with nothing written | Invariant tests (`installer_templates_contract::test_install_detects_distro_from_os_release_id_and_id_like`, `installer_templates_contract::test_install_rejects_unknown_distro_value`) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT3 | Packaging selects its own family (`--distro debian` in `build_deb.sh` and `debian/rules`, `--distro arch` in `build_arch.sh`), no packaging file references `/etc/pam.d/soos.snippet`, the PKGBUILD installs the snippet under `/usr/share/soos/pam/`, and `test_packages.sh` asserts the per-distribution package content (`verify_distro_templates`); `uninstall.sh` removes the relocated and the legacy snippet | Invariant tests (`installer_templates_contract::test_packaging_selects_one_distro_template_and_never_ships_snippet_in_pam_d`, `installer_templates_contract::test_uninstall_removes_relocated_and_legacy_arch_snippet`); the Docker package branches themselves run in CI jobs `package-deploy` / `distro-deploy` | ✅ Verified |
| IDT4 | `download_models.sh` reports and downloads only the manifest `source_url` (no legacy table, no rewriting, even for the former table ids) | Invariant test (`installer_templates_contract::test_download_models_dry_run_reports_only_manifest_urls`) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT5 | Every model file is bounded by `MAX_MODEL_BYTES` before hashing (oversized file discarded, nothing left in the target), `SOOS_MODEL_MAX_BYTES` only tightens the cap and a malformed value fails before a write; curl is HTTPS-only, TLS >= 1.2, time- and size-bounded; the temporary file comes from `mktemp` under `umask 077` | Invariant tests (`installer_templates_contract::test_download_models_enforces_size_cap_before_hashing`, `installer_templates_contract::test_download_models_hardens_curl_and_temporary_files`) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT6 | `soos-daemon.service` `[Unit]` carries `ConditionPathExists=/var/lib/soos/models/manifest.toml` (the path `download_models.sh` deploys) and bounds restarts to 5 in 320 s *(60 s before GitHub #287, see DFU6)* | Invariant test (`installer_templates_contract::test_daemon_unit_requires_deployed_models_and_bounds_restarts`); the systemd `condition failed` state itself needs a systemd container (walkthrough 116 §5) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT7 | A live install fails its preflight when the `soos` group is missing and cannot be created; `install.sh --start` starts the unit after model deployment and waits through `scripts/wait_daemon_ready.sh` | Invariant test (`installer_templates_contract::test_install_requires_group_and_offers_bounded_readiness`) | ✅ Verified |
| IDT8 | `wait_daemon_ready.sh` succeeds only when the manifest exists, the socket is a Unix socket and `soos-admin --format json --socket-path <sock> status` succeeds; a missing manifest fails without waiting, a missing or non-socket path fails after the bounded `--timeout`, a failing status fails, and a timeout outside 1..300 is a usage error (exit 2) | Invariant tests (`installer_templates_contract::test_wait_daemon_ready_succeeds_when_socket_and_status_answer`, `installer_templates_contract::test_wait_daemon_ready_fails_closed_and_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |

| FIL3 | `soos-test-fixtures` is never a normal dependency of any crate, and no `#[path]` include of `tests/fixtures/mod.rs` may appear (the legacy list `LEGACY_PATH_INCLUDES` is empty since the last three includes were migrated) | Invariant tests (`fixtures_contract::test_fil_fixtures_crate_is_never_a_runtime_dependency`, `fixtures_contract::test_fil_fixture_path_includes_are_limited_to_legacy_files`) | ✅ Verified |

| FIL5 | PAM production code has no `.unwrap()`, `.expect(`, `panic!`, `unreachable!`, `todo!`, `unimplemented!`, `panic_any` nor a silenced panic/indexing Clippy lint (feature-gated `fault_injection.rs` exempt) | Invariant test (`lexing_contract::test_fil_pam_production_code_has_no_panicking_constructs`) | ✅ Verified |
| FIL6 | No async runtime (`tokio`, `async-std`, `smol`, `async-io`, `async-executor`) is reachable from `soos-pam` in the resolved normal dependency graph (all targets, all features), with a non-vacuity check on `soos-daemon` | Invariant test (`lexing_contract::test_fil_pam_dependency_graph_has_no_async_runtime`) | ✅ Verified |
| FIL7 | `scripts/candid_review.sh` audits PAM production lines containing the substring `tests`, ignores code added inside `#[cfg(test)]` modules, and still audits production code after `#[cfg(test)] mod tests;` | End-to-end tests in scratch repositories (`candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_flags_pam_unwrap_on_line_containing_tests_substring`, `candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_allows_unwrap_inside_cfg_test_module`, `candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_cfg_test_mod_declaration_does_not_hide_production`) | ✅ Verified |
| FIL8 | The language audit accepts the English words `pour` and `attention` and still rejects French comments | End-to-end tests (`candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_accepts_english_attention_and_pour`, `candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_still_flags_french_comments`) | ✅ Verified |
| FIL9 | Every candid audit diffs against `git merge-base origin/main HEAD`: commits landing on `main` after the branch point are not audited as branch changes | End-to-end test (`candid_review_contract::test_fil_candid_ignores_upstream_commits_after_branch_point`) | ✅ Verified |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DDS1 | Every `deny.toml` `[bans] skip` entry is a version locked in `Cargo.lock` for a crate locked in more than one version | Invariant tests (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_deny_skip_entries_are_locked_versions`, `test_parse_lockfile_resolves_direct_dependents`) | ✅ Verified |
| DDS2 | Every skip reason names at least one direct dependent of the skipped version (the `Cargo.lock` graph printed by `cargo tree -i`); a version used directly by a workspace crate is never called transitive | Invariant tests (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_deny_skip_reasons_name_a_real_direct_dependent`, `test_deny_skip_reasons_never_call_a_direct_workspace_dependency_transitive`). Red: 13 stale reasons, `getrandom@0.3.4` described as transitive while six workspace crates used it directly | ✅ Verified |
| DDS3 | Every version of a crate locked three times (`getrandom`, `redox_syscall`, `windows-sys`) is named in `deny.toml`, including the unskipped `getrandom@0.4.3` | Invariant test (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_deny_documents_every_version_of_crates_locked_three_times`) | ✅ Verified |
| DDS4 | Workspace crates use `getrandom` 0.4; a pre-release workspace dependency is `=`-pinned (`ort = "=2.0.0-rc.13"`); `cargo deny --locked check bans licenses sources` stays green | Invariant test (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_prerelease_workspace_dependencies_are_exact_pinned`); `cargo deny` run in walkthrough 130 | ✅ Verified |
| DDS5 | `tests/docker/test_suite.sh` always rebuilds `libpam_soos.so` (unguarded top-level `cargo build --locked --release -p soos-pam` before the deploy), so a stale host artifact is never deployed | Invariant test (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_docker_suite_always_rebuilds_the_pam_module`) — regression guard, already green on `origin/main` | ✅ Verified |
| DDS6 | The Docker suite synchronizes the toolchain with `rust-toolchain.toml` (`rustup toolchain install`) before its first cargo call, logs `rustc --version`, and CI makes a failed sync fatal (`-e SOOS_REQUIRE_TOOLCHAIN_SYNC=1`) | Invariant tests (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_docker_suite_syncs_toolchain_with_rust_toolchain_toml`, `test_ci_pam_integration_requires_toolchain_sync`); Docker run: green `PAM Integration (Docker)` job of the `main` run of `abd6016` (2026-09-30, log "Toolchain in use: rustc 1.98.1" with the fatal sync flag set), https://github.com/Mysticaly622/soos/actions/runs/36777041130/job/110097611926 (recorded by QFX5, re-checked 2026-10-01, walkthrough 148) | ✅ Verified |
| DDS7 | `save.sh` never stages every untracked file: `git add -u` plus new files under the source directories only, other untracked files listed | Invariant test (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_save_sh_never_stages_every_untracked_file`) | ✅ Verified |
| DDS8 | Without `-m`, `save.sh` infers one subject line from the branch prefix (type) and the single staged crate (scope), refuses other prefixes, empty descriptions and subjects above 72 characters, and never generates `feat(...)` placeholders, body bullets or file counters | Behavioral tests of `scripts/commit_message.sh` (`dependency_tooling_contract::test_commit_helper_takes_type_from_branch_prefix_and_scope_from_one_crate`, `test_commit_helper_refuses_to_guess_without_a_known_branch_prefix`, `test_commit_helper_sanitizes_and_bounds_the_subject`, `test_save_sh_never_generates_generic_subjects`) | ✅ Verified |

| PMC7 | `crop_pad_context` resizes the window with the `cv2.resize` `INTER_LINEAR` half-pixel convention and matches the committed golden fixtures (NumPy transcription of the upstream crop, `generate_golden.py`: interior 2.7×, scale-capped edge 4.0×, upscaled 2.7×) within ±1 LSB; degenerate boxes give an all-black crop; buffer and dimension errors are typed | Unit tests (`pad_crop_geometry_tests::test_crop_pad_context_uses_half_pixel_centre_sampling`, `test_crop_pad_context_matches_upstream_golden_fixture`, `test_crop_pad_context_golden_windows_match_generator_report`, `test_crop_pad_context_degenerate_box_yields_black_crop`, `test_crop_pad_context_validates_buffers_and_dimensions`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC8 | Generic `crop_and_resize` and `OrtPadDetector::prepare_input` (non-80×80 crops) sample pixel centres bilinearly instead of top-left / nearest-neighbour; an 80×80 PAD crop is an exact identity, so the real-model golden logits are unchanged | Unit tests (`crop_center_sampling_tests::test_crop_and_resize_downscale_samples_pixel_centres`, `test_crop_and_resize_upscale_replicates_border_half_pixel`, `pad_resample_tests::test_pad_prepare_input_downscale_uses_half_pixel_centres`, `test_pad_prepare_input_upscale_clamps_to_border`, `test_pad_prepare_input_80x80_is_identity`); real model (`pad_real_model_tests::test_real_pad_model_golden_logits_on_synthetic_inputs`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC9 | Multi-scale fusion: `fuse_pad_results` returns one member unchanged and otherwise the mean live probability thresholded fail-closed (NaN/inf member or NaN threshold rejects); `with_additional_pad_model` rejects non-finite / non-positive scales and more than `MAX_PAD_ENSEMBLE_MODELS`; the pipeline gives every member its own upstream-geometry crop at its scale, rejects a fused score below the (IR-aware) threshold, accepts a fused score above it, fails closed on a member inference error, and `analyze_frame` reports the fused result | Unit tests (`pad_ensemble_tests::test_fuse_pad_results_is_mean_of_live_probabilities`, `test_fuse_pad_results_single_member_is_passthrough_and_empty_is_none`, `test_fuse_pad_results_non_finite_fails_closed`, `test_additional_pad_model_rejects_invalid_scale_and_bounds_members`, `test_pipeline_primary_pad_crop_uses_upstream_geometry`, `test_pipeline_runs_each_member_on_its_own_scale_crop`, `test_pipeline_fused_score_below_threshold_is_pad_failed`, `test_pipeline_fused_score_above_threshold_passes_even_if_one_member_is_lower`, `test_pipeline_member_inference_error_fails_closed`, `test_pipeline_monochrome_ensemble_uses_ir_threshold`, `test_analyze_frame_reports_fused_result`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC10 | Real-model validation (model-gated, `/var/lib/soos/models`): the attested MiniFASNetV2 input `[N, 3, 80, 80]` equals the pipeline PAD crop at scales 2.7 and 4.0, and the fused pipeline score on real outputs equals the mean of the real per-crop scores (synthetic non-face rejected) | Real-model tests (`pad_crop_real_model_tests::test_real_pad_model_input_matches_pipeline_pad_crop`, `test_real_pad_multiscale_fusion_is_mean_of_real_scores`) | ✅ Verified |
| PMC11 | Production wiring of the 4.0× MiniFASNetV1SE member (manifest entry with SHA-256 and I/O shapes, daemon / enroll / GUI construction, fused threshold re-measured on the PAD-06 corpus) | Not implemented: the model is not in the attested model set (GitHub #212 follow-up) | ⬜ Pending (model not attested) |

## Component: `pad-startup-validation` (Review finding PAD-09 — GitHub #214)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PSV1 | `OrtPadDetector::interpret_probabilities` returns `InferenceError::PadFailed` when `live_class_index` does not address an element of the probability vector (3-class, 2-class and single-output cases), instead of reading `p_live = 0.0` (permanent spoof); in-range indices still classify | Unit tests (`pad_output_contract_tests::test_interpret_probabilities_rejects_out_of_range_index_three_class`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_interpret_probabilities_rejects_out_of_range_index_two_class`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_interpret_probabilities_rejects_out_of_range_index_single_output`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_interpret_probabilities_in_range_index_still_classifies`) | ✅ Verified |
| PSV2 | Every ORT PAD inference must yield exactly `MINIFASNET_CLASS_COUNT` (3) logits with an in-range live class index (`validate_pad_output_contract`); a single-logit head (softmax = 1.0, formerly scored live on every frame) and a flattened 19200-value head are `PadFailed` | Real ORT sessions over hand-encoded graphs (`tests/fixtures/pad_onnx.rs`; `pad_output_contract_tests::test_validate_pad_output_contract`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_evaluate_liveness_rejects_single_logit_head`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_evaluate_liveness_rejects_wrong_output_length`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_evaluate_liveness_three_class_head_classifies`) | ✅ Verified |
| PSV3 | The expected PAD class count comes from the manifest `output_shapes` (`pad_class_count_from_manifest`: `[[1, 3]]` gives 3, an omitted entry falls back to 3, any other class count, a rank-0 shape or several outputs fail closed) and `OrtPadDetector::self_test` checks it on a fixed synthetic 80x80 fixture, returning a `PadSelfTestReport` | Unit and ORT tests (`pad_output_contract_tests::test_pad_class_count_from_manifest`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_passes_for_three_class_head`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_fails_closed_on_single_logit_head`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_fails_closed_on_wrong_output_length`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_fails_closed_on_out_of_range_index`, `pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_fails_closed_on_manifest_class_count_mismatch`); the attested MiniFASNetV2 passes (`pad_output_contract_tests::test_self_test_passes_on_real_minifasnet_model`, skipped without an installed model) | ✅ Verified |
| PSV4 | `soos-daemon` runs `pipeline::validate_pad_detector` (manifest entry `PAD_MODEL_ID` required, self-test, info log of class count, live class index and threshold) inside `initialize_pipeline` before the `VisionPipeline` is assembled and propagates its error with `?`, so a wrong PAD head or index stops the daemon at startup | Daemon tests (`pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_accepts_shipped_contract`, `pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_fails_closed_on_single_logit_head`, `pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_fails_closed_on_wrong_output_length`, `pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_fails_closed_on_out_of_range_index`, `pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_fails_closed_on_manifest_class_count_mismatch`, `pad_startup_validation_tests::test_validate_pad_detector_fails_closed_without_pad_manifest_entry`) and invariant (`pad_startup_contract::test_initialize_pipeline_runs_pad_self_test_fail_closed`) | ✅ Verified |
