| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PQG1 | `process_frame` rejects a face whose smaller box side is below `min_face_width_px` (default `DEFAULT_MIN_FACE_WIDTH_PX` = 48 px) with `VisionError::FaceTooSmall` before PAD is consulted; a 120 px face passes | Unit tests (`face_quality_gate_tests::test_process_frame_rejects_30px_face_as_too_small_before_pad`, `test_process_frame_accepts_120px_face`, `test_face_size_gate_uses_smaller_box_side`, `test_quality_gate_default_constants`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG2 | The size gate is fail-closed: a non-finite box or a NaN threshold rejects the face | Unit tests (`face_quality_gate_tests::test_face_size_gate_rejects_non_finite_box`, `test_face_size_gate_fails_closed_on_nan_threshold`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG3 | `laplacian_variance` is exact on flat crops (0), high on sharp edges and `None` on malformed buffers; with `min_pad_crop_sharpness` > 0 a blurred crop is rejected with `VisionError::FaceBlurred` before PAD, a sharp one passes; the default floor is 0.0 (disabled pending calibration) | Unit tests (`face_quality_gate_tests::test_laplacian_variance_flat_is_zero_and_edges_are_high`, `test_laplacian_variance_rejects_invalid_buffers`, `test_blurred_pad_crop_rejected_when_sharpness_gate_enabled`, `test_sharp_pad_crop_passes_sharpness_gate`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG4 | `analyze_frame` reports `quality_rejection` (`TooSmall` / `Blurred`) and then produces no PAD result and no embedding | Unit tests (`face_quality_gate_tests::test_analyze_frame_reports_quality_rejection_and_skips_pad_and_embedding`, `test_analyze_frame_reports_blurred_crop`, `test_analyze_frame_accepts_good_face_without_quality_rejection`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG5 | The daemon treats a quality-gated face as an unusable capture: `Deny` / `NoFace`, never `Allow` and never `InternalError`, and PAD is not called | Integration tests (`crates/daemon/tests/face_quality_gate_tests.rs`: `test_218_too_small_face_returns_deny_no_face`, `test_218_well_sized_face_still_allows`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG6 | Guided enrollment with `LivenessPolicy::strict()` records a sample only after 3 consecutive live frames; intermittent liveness never yields a sample; a spoof frame discards the current step and resets the streak | Unit tests (`guided_enrollment_liveness_tests::test_strict_session_requires_k_consecutive_live_frames_before_first_sample`, `test_intermittent_liveness_never_yields_a_sample`, `test_spoof_frame_discards_samples_of_current_step`, `test_liveness_policy_constants_and_strict_defaults`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG7 | The `max_spoof_events`-th spoof frame aborts the session: every sample is discarded, `SessionAborted` is returned from then on and the composite template is refused (also for `GuidedEnrollmentSession::new`); policy values are clamped to `1..=32` | Unit tests (`guided_enrollment_liveness_tests::test_repeated_spoof_frames_abort_the_session`, `test_new_session_keeps_single_frame_gate_but_aborts_on_repeated_spoofs`, `test_liveness_policy_values_are_clamped`, `test_interrupted_streak_requires_fresh_consecutive_live_frames`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG8 | The GUI uses the strict policy; frames without a PAD verdict or rejected by the quality gate break the streak without counting as spoof events and are never sampled; spoof or below-threshold frames count as spoof events | Unit tests (`guided_liveness_feed_tests::test_gui_session_requires_consecutive_live_frames`, `test_missing_pad_verdict_breaks_streak_without_spoof_event`, `test_quality_rejected_face_is_never_sampled`, `test_spoof_and_below_threshold_frames_count_as_spoof_events`, `test_frame_without_face_breaks_streak_and_reports_nothing`) | ✅ Verified |
| PQG9 | Sharpness floor calibrated on real camera captures (genuine vs. far or blurred presentations) and enabled by default | Hardware capture campaign | ⬜ Pending (needs real camera data; default stays 0.0) |

## Component: `vision-attestation-and-scrfd-contract` (Review findings VIS-04 — GitHub #246, VIS-05 — GitHub #247, VIS-07 — GitHub #249)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| VTS1 | `ModelManifest::read_verified_model` reads a model file once (type and size checked on the opened handle, at most `MAX_MODEL_FILE_BYTES` = 512 MiB) and returns exactly the bytes it hashed; tampered bytes fail with `ChecksumMismatch`, a missing file with `ModelNotFound`, an oversized file with `ModelIo` | `registry_attestation_tests::test_read_verified_model_returns_exactly_the_hashed_bytes`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_read_verified_model_rejects_tampered_bytes`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_read_verified_model_missing_file_is_model_not_found`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_read_verified_model_enforces_size_bound`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_max_model_file_bytes_bounds_the_attested_models` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS2 | `ModelRegistry::get_or_load_session` builds the ORT session with `commit_from_memory` from the verified bytes (never re-opening the path); tampered bytes never load and are not cached; a loaded session is independent of later file changes | `registry_attestation_tests::test_registry_rejects_tampered_bytes`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_registry_session_is_independent_of_the_file_after_load`, `vision_attestation_contract::test_inference_ort_never_loads_a_session_by_path` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS3 | `verify_integrity` hashes each model once and hands the verified bytes to the next `get_or_load_session` (no second hash, no re-open); retained bytes are single-use and dropped on any integrity failure | `registry_attestation_tests::test_registry_loads_bytes_verified_by_integrity_check_without_rehash`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_registry_verified_bytes_are_single_use`, `registry_attestation_tests::test_registry_verify_integrity_rejects_tampered_bytes` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS4 | SCRFD score activation is explicit (`ScoreActivation`, default `Probability`); the production `detect` path decodes with `decode_stride_checked`, which fails closed with `TensorError` on any non-finite or out-of-range probability (or non-finite logit); no per-element sigmoid fallback remains | `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_default_score_activation_is_probability`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_rejects_out_of_range_scores`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_probability_scores_pass_through_unchanged`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_logit_activation_is_monotonic_and_rejects_non_finite`, `vision_attestation_contract::test_scrfd_detect_uses_explicit_fail_closed_activation`; existing `scrfd_tests::test_scrfd_supports_preactivated_probabilities` unchanged | ✅ Verified |
| VTS5 | The historical `decode_stride` entry point infers one activation per tensor, so its mapping is monotonic across the `[0, 1]` boundary | `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_decode_stride_is_monotonic_across_unit_boundary`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_score_activation_infer_is_tensor_wide` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS6 | `OrtScrfdDetector::new` validates the session output metadata (`validate_output_dims`): 9 rank-3 outputs, batch 1 or symbolic, 3 heads each of 1/4/10 channels, concrete anchor dims in {12800, 3200, 800} without duplicates; the attested graph (`[-1, -1, k]`) passes and its scores are probabilities | `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_validate_output_dims_accepts_symbolic_and_rejects_wrong_layouts`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_new_rejects_nine_outputs_with_wrong_shapes`, `scrfd_contract_tests::test_scrfd_new_accepts_scrfd_output_shapes`, `scrfd_real_model_tests::test_real_scrfd_session_passes_startup_shape_validation`, `scrfd_real_model_tests::test_real_scrfd_scores_are_probabilities` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS7 | The UltraFace inference path (`OrtFaceDetector` session, priors, decoding) and the `OrtScrfdDetector::unproject` / `letterbox_pad` wrappers are removed, `soos-inference-ort` no longer depends on `ndarray`, and `scripts/download_models.sh` resolves no legacy model URL | `vision_attestation_contract::test_legacy_four_model_pipeline_artefacts_are_removed` | ✅ Verified |
| VTS8 | Complete removal of `OrtFaceDetector::prepare_input`, `LandmarkDetector` and `MockLandmarkDetector`; the pre-existing tests that only exercised them were removed with user approval (2026-09-30), the SCRFD zeroization block keeps the detector input coverage | Invariant test (`vision_attestation_contract::test_vts8_legacy_detector_types_removed`); unit test (`zeroize_tests::test_inference_input_buffers_zeroized`) | ✅ Verified |

| VAY4 | `OrtScrfdDetector` reuses one zeroized letterbox scratch tensor per detector: `letterbox_pad_into` is bit-identical to `letterbox_pad` on a dirty scratch, zero-fills on an invalid frame, rejects a wrong scratch length; the nine outputs are decoded from borrowed ORT slices; detections are identical across scratch reuse on the real model and a p50/p95 latency report is printed (#252) | Unit tests (`scrfd_robustness_tests::test_letterbox_pad_into_matches_letterbox_pad_with_dirty_scratch`, `test_letterbox_pad_into_invalid_frame_yields_zero_tensor`, `test_letterbox_pad_into_rejects_wrong_scratch_length`); real-model tests, skipped without models (`scrfd_hot_path_real_model_tests::test_real_scrfd_scratch_reuse_is_deterministic_across_frames`, `test_real_scrfd_detect_latency_report`) | ✅ Verified |
| VAY5 | `convert_to_rgb_cow` borrows an RGB24 frame without copying it (`Cow::Borrowed`, same pointer) after the same validation as `convert_to_rgb`, and returns the `convert_to_rgb` output for every other format; `process_frame` uses it (#252) | Unit tests (`frame_robustness_tests::test_convert_to_rgb_cow_borrows_rgb24_without_copy`, `test_convert_to_rgb_cow_rejects_wrong_rgb24_length`, `test_convert_to_rgb_cow_converts_other_formats_like_convert_to_rgb`) | ✅ Verified |
| VAY6 | Every production ORT `Session::run` is wrapped in `ZeroizingOutputs`, which overwrites every ORT-owned `f32` output tensor in place (`try_extract_tensor_mut`) on drop or `wipe()`; outputs are never moved out (`outputs.into_iter()`), and the only copies of extracted output data (`pad.rs` logits, `embedding.rs` raw embedding via `.to_vec()`) go straight into wipe-on-drop containers (`Zeroizing::new(...)`, `BiometricEmbedding::new(...)`), enforced for every `.to_vec()` of the run sites by RFX5 (GitHub #285); ORT-internal activations are a documented limitation (#255) | Unit tests on an in-memory ONNX identity graph (`output_zeroize_tests::test_zeroizing_outputs_wipes_ort_owned_output_tensor`, `test_zeroizing_outputs_exposes_outputs_before_wipe`, `test_zeroizing_outputs_wipe_is_idempotent`); invariant tests (`ort_output_zeroize_contract::test_every_ort_session_run_is_wrapped_in_zeroizing_outputs`, `test_ort_outputs_are_never_moved_out_or_copied`, `test_zeroizing_outputs_guard_wipes_in_place_on_drop`) | ✅ Verified |
| VAY7 | ORT intra-op threads default to `min(4, available_parallelism)` with spin-waiting disabled; `RegistryConfig::with_intra_threads` clamps to `1..=MAX_INTRA_THREADS` (16); `soos-daemon` `[pipeline] inference_intra_threads` is parsed, rejects `0` and values above the bound fail-closed, and is passed to the registry by `registry_config_for` (#252) | Unit tests (`registry_threads_tests::test_default_intra_threads_is_bounded_by_parallelism`, `test_registry_config_defaults_use_default_intra_threads_without_spinning`, `test_registry_config_with_intra_threads_is_clamped`; `inference_threads_config_tests::test_pipeline_config_defaults_to_bounded_intra_threads`, `test_pipeline_config_parses_inference_intra_threads`, `test_pipeline_config_rejects_out_of_range_intra_threads`, `test_registry_config_for_uses_pipeline_threads_and_models_dir`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `strict-codec-client-tag-integration` (GitHub #224 x #204, `fix/p2-batch-b` integration)

The strict codec (#224) and the client message tag trailer (#204) interact: every tagged client frame left one unconsumed byte. The strict decoder now tolerates exactly the matching tag and nothing else (ADR 2026-09-30 "Strict Codec Accepts Only the Matching Client Message Tag Trailer"). PCZ6 (tagged discriminator) is superseded by the #204 trailer, which closes the #224 discriminator item without a protocol v2 envelope (row RFX6; the single-interpretation regression rows PCX1–PCX4 are in the `wire-single-interpretation-regression` section).

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| BBX1 | `codec::decode::<Request>` / `decode::<Event>` decode `encode_request` / `encode_event` frames; `decode_payload` accepts exactly one trailing `MESSAGE_TAG_REQUEST` after a `Request` and exactly one `MESSAGE_TAG_EVENT` after an `Event` | Contract tests (`tag_trailer_codec_tests::test_strict_decode_accepts_tagged_request_frame`, `test_strict_decode_accepts_tagged_event_frame`, `test_decode_payload_accepts_exactly_the_matching_tag`); pre-existing `client_message_tests::test_tagged_frame_stays_readable_by_v1_decoders` | ✅ Verified |
| BBX2 | Any other single trailing byte is rejected with `TrailingBytes { unconsumed: 1 }`: all 255 non-matching bytes after a `Request` and after an `Event`, the other type's tag, and both tags after a `Response` | Contract tests (`tag_trailer_codec_tests::test_every_non_tag_single_trailing_byte_is_rejected`, `test_request_followed_by_the_event_tag_is_rejected`, `test_event_followed_by_the_request_tag_is_rejected`, `test_response_never_accepts_a_client_message_tag`); `strict_codec_tests::prop_response_with_trailing_bytes_never_decodes` | ✅ Verified |
| BBX3 | Two or more trailing bytes are always rejected with their exact count, including a doubled tag and a tag followed by an extra byte inside the declared frame | Contract tests (`tag_trailer_codec_tests::test_two_trailing_bytes_are_rejected_even_when_one_is_a_tag`, `test_framed_request_with_tag_and_extra_byte_is_rejected`, `prop_multi_byte_remainder_after_request_is_rejected`) | ✅ Verified |
| BBX4 | `decode_client_message` decodes the tag-stripped body through the zero-remainder `codec::decode_payload_exact` (a doubled tag is `Malformed`), the daemon dispatcher classifies with `decode_client_message`, and the nightly fuzz workflow runs the `decode_client_message` target | Contract test (`tag_trailer_codec_tests::test_client_message_with_a_doubled_tag_is_rejected`); invariant tests (`protocol_codec_contract::test_no_crate_decodes_wire_payloads_with_postcard_directly`, `pam_feedback_contract::test_nightly_fuzz_workflow_runs_every_target`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-logging-shutdown` (GitHub #257, #258, #259, `fix/p3-daemon-logging-shutdown`)

Daemon log anonymization of the request nonce, clock-stamped responses on every verdict path, tracked connection handlers with a bounded shutdown drain, and payload-free panic reporting (ADR 2026-09-30 "Daemon Logging Anonymization, Response Stamping and Bounded Shutdown Drain").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| DLX1 | No daemon log macro (multi-line aware) references the raw `request_id` or logs a whole request/response; the only permitted form is `request_id = %short_request_id(..)`, and the scanner itself flags raw, custom-formatted and whole-request forms (#257) | Contract tests (`request_id_logging_tests::test_daemon_logs_never_carry_the_raw_request_nonce`, `test_scanner_flags_raw_nonce_and_accepts_short_id`) | ✅ Verified |
| DLX2 | `short_request_id` renders 8 lowercase hex digits (`Display` and `Debug` alike), is deterministic, discriminates nonces differing in one bit, is a domain-separated SHA-256 digest and never a verbatim slice of the nonce hex (#257) | Unit tests (`request_id_logging_tests::test_short_request_id_is_short_lowercase_hex`, `test_short_request_id_is_deterministic_and_discriminating`, `test_short_request_id_is_not_a_prefix_of_the_nonce`) | ✅ Verified |
| DLX3 | Every rendered `Response` is stamped from the dispatcher clock (`issued > 0`, `expires = issued + RESPONSE_VALIDITY_NS` = 2 s) on the wire-validation, UID-mismatch, preview-refusal and no-pipeline paths; on clock failure `issued = expires = 0` and the verdict is never `Allow` (#258) | Contract tests (`response_timestamp_tests::test_wire_validation_rejection_is_stamped`, `test_uid_mismatch_rejection_is_stamped`, `test_preview_authorization_refusal_is_stamped`, `test_no_pipeline_fallback_is_stamped`, `test_clock_failure_yields_expired_non_allow_response`, `test_response_validity_window_is_two_seconds`) | ✅ Verified |
| DLX4 | Connection handlers are tracked in `ConnectionTasks`: finished handlers are reaped during the accept loop; on shutdown the accept loop returns, no new connection is accepted, and the drain finishes within its budget, aborting stragglers (client sees EOF, no verdict); `soos-daemon` unlinks the socket before a drain bounded by `connection_timeout` (#259) | Contract tests (`graceful_shutdown_tests::test_drain_waits_for_in_flight_handler_within_budget`, `test_drain_aborts_handlers_that_exceed_the_budget`, `test_accept_loop_reaps_finished_handlers`, `test_shutdown_stops_accepting_and_drains_in_flight_connection`, `test_drain_logs_its_outcome`, `test_production_main_wires_tracked_handlers_and_bounded_drain`) | ✅ Verified |
| DLX5 | A handler panic is logged through `tracing` at error level without its payload; the process panic hook logs file, line and thread through `tracing` and never the panic message (#259) | Contract tests (`graceful_shutdown_tests::test_handler_panic_is_logged_via_tracing_without_payload`, `panic_hook_tests::test_panic_hook_logs_location_via_tracing_without_payload`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-followups` (GitHub #287 "Daemon" section, `fix/p3fu-daemon`, walkthrough 147)

Follow-ups of the daemon logging/shutdown work (DLX rows) and of the systemd readiness work (DHX rows) (ADR 2026-10-01 "Debug-Only Panic Messages, Accept Backoff and Tracked Evidence Writes").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| DFU1 | The process panic hook logs the bounded panic message (`MAX_DEBUG_PANIC_MESSAGE_CHARS` = 512) only under `PanicMessagePolicy::LogMessage`, selected by `PanicMessagePolicy::for_build()` in debug builds; a release build installs the unchanged payload-free `install_panic_hook()` and never logs the message (owner decision 2026-10-01) | Contract tests (`panic_hook_build_policy_tests::test_panic_message_policy_follows_debug_assertions`, `test_debug_policy_logs_message_and_release_policy_withholds_it`, `test_production_main_selects_panic_policy_from_build_profile`); pre-existing `panic_hook_tests::test_panic_hook_logs_location_via_tracing_without_payload` | ✅ Verified |
| DFU2 | A failed `accept()` is retried after a bounded exponential `AcceptBackoff` (5 ms doubling, capped at 1 s, reset on success, never zero); the loop never stops, keeps serving after the errors, and the shutdown signal interrupts a backoff sleep | Contract tests (`daemon_followups_tests::test_accept_backoff_doubles_from_initial_and_caps_at_max`, `test_accept_backoff_defaults_and_degenerate_bounds`, `test_accept_errors_back_off_without_stopping_the_loop`, `test_shutdown_interrupts_accept_backoff_promptly`, `test_accept_until_shutdown_still_serves_a_real_listener`) | ✅ Verified |
| DFU3 | Spoof evidence writes run in the tracked `BlockingTasks` set (`ConnectionDispatcher::evidence_writes`), never detached; finished writes are reaped on spawn; `soos-daemon` drains them after the connection drain with what is left of the one-`connection_timeout` budget, a write still running at the deadline being reported as abandoned | Contract tests (`daemon_followups_tests::test_blocking_tasks_drain_waits_for_in_flight_write`, `test_blocking_tasks_drain_is_bounded_by_budget`, `test_blocking_tasks_reap_finished_jobs_on_spawn`, `test_blocking_task_panic_is_counted_not_propagated`, `test_spoof_evidence_write_is_tracked_and_drained_at_shutdown`, `test_production_main_drains_evidence_writes_within_the_shutdown_budget`) | ✅ Verified |
| DFU4 | The defensive downgrade branch is exercised: `stamp_response` (the only stamping path of `build_response`) turns `Allow` into `Unavailable` / `InternalError` with `issued = expires = 0` on a clock error or a zero reading, keeps every other verdict, and never yields `Allow` without a clock | Contract tests (`daemon_followups_tests::test_clock_failure_downgrades_allow_to_unavailable_internal_error`, `test_zero_clock_reading_downgrades_allow`, `test_clock_failure_never_yields_allow_for_any_verdict`, `test_working_clock_keeps_verdict_and_stamps_validity_window`, `test_build_response_renders_through_stamp_response`) | ✅ Verified |
| DFU5 | `soos-admin status` shows `StatusResponse::memory_locked` as `DaemonStatusReport::memory_locked` (`Some(true)` LOCKED, `Some(false)` NOT LOCKED, `None` / JSON `null` / `N/A` when the daemon is unreachable, never claimed locked); two existing report literals gained the field as a setup-only change | Contract tests (`status_memory_locked_tests::test_status_reports_memory_locked_from_daemon`, `test_status_reports_memory_unlocked_from_daemon`, `test_offline_status_never_claims_memory_locked`) | ✅ Verified |
| DFU6 | `packaging/soos-daemon.service` sets `StartLimitIntervalSec=320` so that `StartLimitIntervalSec >= StartLimitBurst * (TimeoutStartSec + RestartSec)` (5 × (60 + 2) = 310): a start that always times out reaches the start limit instead of retrying forever; the IDT6 literal assertion was migrated from 60 to 320 with owner approval 2026-10-01 | Invariant tests (`installer_templates_contract::test_daemon_unit_start_limit_interval_covers_burst_of_timed_out_starts`, `installer_templates_contract::test_daemon_unit_requires_deployed_models_and_bounds_restarts`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-review-minors` (GitHub #315, review findings DMN-NEW-2..5 of 2026-10-02, `fix/daemon-review-minors`, walkthrough 169)

Daemon minor findings of the 2026-10-02 review (ADR 2026-10-02 "Decaying Inference Estimate, Session Policy Locked to the Mock Camera, Shared Bounded Config Reader").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| DRM1 | `InferenceEstimator::decay_toward_default` halves the distance to `DEFAULT_INFERENCE_ESTIMATE_MS`, converges to it exactly, never goes below it and never raises an estimate already at or below it; `InferenceGate::decay_estimate` delegates to it | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_estimator_decay_moves_a_saturated_estimate_toward_the_default`, `test_drm_estimator_decay_never_raises_a_low_estimate`, `test_drm_gate_decay_estimate_delegates_to_the_estimator`) | ✅ Verified |
| DRM2 | With the gate seeded at `MAX_INFERENCE_ESTIMATE_MS`, repeated 1000 ms PAM requests recover face authentication: the first request is finalized by the estimate gate with zero captures evaluated, fails closed (`Unavailable` / `Timeout`, no inference) and decays the estimate; an `Allow` follows within three requests (DMN-NEW-2) | Dispatcher test (`inference_budget_tests::test_drm_estimate_seeded_at_max_recovers_for_1000ms_requests`); pre-existing `inference_budget_tests::test_159_estimate_exceeding_budget_times_out_and_records_attempt`, `test_159_client_deadline_shorter_than_estimate_skips_inference` unchanged | ✅ Verified |
| DRM3 | `[dispatcher] enforce_active_session = false` is refused by `DaemonConfig::validate` / `from_toml_str` with an error naming the key unless `[pipeline] use_mock_camera = true`; `true` is always accepted (DMN-NEW-3, orchestrator decision) | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_enforce_active_session_false_is_refused_in_production_config`, `test_drm_enforce_active_session_false_is_accepted_with_mock_camera`, `test_drm_validate_refuses_disabled_session_policy_without_mock_camera`, `test_drm_enforce_active_session_true_is_always_accepted`) | ✅ Verified |
| DRM4 | `daemon.toml` is read through `soos_camera_v4l::daemon_config::read_daemon_config_text` (`O_PATH`, regular file, 1 MiB bound): a FIFO is refused promptly at the explicit and at the system path, a file above 1 MiB and a directory are refused, no check-then-open remains (only `NotFound` falls back to the defaults) (DMN-NEW-4) | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_load_from_path_refuses_a_fifo_promptly`, `test_drm_system_config_fifo_is_refused_promptly`, `test_drm_load_from_path_refuses_a_file_above_one_mib`, `test_drm_load_from_path_accepts_a_file_at_the_limit_boundary`, `test_drm_system_config_directory_is_refused`, `test_drm_config_loader_has_no_unbounded_read_or_is_file_check`); pre-existing `warmup_default_tests::test_load_or_default_without_system_file_uses_runtime_default` unchanged | ✅ Verified |
| DRM5 | An unknown `[pipeline] sensor_preference` keeps the default and is reported in `DaemonConfig::warnings` naming the key, never the value; `main.rs` logs each warning at `warn` after logging is initialized (DMN-NEW-4) | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_unknown_sensor_preference_is_reported_as_a_load_warning`, `test_drm_known_sensor_preference_has_no_warning`, `test_drm_main_logs_config_warnings_after_logging_init`) | ✅ Verified |
| DRM6 | `SocketConfig::validate` refuses a `socket_path` that is not a file name directly inside `socket_dir` (nested, sibling, `..`, root), also at load time | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_socket_path_must_be_a_direct_child_of_socket_dir`, `test_drm_socket_path_inside_socket_dir_is_accepted`, `test_drm_socket_path_outside_socket_dir_is_refused_at_load`) | ✅ Verified |
| DRM7 | `bind_socket` checks, before binding and on the locked directory descriptor, that the socket directory belongs to the configured `socket_group` when `enforce_root_owner` is set (`socket::validate_directory_group`); the shipped unit still starts under a real systemd (`RuntimeDirectory=soos`, `Group=soos`) | Contract tests (`daemon_review_minor_tests::test_drm_socket_directory_group_must_match_the_socket_group`, `test_drm_bind_socket_checks_the_directory_group_when_root_owned_is_enforced`); `tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh --models host` (parts 2 and 5: READY=1, socket 0660 root:soos, clean stop) | ✅ Verified |
| DRM8 | Docs match the code: `Docs/DAEMON.md` §4 start-up steps are numbered 1..N without duplicates and include the inference warm-up before the bind / `READY=1`, no start-up step is left in §5; `AI/ARCHITECTURE.md` names both evidence reasons, Invariant 3 claims no `/etc/passwd` cross-check, the `root:soos` directory claim is backed by `validate_directory_group`; the `sd_notify` module doc says an abstract address is network-namespace scoped (DMN-NEW-5, suggestion) | Invariant tests (`daemon_docs_contract::test_daemon_doc_startup_steps_are_sequential_and_include_warmup`, `test_architecture_doc_matches_daemon_code_claims`, `test_sd_notify_doc_does_not_overclaim_private_network_reachability`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `systemd-unit-acceptance` (GitHub #211 ONB-15 residual — real systemd PID 1, `test/systemd-unit-acceptance`, walkthrough 158)

`tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh` boots ubuntu:24.04 with systemd as PID 1 (`tests/docker/Dockerfile.systemd`, privileged container with a private cgroup namespace, host V4L2 nodes removed), installs the freshly built release daemon and the shipped unit with `scripts/install.sh`, and drives the unit with `systemctl`. CI job `systemd-unit` (part of `CI Success`) runs it with `--models download`; locally `--models auto` copies the host's attested models read-only. Evidence of the 2026-10-01 runs is in walkthrough 158.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| SUA1 | `systemd-analyze verify` accepts the installed unit (byte-identical to `packaging/soos-daemon.service`) with exit 0 and no unknown, ignored or invalid directive; systemd loads every hardening directive (`systemctl show`) and the shipped values `Type=notify`, `TimeoutStartSec=60`, `RestartSec=2`, `StartLimitBurst=5`, `StartLimitIntervalSec=320` unchanged (no drop-in); `systemd-analyze security` reports 1.7 | Docker harness part 4 (`tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh`); invariant tests (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_rebuilds_and_installs_like_an_operator`, `systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_exists_and_isolates_the_host`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA2 | Without `/var/lib/soos/models/manifest.toml`, `systemctl start` exits 0 and leaves the enabled unit `inactive` with `ConditionResult=no` ("start condition unmet"), zero daemon executions and `NRestarts=0` after three `RestartSec` periods; after a container reboot the enabled unit is skipped at boot the same way | Docker harness part 1 and part 1 (boot); invariant test (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_asserts_condition_failed`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA3 | A daemon that always fails to start (manifest present, corrupt models) runs exactly `StartLimitBurst` = 5 times (`NRestarts=5`, about 11 s), then systemd logs "Start request repeated too quickly" (start-limit-hit) well within `StartLimitIntervalSec=320` and leaves it `failed`; a later `systemctl start` is refused without running the daemon. systemd 255 keeps `Result=exit-code` (the first failure of the run) rather than `start-limit-hit` | Docker harness part 3; invariant test (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_asserts_start_limit_hit_with_shipped_values`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA4 | With the attested models deployed by `scripts/download_models.sh` and the mock camera, `systemctl start` returns only after `READY=1`: at that moment `/run/soos/daemon.sock` exists (`0660 root:soos`, `/run/soos` `0750 root:soos`), `ActiveState=active`, the journal order is socket bound → readiness reported → `Started`, and `scripts/wait_daemon_ready.sh` (`soos-admin --format json status`) reports `is_healthy`; start-to-ready (`ExecMainStartTimestamp` → `ActiveEnterTimestamp`) measured 685–826 ms on a 16-thread i7-13620H, under the 30 s harness bound and `TimeoutStartSec=60` | Docker harness part 2 (`--models host` or `--models download`); invariant test (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_asserts_ready_ordering_and_clean_stop`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA5 | `systemctl stop` is clean: SIGTERM logged, graceful drain finished ("soos-daemon terminated cleanly"), `Result=success`, exit status 0, socket removed, no timeout or SIGKILL, 65–72 ms against `TimeoutStopSec` 90 s | Docker harness part 5; invariant test (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_asserts_ready_ordering_and_clean_stop`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA6 | CI job `systemd-unit` (after `lint`, SHA-pinned checkout, no persisted credentials, 30 min timeout) runs the harness with `--models download` on every pull request and is part of the `CI Success` aggregate | Invariant test (`systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_ci_runs_systemd_acceptance_and_gates_on_it`) | ✅ Verified |
| SUA7 | Start-to-ready stays under `TimeoutStartSec=60` on the slowest supported target CPU, with the real V4L2 camera instead of the mock | Real-host measurement on the slowest target hardware | ⬜ Pending (needs the slowest target CPU and a real camera) |
| SUA8 | The privileged systemd container never writes host kernel or firmware state: `tests/docker/Dockerfile.systemd` masks `systemd-sysctl`, `systemd-modules-load`, `systemd-binfmt` (+ binfmt_misc mount/automount), rfkill, backlight, random-seed and TPM/PCR units (the image build fails if the sysctl, modules-load or binfmt unit is no longer shipped under its name; the host sysctl comparison is the backstop), removes the TPM and rfkill nodes before init; the harness asserts the masks after boot and fails when the host `kernel.*`/`vm.*`/`fs.*` sysctls differ after the run | `systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_container_never_writes_host_state`; local run `--models host`: "Host-state boot units are masked (13 units ...)", "Host kernel.*, vm.* and fs.* sysctls are unchanged after the privileged container ran" (walkthrough 158, candid review fix) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `packaging-ownership-and-arch-pam` (GitHub #301 ONB-NEW-1, #308 TCI-NEW-1, #309 ONB-NEW-2, #316 ONB-NEW-3 / ONB-NEW-4 / TCI-NEW-2 / TCI-NEW-3, `fix/packaging-ownership-and-arch-pam`, walkthrough 166)

Native packages are `root:root` whoever builds them, Docker harnesses never write root-owned files into the host `target/`, the Arch `system-auth` integration is one exact edit of the stock pambase stack proven on the real stock file, packages own the unit under `/usr/lib/systemd/system` without shipping `/run`, the Debian pam-configs are opt-in and the sandbox base images are pinned. Static and functional rows live in `tests/invariants/src/packaging_ownership_contract.rs`; Docker evidence of the 2026-10-02 runs is in walkthrough 166 §8.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| POA1 | An Arch package built by a non-root user (stub artifacts, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`) records uid 0, gid 0 and the names `root`/`root` (pax overrides included) for every entry, on the gzip and the zstd path, with bsdtar and with GNU tar; `.PKGINFO`, `soos-daemon`, `pam_soos.so` and `provision-master-key` are present | `packaging_ownership_contract::test_arch_package_archive_entries_are_root_owned_on_every_archive_path` (Red on `main`: every entry `1000:1000 (<builder>/<builder>)`) | ✅ Verified |
| POA2 | Every archive command of `scripts/build_arch.sh` carries the owner flags of both tar implementations; `scripts/build_deb.sh` keeps `dpkg-deb --build --root-owner-group` | `packaging_ownership_contract::test_package_builders_force_root_ownership_statically` | ✅ Verified |
| POA3 | Every read-write `/workspace` bind mount in `run_tests.sh`, `save.sh`, `scripts/`, `tests/docker/`, `tests/distro/` and `.github/workflows/` also mounts a named Docker volume on `/workspace/target` (`run_tests.sh` and the CI job `pam-integration` use `soos-sandbox-target`) | `packaging_ownership_contract::test_read_write_workspace_mounts_overlay_target_with_a_volume` (Red on `main`: `run_tests.sh` and the `pam-integration` step of `.github/workflows/ci.yml`) | ✅ Verified |
| POA4 | `scripts/install.sh --build` run as root builds through `runuser -u <user>` as the `SUDO_USER`, `DOAS_USER` or `PKEXEC_UID` user and never runs cargo as root; with no invoking user and a checkout not owned by root it refuses ("Refusing to build as root") before building or installing anything | `packaging_ownership_contract::test_install_build_as_root_drops_to_invoking_user_or_refuses` (PATH shims for `id`, `runuser`, `getent`, `cargo`; Red on `main`: `doas` case ran `cargo build --release --locked --workspace` as root) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `arch-faillock-ci-cache-prefix` (GitHub #318 review follow-ups of 2026-10-02, `fix/arch-faillock-ci-prefix`, walkthrough 170)

On Arch a face match takes the stock pambase success path (`pam_permit`, `pam_env`, `pam_faillock authsucc`) so it resets the faillock tally, while a locked account still fails; `tests/docker/Dockerfile.systemd` is pinned by digest; the `ort-sys` ONNX Runtime download of the required CI jobs is cached (Cargo.lock key, written by `main` only) and retried a bounded number of times; `soos-gui` runs `soos-enroll` from the install prefix it was built for. Static and functional rows live in `tests/invariants/src/arch_faillock_ci_contract.rs` and `crates/gui/tests/program_path_tests.rs`; Docker evidence is in walkthrough 170 §8.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| AFC1 | The Arch primary rule is exactly `auth  [success=4 default=ignore]  pam_soos.so`, after `pam_faillock.so preauth`; its jump lands on `pam_permit.so`, followed by exactly `pam_env.so` and `pam_faillock.so authsucc`, and skips neither; the snippet carries the same line; `Docs/DISTRIBUTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` §5.2, ARCHITECTURE §5 ("Arch Linux only") and the ADR register describe it; Debian/Fedora/GDM keep `success=done` | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_arch_face_match_lands_on_faillock_authsucc_and_pam_env` (Red on `main`: control `[success=done default=ignore]`); migrated Arch expectations of `soos-invariants::tests::test_pam_config_ordering_matches_spec` and `packaging_ownership_contract::test_arch_system_auth_is_stock_pambase_with_exact_soos_edit` (owner-approved) | ✅ Verified |
| AFC2 | On the real stock pambase `system-auth` with the documented edit: after `deny - 1` wrong passwords a face Allow succeeds with no event and resets the faillock tally to 0; after `deny` wrong passwords a face Allow fails and the tally stays at `deny`; the earlier scenarios (face Allow, valid password without event and tally 0, wrong password with one event and tally 1) still pass | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_arch_harness_face_login_resets_tally_and_lock_still_fails`; Docker `tests/distro/run_distro_validation.sh arch` (walkthrough 170 §8). Red (new scenarios on the `success=done` stack): `face Allow after 2 wrong password(s): faillock tally is 2, expected 0` | ✅ Verified |
| AFC3 | `tests/docker/Dockerfile.systemd` has one `FROM ubuntu:24.04@sha256:<64 hex>` line, identical to the `FROM` line of `tests/docker/Dockerfile.ubuntu` | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_systemd_runtime_image_uses_the_build_image_digest`; migrated `systemd_unit_acceptance_contract::test_systemd_acceptance_harness_exists_and_isolates_the_host` (owner-approved: digest required instead of the bare tag) and `packaging_ownership_contract::test_docker_base_images_are_pinned_by_digest` (file added to its list); `./tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh --models host` passed on the pinned image (walkthrough 170 §8) | ✅ Verified |
| AFC4 | The required jobs `clippy`, `test`, `package-deploy` and `systemd-unit` restore `~/.cache/ort.pyke.io` with `actions/cache/restore` pinned to SHA `55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9` (v6.1.0), key `ort-${{ runner.os }}-${{ hashFiles('Cargo.lock') }}`, before their first build; they save it only on `main` and on a cache miss; host jobs set `ORT_CACHE_DIR` and run the bounded prefetch before cargo, Docker jobs export `SOOS_ORT_CACHE_DIR`; no job-level `permissions:` and no write permission | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_ci_caches_and_retries_the_onnxruntime_download` | ✅ Verified |
| AFC5 | `scripts/prefetch_onnxruntime.sh` runs `cargo check --locked -p ort`, stops at the first success, makes at most 3 attempts by default and exits 1 after the last one, and refuses (exit 2, nothing run) an attempt count outside 1..5 or a base delay above 60 s | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_onnxruntime_prefetch_retries_a_bounded_number_of_times` (`cargo` PATH shim) | ✅ Verified |
| AFC6 | `tests/distro/{debian_ubuntu,fedora_rhel,arch_linux}_test.sh` prefetch before their release build; `tests/distro/run_distro_validation.sh` and `tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh` mount `SOOS_ORT_CACHE_DIR` on `/ort-cache` with `ORT_CACHE_DIR=/ort-cache` only when it is an existing absolute directory (otherwise they fail), and the systemd harness prefetches before its release build | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_docker_harnesses_prefetch_and_share_the_onnxruntime_cache` | ✅ Verified |
| AFC7 | `SOOS_ENROLL_PROGRAM` is `/usr/bin/soos-enroll` when `SOOS_BINDIR` is unset or `/usr/bin`, `<SOOS_BINDIR>/soos-enroll` otherwise, and the import helper uses it; `crates/gui/build.rs` rejects a relative, non-normalized (`//`, `.`, `..`, trailing `/`, `/` alone), over-long or non-`[A-Za-z0-9._+-]` value; `pkexec`/`systemctl` stay `/usr/bin`; `install.sh --build --prefix /opt/soos` builds with `SOOS_BINDIR=/opt/soos/bin` (through `runuser` too) and installing prebuilt artifacts under another prefix warns | `crates/gui/tests/program_path_tests.rs::test_soos_enroll_program_follows_build_time_bindir` (Red with `SOOS_BINDIR=/opt/soos/bin`: `left: "/usr/bin/soos-enroll"`), `crates/gui/tests/program_path_tests.rs::test_bindir_validation_rejects_relative_and_unsafe_paths`, `crates/gui/tests/program_path_tests.rs::test_bindir_validation_accepts_normalized_absolute_directories`, `crates/gui/tests/program_path_tests.rs::test_system_tools_stay_under_usr_bin`, `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_install_build_exports_the_prefix_bindir` | ✅ Verified |
| AFC8 | Every package builder (`scripts/build_{deb,arch,rpm,packages}.sh`, `packaging/arch/PKGBUILD`, `packaging/rpm/soos.spec`, `packaging/debian/rules`) sets `SOOS_BINDIR` to `/usr/bin` (`%{_bindir}`) before its release build | `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_package_builders_pin_soos_bindir_to_usr_bin` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-hygiene` (Review findings PAM-09 / PAM-16 / PAM-17 — GitHub #263, #264, #265)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PHY1 | Calling a PAM entry point never takes over the process panic hook: a panic raised outside every soos entry point after `pam_sm_authenticate` returned reaches the hook installed before the module was first used, on every later call too; the hook is installed once (`Once`) with `std::panic::take_hook()` chaining (#263) | Integration test (`panic_hook_chain_tests::test_panic_outside_entry_point_still_reaches_prior_hook`); invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_panic_hook_chains_to_prior_hook`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY2 | Inside `syslog::catch_entry` a panic is caught, its source location is recorded for the syslog line and the prior hook is not called (no stderr output); nested entry points restore the depth; an outside panic is not recorded as a soos location (#263) | Integration test (`panic_hook_capture_tests::test_entry_panic_is_captured_silently_and_outside_panic_is_chained`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY3 | Rejected PAM arguments keep the safe default and each yields one `ConfigWarning`: unparsable `timeout_ms=` / `uid=`, clamped `timeout_ms=`, unknown `event=`, empty `socket_path=` / `service=` / `disable_if_file=`, an argument of 256 bytes or more (also through `parse_cstrs`), a null `argv` entry, more than 64 arguments, non-UTF-8, unknown keys; valid arguments produce none (#265) | Integration tests (`config_warning_tests::test_valid_arguments_produce_no_warning`, `config_warning_tests::test_unparsable_timeout_keeps_default_and_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_out_of_range_timeout_is_clamped_and_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_unparsable_uid_keeps_none_and_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_empty_values_warn`, `config_warning_tests::test_unknown_event_value_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_argument_of_max_len_or_more_is_dropped_and_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_null_argument_pointer_warns`, `config_warning_tests::test_too_many_arguments_warns_once`, `config_warning_tests::test_non_utf8_argument_warns_through_cstr_parser`, `config_warning_tests::test_cstr_parser_enforces_the_same_length_bound`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY4 | `service=` longer than `MAX_SERVICE_LEN` is cut on a UTF-8 character boundary (no fallback to `pam_soos` when byte 64 is inside a multibyte character) and reports `ServiceTruncated` (#265) | Integration tests (`config_service_boundary_tests::test_argv_service_cut_inside_multibyte_char_keeps_prefix`, `config_service_boundary_tests::test_cstr_service_cut_inside_multibyte_char_keeps_prefix`, `config_warning_tests::test_long_service_is_truncated_and_warns`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY5 | Warnings never echo a rejected value (an unknown key only when it is at most 32 bytes of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`), are single-line, and are logged at `LOG_AUTHPRIV \| LOG_WARNING` by `parse_argv` / `parse_cstrs` / `collect_argv_logged` (#265) | Integration tests (`config_warning_tests::test_unknown_argument_reports_key_but_never_value`, `config_warning_tests::test_unknown_argument_with_unprintable_key_is_not_echoed`, `config_warning_tests::test_every_warning_display_is_single_line_and_value_free`); invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_config_warnings_are_logged_at_warning_level`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY6 | The IPC client has no tautological `total_received` truncation re-check (the counted read reports truncation), accepts a response through `Response::matches_request`, and the PAM verdict mapping uses `Verdict::should_ignore`, so the protocol crate owns both predicates (#264) | Invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_ipc_client_uses_protocol_acceptance_predicates`); behaviour unchanged (`ipc_tests::*`, `strict_decode_tests::*`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY7 | The exported `pam_sm_authenticate` symbol calls `SoosPam::sm_authenticate` for every non-null handle inside its own `catch_c_entry` boundary, so the `PamHooks` implementation is production code (#264) | Invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_exported_authenticate_symbol_delegates_to_pam_hooks`); behaviour (`pam_silent_tests::*`, `pam_bindings_tests::*`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY8 | `parse_argv` has no unreachable null check on `argv.add(i)` (only the entry itself is checked) (#264) | Invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_parse_argv_has_no_unreachable_null_check`); unit (`config_warning_tests::test_null_argument_pointer_warns`) | ✅ Verified |
| PHY9 | `crates/pam/build.rs` emits `rerun-if-changed` for the `libpam.so` it found, or for the `libpam.so.0` fallback and its directory, so installing `libpam0g-dev` later re-runs the script (#264) | Invariant (`pam_hygiene_contract::test_pam_build_script_reruns_on_libpam_changes`) | ✅ Verified |

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|

| PEV3 | Spoof evidence respects `daily_cap_per_uid`: three spoof-vetoed requests with a cap of 1 store one snapshot | Integration test (`pad_evidence_tests::test_261_spoof_evidence_respects_daily_cap_per_uid`) | ✅ Verified |
| PEV4 | A detected spoof stays the final reason: spoof then no-face (or any later capture class) reports `Deny` / `PadFailed`, never `NoFace`; no-face without a spoof still reports `NoFace` | Unit and property tests (`pad_final_reason_tests::test_262_spoof_then_no_face_frames_keeps_pad_failed_reason`, `test_262_no_face_without_spoof_still_reports_no_face`, `prop_262_spoof_reason_outranks_every_later_capture`); integration test (`pad_evidence_tests::test_262_spoof_then_no_face_captures_reports_pad_failed`) | ✅ Verified |
| PEV5 | Spoof evidence captured on real hardware (printed photo or screen replay in front of the physical camera) decodes to a recognizable RGB image through `EvidenceStore::load_snapshot` and `EvidenceRecord::to_rgb24` | Physical validation campaign | ⬜ Pending (needs real camera and PAD model) |

## Component: `storage-aad-binding` (Review finding STO-22 — GitHub #266)

AES-GCM associated data (UID or date/snapshot id, file role, payload format version 2) for the biometric and evidence stores, with a read path for legacy unbound files (ADR 2026-09-30 "AES-GCM Associated Data for Stored Templates and Evidence").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| SAD1 | `BiometricStore::enroll` writes the bound envelope (`SOOSBIO1` magic kept, `AAD\x02` marker, clear big-endian UID); the file never authenticates with the unbound codec | `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_enrolled_template_declares_bound_format_and_uid`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_enrolled_template_is_not_readable_as_unbound_payload`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_format_constants`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_template_aad_binds_domain_version_and_uid` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD2 | A bound template bound to another UID is refused by the codec with `CorruptFile` before any CBOR parsing; a copied file is refused by `get` / `get_metadata`; a rewritten clear UID, any bit flip, truncation or a wrong key fail authentication | `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_decrypt_with_other_uid_fails_before_cbor_parsing`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_bound_payload_rejects_tampering_and_wrong_key`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_bound_template_copied_to_other_uid_is_refused`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_forged_clear_uid_fails_authentication` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD3 | Legacy unbound templates stay readable (`PayloadFormat::LegacyV1`) with the plaintext UID check; re-enrollment and `migrate_legacy_template` upgrade them to the bound format without content change; a legacy file with a foreign UID is refused and left untouched | `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_legacy_unbound_template_is_still_readable`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_reenroll_upgrades_legacy_template_to_bound_format`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_legacy_template_with_foreign_uid_is_still_refused`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_decrypt_template_payload_reports_legacy_format`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_template_format_reports_legacy_bound_and_missing`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_migrate_legacy_template_reencrypts_once_and_preserves_content`, `aad_migration_tests::test_sad_migrate_refuses_legacy_template_with_foreign_uid` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD4 | Evidence snapshots are written with the bound envelope (`SOOSEVD1` magic kept, `AAD\x02` marker) and bound to their date partition and snapshot id: moving to another partition or renaming to another id fails, the unbound codec cannot read them, field boundaries of the AAD are unambiguous | `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_stored_snapshot_declares_bound_format`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_stored_snapshot_is_not_readable_as_unbound_payload`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_snapshot_moved_to_another_date_partition_is_refused`, `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_snapshot_renamed_to_another_id_is_refused`, `aad_codec_tests::test_sad_evidence_format_constants`, `aad_codec_tests::test_sad_snapshot_aad_binds_domain_date_and_id`, `aad_codec_tests::test_sad_snapshot_payload_roundtrip_and_binding` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD5 | Legacy unbound evidence snapshots stay readable (including the historical `.webp.enc` suffix, which is not bound) | `aad_binding_tests::test_sad_legacy_unbound_snapshot_in_partition_is_still_readable`, `aad_codec_tests::test_sad_snapshot_codec_reads_legacy_unbound_payload`; pre-existing `frame_format_tests::test_legacy_v1_record_still_decodes`, `opaque_suffix_tests::test_legacy_webp_enc_snapshot_is_listed_decrypted_and_purged` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD6 | Store write/read paths use only the AAD-bound codecs, both crypto modules pass `aead::Payload` with format version 2, and both crate documents state the binding and the rollback limit | `storage_aad_contract::test_stores_write_only_aad_bound_payloads`, `storage_aad_contract::test_crypto_modules_pass_associated_data`, `storage_aad_contract::test_storage_docs_state_the_rollback_limit` | ✅ Verified |
| SAD7 | Rollback of an older still-valid ciphertext of the same UID / same snapshot path is detected | Not implemented by design (needs a monotonic counter outside root's reach, e.g. a TPM NV index; root attackers are out of scope); documented in `Docs/BIOMETRIC_STORE_CRATE.md` and `Docs/EVIDENCE_STORE_CRATE.md` | ⬜ Pending (out of scope: requires trusted monotonic storage) |

## Component: `install-rustup-bootstrap-and-unit-guards` (Review findings ONB-16, ONB-15 — GitHub #260, #211)

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| IRP1 | `scripts/install_rustup.sh` pins an exact rustup release, one lowercase SHA-256 digest per supported triple (`x86_64` / `aarch64` `unknown-linux-gnu`), the versioned `static.rust-lang.org/rustup/archive/` URL, HTTPS / TLS 1.2+ curl, `sha256sum` verification, never `sh.rustup.rs`, and defaults to the channel of `rust-toolchain.toml` | Invariant test (`rustup_bootstrap_contract::test_rustup_installer_pins_release_checksums_and_toolchain`) | ✅ Verified |
| IRP2 | No sandbox Dockerfile downloads `sh.rustup.rs` or pipes into a shell; each `COPY`s `scripts/install_rustup.sh` and runs it with `--default-toolchain`; `.dockerignore` sends only that script | Invariant test (`rustup_bootstrap_contract::test_sandbox_dockerfiles_use_verified_rustup_installer`) | ✅ Verified |
| IRP3 | A downloaded `rustup-init` whose digest differs is refused with a checksum error, is never executed, and the temporary download directory is removed | Invariant test with stub `curl` (`rustup_bootstrap_contract::test_rustup_installer_refuses_checksum_mismatch_without_executing`) | ✅ Verified |
| IRP4 | Floating channels (`stable`, `beta`, `nightly`), partial versions and injected values are usage errors (exit 2) and an architecture without a pinned digest fails, both before any download | Invariant test with stub `curl` / `uname` (`rustup_bootstrap_contract::test_rustup_installer_rejects_floating_channel_and_unknown_arch`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `wire-single-interpretation-regression` (Review finding PAM-12 — GitHub #224, walkthrough 143)

Every PAM-12 point was already fixed on `main` (strict codec, PCZ1-PCZ2; tagged client frames, #204 and BBX1-BBX4). This component pins the property the review said the tests could not express: a canonically encoded frame is accepted by exactly one decoder. The #224 discriminator item (PCZ6) is closed by the #204 trailer; see the `strict-codec-client-tag-integration` section above, which records the supersession and its ADR (no protocol v2 envelope).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PCX1 | A tagged `Request` frame decodes only as `Request` and a tagged `Event` frame only as `Event` (never as the other client type, `Response` or `StatusResponse`); `Response` and `StatusResponse` frames never decode as each other | Property tests (`wire_exclusivity_tests::prop_pcx_tagged_request_decodes_as_no_other_type`, `wire_exclusivity_tests::prop_pcx_tagged_event_decodes_as_no_other_type`, `wire_exclusivity_tests::prop_pcx_response_and_status_response_are_mutually_exclusive`) | ✅ Verified |
| PCX2 | The PAM-12 collision (a `Request` whose untagged body also decodes as an `Event`, `uid_hint` 0) has exactly one interpretation when tagged, and a tagged `Request` carrying an unknown extra field (schema evolution) is `Malformed`, never re-routed as an `Event` | Contract tests (`wire_exclusivity_tests::test_pcx_tagged_ambiguous_request_has_exactly_one_interpretation`, `wire_exclusivity_tests::test_pcx_tagged_request_with_unknown_extra_field_is_malformed_not_event`) | ✅ Verified |
| PCX3 | The client-side `Response` decoder has no tolerated remainder: any single trailing byte, including either client message tag, is `TrailingBytes { unconsumed: 1 }`, and an `Allow` followed by a client tag returns `PAM_IGNORE` from `pam_sm_authenticate` | Tests (`wire_exclusivity_tests::test_pcx_response_with_any_client_tag_is_rejected`, `wire_exclusivity_tests::prop_pcx_response_rejects_every_single_trailing_byte`, `pcx_client_tag_tolerance_tests::test_pcx_allow_followed_by_a_client_tag_returns_ignore`, `pcx_client_tag_tolerance_tests::test_pcx_direct_authenticate_rejects_allow_with_a_client_tag`) | ✅ Verified |
| PCX4 | The daemon answers the tagged form of the PAM-12 collision sent by a peer whose UID differs from `uid_hint` (the case the removed heuristic consumed as an Event, leaving the client to time out) with a fail-closed `Response` bound to the request nonce | Integration test (`pcx_wire_routing_tests::test_pcx_tagged_ambiguous_request_with_foreign_uid_hint_gets_a_response`) | ✅ Verified |

| SFX3 | The `127.5` versus upstream `128.0` divisor is template-neutral (cos > 0.999 on every synthetic pattern) while the channel order is not (cos < 0.999 on at least one pattern), so a switch to the upstream RGB order is a template-format change | Real-model report test (`embedding_preprocessing_evaluation_tests::test_real_embedding_preprocessing_sensitivity_report`) | ✅ Verified |
| SFX4 | Remaining owner decisions: channel order switched to the upstream RGB with re-enrollment (or kept BGR after measuring real faces), a licensed replacement model (e.g. Apache-2.0 SFace, re-attested) if the ArcFace licence cannot be cleared, and `match_threshold` recalibrated on labelled real captures (FAR/FRR) | Requires owner decisions, approved changes to pinned contract tests and a labelled real face dataset | ⏳ Pending |
| SFX5 | The manifest records the ArcFace licence as SPDX `NOASSERTION` (the source repository declares none; user decision 2026-10-01, pre-existing assertion changed with approval), and `models/README.md` says so | Inference test (`manifest_tests::test_parse_workspace_manifest_file`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `embedding-real-face-evaluation` (GitHub #278 fourth item — real-face LFW evaluation of the shipped pipeline, walkthrough 160)

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| EVR1 | The LFW evaluation harness runs the production path (MJPEG decode, `OrtScrfdDetector` with the production confidence / NMS defaults, `align_face_112`, `OrtEmbeddingExtractor`), loads every model through `ModelRegistry` + `verify_integrity`, and refuses to run without `SOOS_EVAL_LFW_DIR` / `SOOS_EVAL_LFW_PAIRS` / `SOOS_MODELS_DIR` or with data inside the repository | Vision tests (`embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_harness_refuses_to_run_without_env_vars`, `embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_harness_refuses_data_inside_the_repository`) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR2 | The official `pairs.txt` parser is bounded and rejects traversal names, zero image numbers, truncated files and unbounded headers; the FAR threshold, 10-fold accuracy and summary arithmetic are exact on synthetic scores | Vision tests (`embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_pairs_parser_reads_the_official_layout_and_rejects_traversal`, `embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_metrics_threshold_at_far_and_ten_fold_accuracy`) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR3 | Real faces (LFW, 6000 official pairs and 29.2 M extended impostor pairs, 55 of 7701 images failed to detect): RGB input beats the production BGR order (10-fold accuracy 0.9738 vs 0.9668, TAR@FAR 1e-3 0.886 vs 0.836, TAR@FAR 1e-4 0.746 vs 0.666) and the `/127.5` versus `/128` divisor changes no metric by more than 0.001 | Ignored real-face evaluation (`embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_real_face_evaluation_report`, run 2026-10-01, aggregates in walkthrough 160 §3) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR4 | `match_threshold = 0.70` is measured at an impostor rate of about 1e-6 (BGR) / 1e-7 (RGB) with TAR 0.117 / 0.152 on LFW; the documented target FAR <= 1e-3 is reached at a cosine of 0.402 (BGR) / 0.394 (RGB) | Ignored real-face evaluation (`embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_real_face_evaluation_report`, walkthrough 160 §3) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR5 | Licence survey of lighter embedding models with sources (walkthrough 160 §4); the only permissively licensed, directly downloadable ONNX candidate (OpenCV Zoo SFace, Apache-2.0 files, undocumented training data) is attested by SHA-256 and measured with the same harness: 10-fold accuracy 0.9848, TAR@FAR 1e-4 0.984, embedding p50 10.3 ms vs 35.3 ms for ArcFace | Ignored real-face evaluation with `SOOS_EVAL_CANDIDATE_DIR` (`embedding_lfw_evaluation_tests::test_lfw_real_face_evaluation_report`) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR6 | The evaluation stays `#[ignore]`d and env-gated, its downloader `scripts/fetch_lfw_eval.sh` is HTTPS-only, size-bounded, SHA-256 verified and refuses (without creating) a cache inside the repository, and no LFW artefact exists in the source tree | Invariant tests (`embedding_evaluation_contract::test_lfw_evaluation_harness_is_ignored_and_env_gated`, `embedding_evaluation_contract::test_lfw_fetch_script_refuses_a_cache_inside_the_repository`, `embedding_evaluation_contract::test_no_lfw_data_is_in_the_source_tree`) | ✅ Verified |
| EVR7 | Owner decision on the ADR proposal "Real-Face Embedding Evaluation and Recalibration Proposal": SFace adoption under a new manifest id with re-enrollment and `match_threshold = 0.50` (decided 2026-10-01, ADR "SFace Embedding Model Replaces ArcFace ResNet34", implemented by rows SFC1–SFC18) | ADR in `AI/DECISIONS.md`, invariant `embedding_model_docs_contract::test_docs_state_the_sface_model_and_the_050_default`; same-camera genuine scores pending the owner's face session (GitHub #296) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `daemon-template-check-order` (GitHub #298 first and fifth items, `fix/daemon-template-check-order`, walkthrough 163)

| ID | Criterion | Verification Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| TCO1 | A foreign template (retired ArcFace id, or current id with another vector length) is answered `Unavailable` / `ModelUnavailable` without waking the camera (`notify_activity`) or reading a capture (`latest_frame`), and no inference runs (spy camera + spy extractor) | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_foreign_template_never_wakes_the_camera`, `template_check_order_tests::test_wrong_dimension_template_never_wakes_the_camera`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO2 | With a cold camera that never becomes ready, a foreign template is answered `ModelUnavailable` at once (no wake wait, not `CameraUnavailable`) | Integration test (`template_check_order_tests::test_foreign_template_does_not_wait_for_a_cold_camera`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO3 | A current SFace template still wakes the camera, reads captures, runs inference and authorizes (`Allow` / `FaceMatch`) | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_current_template_still_wakes_the_camera_and_authenticates`, `sface_template_binding_tests::test_current_sface_template_is_evaluated`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO4 | A UID without a template keeps its verdict (`Unavailable` / `InternalError`), is answered before the camera wake and still consumes one attempt (GitHub #200 contract kept) | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_missing_template_is_refused_before_the_camera_wake_and_still_counts`, `rate_limit_reservation_tests::test_200_attempt_ending_before_vision_work_is_recorded`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO5 | A foreign template consumes no rate-limit attempt, sequentially or concurrently (limiter tracks nothing; never `RateLimited`) | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_foreign_template_consumes_no_rate_limit_attempt`, `template_check_order_tests::test_concurrent_foreign_template_requests_reserve_nothing`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO6 | For a current template the attempt is already reserved (one attempt, under the policy write lock) when the camera is woken and when the first capture is read | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_current_template_reserves_the_attempt_before_wake_and_capture`, `rate_limit_reservation_tests::test_200_attempt_is_reserved_before_vision_work`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO7 | A rate-limited request with a current template is refused `ProtocolError` / `RateLimited` without waking the camera | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_rate_limited_current_template_never_wakes_the_camera`, `rate_limit_reservation_tests::test_200_allow_then_rate_limited_with_one_attempt`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO8 | Concurrent current-template requests still share the limit atomically (one attempt: exactly one `RateLimited`, at most one `Allow`) | Integration tests (`template_check_order_tests::test_concurrent_current_template_reservations_stay_atomic`, `rate_limit_reservation_tests::test_200_concurrent_matching_auths_with_one_attempt_allow_at_most_once`, `rate_limit_reservation_tests::test_200_concurrent_denied_auths_with_one_attempt_yield_one_evaluation`) | ✅ Verified |
| TCO9 | A store error (tampered or unreadable template file) is answered before the camera wake as `Unavailable` / `InternalError`, runs no inference and still consumes the rate-limit attempt | `crates/daemon/tests/template_check_order_tests.rs::test_store_error_is_refused_before_the_camera_wake_and_still_counts` | ✅ Verified |
