## Component: `sface-gui-spec-doc-followups` (GitHub #298, GUI, extractor and docs follow-ups of the SFace switch, `fix/sface-gui-and-spec-followups`, walkthrough 164)

| ID | Criterion | Verification Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| SGF1 | Selecting a profile replaces the GUI match reference and withdraws the previous score in one critical section: a current template becomes the reference, a foreign template clears reference and score and sets the re-enrollment note, and a concurrently publishing worker leaves no score visible after a foreign selection | GUI tests (`match_reference_selection_tests::test_selecting_current_template_sets_reference_and_withdraws_previous_score`, `match_reference_selection_tests::test_switching_to_foreign_profile_shows_no_stale_score`, `match_reference_selection_tests::test_concurrent_worker_leaves_no_score_after_foreign_selection`) | ✅ Verified |
| SGF2 | A failed or empty template lookup clears the match reference note, the reference and the score | GUI test (`match_reference_selection_tests::test_failed_template_lookup_clears_note_reference_and_score`) | ✅ Verified |
| SGF5 | The GUI match reference (`WorkerSharedInput::match_reference`, the copy of the enrolled template embedding) is a `Zeroizing<Vec<f32>>` cloned straight from the template's `Zeroizing` vector, so it is wiped when replaced, cleared or dropped; scoring borrows it without copying | GUI test (`match_reference_zeroize_tests::test_match_reference_is_a_zeroizing_container`, compile-time type check plus unchanged selection and scoring behavior) | ✅ Verified |
| SGF3 | `OrtEmbeddingExtractor::with_spec` rejects a non-NCHW spec at construction with `InferenceError::InvalidInput` (NCHW or NHWC session alike); the NCHW shipped spec is accepted | Contract tests (`embedding_spec_layout_tests::test_with_spec_rejects_nhwc_spec_for_nchw_session`, `embedding_spec_layout_tests::test_with_spec_rejects_nhwc_spec_for_nhwc_session`, `embedding_spec_layout_tests::test_with_spec_accepts_nchw_shipped_spec`) | ✅ Verified |
| SGF4 | The `Docs/BIOMETRIC_STORE_CRATE.md` CRUD example builds an `sface_2021dec` template of 128 values (no `glintr100`, no 512-D vector) | Invariant (`sface_followups_contract::test_biometric_store_doc_example_uses_shipped_sface_template`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `camera-vision-followups` (GitHub #287, camera / vision follow-ups of the P3 batch)

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| CVF1 | `soos-admin camera list` resolves with `[pipeline] camera_device` and `[pipeline] sensor_preference` of `/etc/soos/daemon.toml` (`--config <PATH>` overrides the path), using the daemon's field types and the shared vocabulary: sentinel devices keep auto-detection, an unrecognized `sensor_preference` keeps `prefer_ir` with a note that does not echo the value | `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_config_tests.rs::test_cvf_camera_list_reads_daemon_config_settings`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_config_tests.rs::test_cvf_daemon_config_sentinel_device_and_unknown_preference` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF2 | `--device` and an explicitly typed `--sensor-preference` override the file (the clap default never does; detected through `ArgMatches::value_source`), and `--device auto` re-enables auto-detection over a configured device | `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_config_tests.rs::test_cvf_camera_list_flags_override_daemon_config`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_config_tests.rs::test_cvf_camera_list_config_flag_and_explicit_preference_detection` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF3 | A missing, non-regular, unreadable, oversized (`MAX_DAEMON_CONFIG_BYTES` = 1 MiB, bounded read) or malformed (TOML error, camera key of the wrong type) configuration falls back to the soos-daemon defaults with a note naming the reason | `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_config_tests.rs::test_cvf_missing_or_invalid_daemon_config_falls_back_with_note` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF4 | A panic of the `v4l` 0.14 crate (non-UTF-8 capability strings, unknown format enums) in the daemon enumeration probe, the frame-size hints, the `soos-admin` probe or the capture open path becomes an `io::Error` (`V4L_PANIC_MESSAGE`, payload dropped) mapped to a skipped node, a probe failure or an `UnsupportedDevice` capture error; no direct `query_caps` / `enum_formats` / `set_format` call outside `crates/camera-v4l/src/v4l_guard.rs` | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_panic_guard_tests.rs::test_cvf_guard_turns_an_injected_panic_into_an_error`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_panic_guard_tests.rs::test_cvf_guard_catches_the_real_v4l_non_utf8_capability_panic`, `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_v4l_metadata_calls_only_through_panic_guard` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF5 | The capture supervisor builds its hints only with `supervisor_sensor_hints` and keeps the by-id IR token on shared stems: it may classify a node Infrared where the resolver said RGB, never the reverse (ADR 2026-10-01 "Capture Supervisor Keeps the By-Id IR Token on Shared Stems") | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_classification_tests.rs::test_cvf_supervisor_keeps_shared_stem_ir_token_decisive`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_classification_tests.rs::test_cvf_by_id_hint_never_downgrades_infrared`, `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_supervisor_hints_single_source_and_adr` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF6 | `BiometricEmbedding::to_vec` returns a wipe-on-drop `Zeroizing<Vec<f32>>` copy (RFX5 still exempts only the `self.vector` receiver) | `crates/inference-ort/tests/embedding_copy_zeroize_tests.rs::test_cvf_embedding_to_vec_copy_is_zeroizing`, `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_embedding_to_vec_returns_zeroizing_copy`, `review_followups_contract::test_rfx_ort_output_copies_stay_in_zeroizing_containers` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF7 | A live `scripts/install.sh` runs the installed `soos-admin camera list --config <sysconfdir>/soos/daemon.toml` through `scripts/camera_report.sh` after `COMMITTED=true` and before the summary, never in a DESTDIR staging build, and a failure is only a warning | `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_install_reports_camera_selection_non_fatally` | ✅ Verified |
| CVF8 | `scripts/camera_report.sh` prints the report and exits 0 whatever happens (no camera selected, missing binary, hang stopped by `--timeout`) | `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_camera_report_helper_is_informational_and_bounded` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `camera-alias-and-v4l-guard-followups` (GitHub #289, camera follow-ups of the #287 batch)

By-id alias lookup of a plain `/dev/videoN` capture path, silent caught `v4l` panics and guarded MMAP stream teardown (ADR 2026-10-01 "By-Id Alias Lookup for Plain Capture Nodes; Silent, Guarded v4l Teardown").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| CAG1 | When the opened capture path carries no by-id name (a plain `/dev/videoN` node), `open_and_stream` completes the `supervisor_sensor_hints` result with `supervisor_alias_hints`: the node's alias in `DEFAULT_BY_ID_DIR` is found through the single bounded scanner `SystemCameraEnumerator::by_id_aliases` (an IR-token alias preferred among several), a name already present is never replaced, the frame sizes are kept, and a missing node, directory or alias leaves the hints unchanged | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_alias_hint_tests.rs::test_cag_plain_node_path_takes_its_by_id_alias_name`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_alias_hint_tests.rs::test_cag_alias_lookup_edge_cases_keep_or_fill_only`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_alias_hint_tests.rs::test_cag_alias_lookup_is_bounded_on_a_crowded_directory`, `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_open_path_looks_up_the_by_id_alias` | ✅ Verified |
| CAG2 | The alias lookup never turns an `Infrared` classification into another type (exhaustive cards × formats × frame sizes × alias sets) | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/supervisor_alias_hint_tests.rs::test_cag_alias_lookup_never_downgrades_infrared` | ✅ Verified |
| CAG3 | A panic caught by `guard_v4l_call` does not reach the process panic hook (thread-local depth flag, nesting supported), while unguarded panics on other threads (also during a concurrent guarded call) and on the same thread after the guard returns still reach the previously installed hook; one wrapper hook, installed once with `take_hook` / `set_hook`, never swapped per call | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_panic_hook_filter_tests.rs::test_cag_guarded_panics_are_silent_and_other_panics_reach_the_previous_hook`, `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_guard_installs_one_chained_hook_filter` | ✅ Verified |
| CAG4 | The MMAP stream is created (`mmap_stream_guarded`) and dropped (`guarded_v4l_drop`) through the guard; a teardown panic becomes `V4L_PANIC_MESSAGE` (payload dropped), `CameraError::StreamTeardown` (`CameraErrorKind::Io`, supervisor backoff, never `Dead`) after an idle suspend, is logged after a shutdown and never masks a streaming error | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_teardown_guard_tests.rs::test_cag_guarded_drop_turns_a_teardown_panic_into_an_error`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_teardown_guard_tests.rs::test_cag_stream_teardown_error_is_recoverable_and_payload_free`, `v4l_impl::tests::test_cag_teardown_failure_after_suspend_backs_off`, `v4l_impl::tests::test_cag_teardown_failure_keeps_shutdown_and_streaming_errors`, `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_stream_create_and_teardown_through_the_guard` | ✅ Verified |
| CAG5 | The decisions (alias lookup monotonicity, hook filter, the unguardable double panic of stream + arena teardown) are recorded as an ADR | `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_decision_recorded` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `camera-capture-backend` (GitHub #198, CAM-16, capture-backend seam below the V4L2 supervisor, `test/camera-capture-backend`)

The capture supervisor runs against a crate-private `CaptureBackend` / `CaptureDevice` seam, and frame-size and format enumeration are guarded and bounded; production uses `V4lBackend` (the guarded `v4l` calls), the hermetic tests a scripted fake that drives the real supervisor thread (ADR 2026-10-01 "Capture Backend Seam Below the V4L2 Supervisor"). Walkthrough 159.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| CCB1 | A failing `open(2)` (`EACCES`, `EIO`) leaves the camera `Recovering` with a typed error status (`PermissionDenied`) and no frame, is retried with exponential backoff (gaps ≥ `min_backoff`, 2×, 4×, capped at `max_backoff`) without consulting the resolver, and the first successful open streams (`Streaming`, `Ready`) | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_open_failure_backs_off_then_recovers` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB2 | `EBUSY` at `VIDIOC_S_FMT` (node streamed by another process) is `DeviceBusy`, closes the device between attempts, never re-resolves the path, and streams once the device is released | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_busy_device_is_retried_on_the_same_path` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB3 | Frames flow with the negotiated geometry and format, carry a monotonic timestamp and increasing sequence, and are stamped with the opened node's sensor type: RGB `YUYV` → `Rgb`; IR card name with `GREY` → `Infrared` / `Grey`; `Y16 `-only node → `Infrared`, normalised to packed 8-bit `Grey`; the requested wire fourcc matches | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_frames_flow_stamped_with_the_sensor_type` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB4 | A stream that delivers nothing escalates to `Starved` only after `MAX_STREAM_STALL`, withdraws frames (`Recovering`), tears the stream down and closes the device, then reopens without re-resolution and recovers on a healthy stream | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_stall_escalates_to_starved_then_reopens` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB5 | `ENODEV` on DQBUF (device vanished mid-stream) is `DeviceNotFound`, consults the resolver, and the supervisor reopens and streams the re-enumerated node (`current_device_path` updated, the vanished node not reopened) | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_vanished_device_is_reresolved_to_the_new_node` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB6 | Without a resolver, a vanished node is retried on its configured path (`DeviceNotFound`, `Recovering`, frames withdrawn, path never substituted) and streams again when it returns | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_vanished_device_without_resolver_keeps_its_path` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB7 | A node without `V4L2_CAP_VIDEO_CAPTURE` starts no stream and consults the resolver, which moves the supervisor to a capture node | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_unsupported_node_is_reresolved` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB8 | Past `idle_timeout` the stream is torn down and the device closed (`Standby`, `Suspended`, no frame); activity reopens the same node and streaming resumes | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_idle_suspend_releases_the_device_and_resumes_on_activity` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB9 | A teardown that panics like `v4l` 0.14 after an idle suspend becomes `StreamTeardown` (`Io`, `Recovering`, no frame), never `Dead`; the supervisor reopens, settles in standby and streams again on activity | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_teardown_failure_recovers_instead_of_dying` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB10 | Shutdown of a stalled stream, of a supervisor in a 5 s backoff, and of a streaming session whose teardown panics completes within `C9_SHUTDOWN_BUDGET`, leaves the manager `Stopped`, frame-free and not `Dead`, closes the device and tears every started stream down | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_ccb_shutdown_releases_the_device_within_budget` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB11 | The supervisor reaches the device only through the seam: `supervise` / `open_and_stream` are generic over `CaptureBackend`, both public spawn functions use `V4lBackend`, which is the only opener of a `v4l::Device`, and the fake exists only in the `#[cfg(test)]` module; the guarded-call invariants CVF4, CVF5, CAG1 and CAG4 still hold | `capture_backend_contract::test_ccb_supervisor_reaches_the_device_only_through_the_backend_seam`, `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_v4l_metadata_calls_only_through_panic_guard`, `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_stream_create_and_teardown_through_the_guard` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB12 | The seam decision and its hardware-only remainder are recorded as an ADR | `capture_backend_contract::test_ccb_decision_recorded` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB13 | Index-based V4L2 enumerations are bounded: `enumerate_indexed_bounded` issues at most `max_indices` queries (a driver that never reports the end is cut, `truncated`, always at the first indices), ends at the first error after index 0, returns the index-0 error, and counts skipped (unconvertible) entries toward the bound | `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_endless_enumeration_is_truncated_at_the_bound`, `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_enumeration_ends_at_the_first_error_after_index_zero`, `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_enumeration_error_at_index_zero_is_an_error`, `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_skipped_entries_count_toward_the_bound` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB14 | `VIDIOC_ENUM_FRAMESIZES` is issued only by `enum_framesizes_guarded` (inside `guard_v4l_call`, bounded by `enumerate_indexed_bounded`); no file of `crates/camera-v4l/src` calls `v4l`'s unbounded `enum_framesizes`, and `device_frame_sizes` bounds each fourcc by `MAX_FRAME_SIZE_HINTS` and logs a truncation at debug level with the node path only | `capture_backend_contract::test_ccb_frame_size_enumeration_is_guarded_and_bounded` | ✅ Verified |
| CCB15 | `VIDIOC_ENUM_FMT` is issued only by `enum_formats_guarded` (inside `guard_v4l_call`, `pixelformat` only, never the UTF-8 description), bounded to `MAX_ENUMERATED_FORMATS` indices by `enumerate_indexed_bounded`: a driver that never reports the end yields exactly the first `MAX_ENUMERATED_FORMATS` fourccs, an index-0 error is an empty list, a later error ends the list, a panic inside the guard is caught, a truncation is logged at debug level with the node path only, and no file of `crates/camera-v4l/src` (the guard included) calls `v4l`'s `enum_formats` | `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_endless_format_enumeration_is_truncated_at_the_bound`, `v4l_guard::bounded_enumeration_tests::test_ccb_format_enumeration_end_error_and_panic_semantics`, `capture_backend_contract::test_ccb_format_enumeration_is_guarded_and_bounded`, `camera_vision_followups_contract::test_cvf_v4l_metadata_calls_only_through_panic_guard` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `config-open-and-diagnostics-followups` (GitHub #291, camera / diagnostics follow-ups of the #289 batch, `fix/p3fu3-camera-diagnostics`)

`O_PATH` open of `daemon.toml`, explicit `test-pam` acceptance fields, re-installable `v4l` panic-hook filter and the 32-bit type check (ADR 2026-10-01 "`O_PATH` Config Open, Re-installable v4l Hook Filter and test-pam Acceptance Fields").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| CDF1 | The shared `daemon.toml` reader opens the path with `O_PATH \| O_CLOEXEC` (links followed), checks that handle (regular file ≤ 1 MiB), then reopens the pinned inode through `/proc/self/fd/<n>` with `O_RDONLY \| O_NONBLOCK \| O_CLOEXEC` and re-checks device, inode and file type; a character device behind a link is refused as `NotARegularFile` without being opened (no inotify `IN_OPEN`); a missing `/proc` or another reopen failure is `Unreadable`, never `NotFound`; the existing DGP4–DGP7 reader tests pass unchanged | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/daemon_config_open_path_tests.rs::test_cdf_symlink_to_a_character_device_is_refused_without_opening_it`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/daemon_config_open_path_tests.rs::test_cdf_regular_file_is_read_through_the_reopened_handle`, `daemon_config::tests::test_cdf_reopen_through_proc_reads_the_pinned_file`, `daemon_config::tests::test_cdf_missing_proc_is_unreadable_not_not_found`, `daemon_config::tests::test_cdf_reopened_handle_must_be_the_pinned_inode`, `daemon_config::tests::test_cdf_reopened_handle_must_stay_regular`, `config_open_diagnostics_contract::test_cdf_reader_opens_with_o_path_then_reopens_through_proc`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/daemon_config_reader_tests.rs` | ✅ Verified |
| CDF2 | `soos-admin test-pam` reports `accepted` (bound and fresh response) and `rejected_reason` (`request_id_mismatch` checked first, then `stale_response`; `null` when accepted) in JSON and as the `Response Accepted:` table line, while `verdict` keeps the raw daemon verdict and no existing field is removed or renamed | `crates/admin-cli/tests/test_pam_acceptance_tests.rs::test_cdf_bound_fresh_response_is_accepted`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/test_pam_acceptance_tests.rs::test_cdf_mismatched_response_is_rejected_with_a_stable_reason`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/test_pam_acceptance_tests.rs::test_cdf_stale_response_is_rejected_and_mismatch_wins`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/test_pam_acceptance_tests.rs::test_cdf_rejection_reasons_are_stable_snake_case` | ✅ Verified |
| CDF3 | `install_v4l_panic_hook_filter()` re-installs the filter on top of a host hook installed after the first guarded call (guarded panics silent again, other panics reported exactly once by every host hook), never wraps the live filter a second time, and `soos-daemon` calls it once right after its own panic hook, before the pipeline opens the camera | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_panic_hook_reinstall_tests.rs::test_cdf_filter_is_reinstalled_on_top_of_a_later_host_hook_without_double_wrapping`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/v4l_panic_hook_filter_tests.rs::test_cag_guarded_panics_are_silent_and_other_panics_reach_the_previous_hook`, `config_open_diagnostics_contract::test_cdf_daemon_reinstalls_the_v4l_hook_filter_after_its_panic_hook`, `camera_alias_guard_contract::test_cag_guard_installs_one_chained_hook_filter` | ✅ Verified |
| CDF4 | The workspace, including the `SYS_setresuid32` branch of `crates/daemon/tests/pcx_wire_routing_tests.rs`, type-checks for `i686-unknown-linux-gnu` (`ORT_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=1 cargo check --locked --workspace --all-targets --all-features`), and the PCX test for `armv7-unknown-linux-gnueabihf`; `timespec` fields are converted with `u64::try_from`, never `cast_unsigned` | `config_open_diagnostics_contract::test_cdf_timespec_fields_are_not_cast_unsigned`, `diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_pcx_root_helper_selects_setresuid32_on_x86` `config_open_diagnostics_contract::test_cdf_ci_type_checks_a_32_bit_target` (CI Clippy job runs the i686 workspace `cargo check` on every PR; the armv7 PCX check stays manual, `Docs/DEVELOPMENT_WORKFLOW.md` §4.2; walkthrough 155 §8 and the review fix section) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `gui-camera-vision-review-fixes` (GitHub #304, #305, #306, #307, #313, #314; review of 2026-10-02, `fix/gui-camera-vision-review`)

GUI guided enrollment samples one face only, the IPC preview keeps the infrared stamp and reports camera failures, virtual V4L2 nodes are never biometric cameras, and the minor vision / camera findings of the same review (ADR 2026-10-02 "Virtual V4L2 Nodes Are Never Biometric Cameras"; walkthrough 168).

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| GCV1 | `VisionAnalysis::face_count` reports every detection (the `process_frame` rule); `analyze_frame` runs PAD, alignment and embedding only for exactly one face; the GUI guided enrollment never samples a frame with another count, breaks the live streak and shows "One face only" (`GuiEnrollmentFeedback::OneFaceOnly`) for several faces (GitHub #304) | `crates/vision/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_analyze_frame_two_faces_skips_pad_and_embedding`, `crates/vision/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_analyze_frame_counts_every_detection_like_process_frame`, `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_two_face_frame_records_no_sample_and_breaks_streak`, `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_gui_feedback_reports_one_face_only`, `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_mock_detector_with_two_faces_never_yields_a_sample` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV2 | A frame stamped `SensorType::Infrared` is previewed as `Grey` (wire format 1, luma of YUYV / NV12 / RGB24 / MJPEG, downscaled as greyscale, empty when truncated); colour sensors keep their format; the GUI decodes a greyscale preview into a Monochrome PAD source (GitHub #305, no protocol bump) | `crates/daemon/tests/preview_ir_sensor_tests.rs::test_gcv_ir_yuyv_preview_is_sent_as_grey_luma`, `crates/daemon/tests/preview_ir_sensor_tests.rs::test_gcv_ir_nv12_and_rgb_previews_are_grey`, `crates/daemon/tests/preview_ir_sensor_tests.rs::test_gcv_wide_ir_frame_is_downscaled_as_grey`, `crates/daemon/tests/preview_ir_sensor_tests.rs::test_gcv_colour_sensor_preview_keeps_its_wire_format`, `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_grey_preview_frame_takes_monochrome_pad_path` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV3 | An empty preview after the first frame withdraws the frame, marks the IPC source not ready and reports `SourceUnavailable` until frames resume; before the first frame it stays `Starting` without an error (GitHub #306) | `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_empty_preview_after_first_frame_marks_source_unavailable`, `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_empty_preview_before_first_frame_is_not_an_error` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV4 | `virtual_node_rejection` refuses memory-to-memory nodes, output-capable nodes and the `v4l2 loopback` / `v4l2loopback` / `vivid` drivers; the enumeration drops them, so a GREY loopback next to a uvc RGB webcam leaves the webcam selected under `PreferIr` (GitHub #307) | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_virtual_node_rejection_rule`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_loopback_grey_node_is_not_enumerated_and_uvc_is_chosen` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV5 | `soos-admin camera list` reports a rejected node with its reason (`rejected_virtual_driver`, `rejected_output_capable`, `rejected_mem_to_mem`) and selects the physical camera; the JSON shape of a node is unchanged | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_diagnostics_report_virtual_node_rejection`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/camera_command_tests.rs::test_cdx_camera_list_json_snapshot` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV6 | The capture supervisor refuses a virtual or output-capable node it opens, explicit `camera_device` included (`CameraError::VirtualDevice`, `UnsupportedDevice`, never streamed), unless `CameraConfig::allow_virtual_device` (`[pipeline] allow_virtual_camera = true`, `false` when absent or mistyped); the decision is an ADR | `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_gcv_explicit_loopback_node_is_refused_without_opt_in`, `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_gcv_output_capable_node_is_refused`, `v4l_impl::supervisor_tests::test_gcv_virtual_node_streams_with_explicit_opt_in`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_allow_virtual_camera_opt_in_is_read_and_defaults_off`, `AI/DECISIONS.md` *(the `soos-daemon` loader and `soos-enroll` read the key since GitHub #318, rows VCO1 and VCO3)* | ✅ Verified |
| GCV7 | The SCRFD channel order is documented truthfully: soos feeds BGR, upstream feeds RGB, the measured difference on 400 LFW images is negligible, and a change needs an owner decision (VIS-NEW-7, docs only) | `Docs/INFERENCE_ORT_CRATE.md` *(doc comment of `letterbox_pad` in the detector source updated too)* | ✅ Verified |
| GCV8 | `OrtEmbeddingExtractor::prepare_input` refuses a zero width or height with `InvalidDimensions` instead of underflowing (VIS-NEW-2) | `crates/inference-ort/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_embedding_prepare_input_rejects_zero_dimensions` | ✅ Verified |

| GCV10 | Every entry of the shipped `models/manifest.toml` declares `input_layout = "NCHW"` (SCRFD and MiniFASNetV2 added; model files and SHA-256s unchanged) (VIS-NEW-4) | `crates/inference-ort/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_shipped_manifest_declares_every_input_layout`, `gcv_review_contract::test_gcv_every_shipped_manifest_entry_declares_input_layout` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV11 | The IR-gate luma, the decoded MJPEG buffer (L8 path included), the PAD softmax exponentials and the probabilities of `OrtPadDetector::evaluate_liveness` are zeroizing (`softmax` keeps its `Vec<f32>` signature) (VIS-NEW-5) | `gcv_review_contract::test_gcv_face_pixel_buffers_are_zeroizing` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV12 | `Debug` of `BiometricEmbedding`, `PipelineOutput`, `VerificationOutcome` and `VisionAnalysis` prints lengths, never pixels or embedding values (VIS-NEW-6) | `crates/inference-ort/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_biometric_embedding_debug_is_redacted`, `crates/vision/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_pipeline_outputs_debug_is_redacted` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV13 | Stale documentation corrected: largest shipped model (SFace 38.7 MB) in `MAX_MODEL_FILE_BYTES`, `detector.rs` module line and startup peak memory (about 43 MB) in the inference doc, NV12 row in the vision colour table (VIS-NEW-8) | `Docs/INFERENCE_ORT_CRATE.md`, `Docs/VISION_CRATE.md` *(plus the `MAX_MODEL_FILE_BYTES` doc comment)* | ✅ Verified |
| GCV14 | A NUL byte in a device path never reaches `v4l`: the shared `daemon.toml` reader ignores such a `camera_device` (mistyped key), every node is opened through `open_device_guarded` (NUL refused, panic guard, guarded close), and the supervisor reports an error instead of dying (CAM-NEW-4) | `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_nul_byte_camera_device_is_refused_at_config_load`, `crates/camera-v4l/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_nul_byte_device_path_never_panics`, `gcv_review_contract::test_gcv_v4l_devices_are_opened_only_through_the_guard` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV15 | `soos-gui --mock` requires `--dev-store` (CAM-NEW-5) | `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_mock_mode_requires_dev_store` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV16 | The GUI preview reply buffer and `LatestFrameData::rgb` / `aligned_crop` are `Zeroizing`; `LatestFrameData`'s `Debug` prints lengths only (CAM-NEW-6) | `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_latest_frame_buffers_are_zeroizing_and_redacted` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV17 | The daemon monitor reads `ActiveState`; `activating` (auto-restart), `deactivating`, `reloading`, `refreshing`, an unknown value and a failed `systemctl` count as running, only `inactive` / `failed` release the device (CAM-NEW-7 a) | `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_transitional_systemd_states_count_as_running` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV18 | `frame_from_preview` refuses unknown wire formats, `255` with data, zero dimensions with data and payloads whose length disagrees with the geometry (`Protocol`); the explicit empty preview is not an error (S2) | `crates/gui/tests/gcv_review_tests.rs::test_gcv_preview_validation_rejects_unknown_format_and_bad_length` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV19 | `soos-gui` runs `pkexec`, `systemctl` and `soos-enroll` (pause, resume, list, delete) by absolute path, and `/usr/bin/soos-enroll` is where `install.sh`, the PKGBUILD and the RPM spec install it (CAM-NEW-7 b) | `gcv_review_contract::test_gcv_gui_privileged_programs_use_absolute_paths` | ✅ Verified |
| GCV20 | `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md` states that `MockCameraManager` is compiled unconditionally (the `mock-camera` feature gates nothing) (S1) | `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `virtual-camera-optin-and-cli-races` (GitHub #318 follow-ups of the 2026-10-02 review batch, `fix/virtual-camera-optin-and-cli-races`)

`soos-daemon` and `soos-enroll` honour `[pipeline] allow_virtual_camera` (fail closed by default), the daemon loader refuses a NUL `camera_device`, `soos-admin gdm restore` re-checks the PAM file right before its rename, and `soos-admin logs --file` reads its tail backwards in bounded chunks. Amends ADR 2026-10-02 "Virtual V4L2 Nodes Are Never Biometric Cameras". Walkthrough 171.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| VCO1 | The `soos-daemon` loader copies `[pipeline] allow_virtual_camera` to `CameraConfig::allow_virtual_device`; absent, `false`, no file and the defaults keep it `false` without a warning; `true` queues one configuration warning (logged at `warn` by `main.rs` at startup) saying a virtual node may be opened; a non-boolean value is a configuration error naming the key | `crates/daemon/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_daemon_allow_virtual_camera_true_allows_virtual_devices`, `crates/daemon/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_daemon_allow_virtual_camera_defaults_false`, `crates/daemon/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_daemon_allow_virtual_camera_mistyped_is_refused` | ✅ Verified |
| VCO2 | A `camera_device` holding a NUL byte is refused at load time (`from_toml_str` and `load_from_path`) with `DaemonError::Config` naming the key and never echoing the value; a clean path still applies | `crates/daemon/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_daemon_rejects_nul_byte_camera_device` | ✅ Verified |
| VCO3 | `soos-enroll` takes the opt-in from the single shared-reader call that resolves the camera (`CameraDeviceChoice::allow_virtual_camera`; `false` when absent, `false`, mistyped (with a note naming the key), unusable or no configuration path) and `build_full_service_with_notes` opens the camera with `enrollment_camera_config(device_path, choice.allow_virtual_camera)` | `crates/enrollment-cli/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_enroll_honours_allow_virtual_camera`, `crates/enrollment-cli/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_enroll_allow_virtual_camera_defaults_false`, `crates/enrollment-cli/tests/virtual_camera_optin_tests.rs::test_vco_enroll_full_service_uses_the_choice_opt_in` | ✅ Verified |
| VCO4 | `gdm restore` snapshots the PAM file (`O_NOFOLLOW`, bounded: device, inode, bytes), compares that snapshot, and right before the rename (temporary file written and synced) re-reads it: other bytes, another inode, or a file that appeared abort the restore with "changed concurrently" and a re-run hint, with or without `--force`, keeping the file, the backup and no temporary file; an unchanged file is restored | `gdm::restore_race_tests::test_vco_gdm_restore_aborts_when_file_changes_before_rename`, `gdm::restore_race_tests::test_vco_gdm_restore_force_still_aborts_on_concurrent_change`, `gdm::restore_race_tests::test_vco_gdm_restore_aborts_when_file_is_replaced_by_same_bytes`, `gdm::restore_race_tests::test_vco_gdm_restore_aborts_when_missing_file_appears`, `gdm::restore_race_tests::test_vco_gdm_restore_without_concurrent_change_restores`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/gdm_restore_stale_backup_tests.rs` | ✅ Verified |
| VCO5 | `logs --file` reads the tail backwards from the `fstat` size in 64 KiB chunks until the requested lines are found: on a 64 MiB log the default tail reads at most 1 MiB and the largest tail about its own region; the output equals the forward reader of GitHub #312 (line cap, lossy UTF-8, empty lines, final line without `\n`) for every size; the regular-file, `O_NONBLOCK`, line-cap and `MAX_LOG_TAIL_LINES` bounds are kept | `logs::tail_tests::test_vco_logs_tail_reads_only_the_tail_region`, `logs::tail_tests::test_vco_logs_backward_tail_matches_the_forward_reader`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/logs_bounded_file_tests.rs`, `crates/admin-cli/tests/logs_tests.rs` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `evidence-sweep-fd-listing` (GitHub #293, evidence sweep and `v4l` teardown hardening items, `fix/p3fu4-evidence-sweep-fd`)

The evidence temporary-file sweep lists, examines and unlinks each date partition through one descriptor, and the `v4l` double teardown panic is documented as a known upstream limitation (ADR 2026-10-01 "Evidence Sweep Lists Partitions Through Their Descriptor; v4l Double Teardown Panic Left Upstream"). Walkthrough 157.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| ESL1 | After the base-directory lock and identity check, `EvidenceStore::sweep_orphaned_temp_files` resolves no path: the base directory is listed through its locked descriptor, each partition is opened relative to it (`O_DIRECTORY \| O_NOFOLLOW`) and listed, examined, unlinked from and synced through that one descriptor; a partition renamed and replaced by another directory with a matching temporary name after the open leaves the replacement untouched and removes the orphan of the opened directory; every SGU5 sweep test passes unchanged | `store::temp_sweep_fd_tests::test_esl_swapped_partition_is_listed_through_the_opened_descriptor`, `store::temp_sweep_fd_tests::test_esl_unswapped_partition_is_still_swept`, `crates/evidence-store/tests/temp_sweep_tests.rs` | ✅ Verified |
| ESL2 | The `v4l` stream drop panic followed by an arena drop panic is documented as a known upstream limitation with its exact condition (`VIDIOC_STREAMOFF` then `munmap` / `VIDIOC_REQBUFS(0)` failing with an error other than `ENODEV`) and its effect (process abort, fail-closed `PAM_IGNORE`, systemd restart), without forking or patching `v4l` | `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md`, `AI/DECISIONS.md` (ADR 2026-10-01 "Evidence Sweep Lists Partitions Through Their Descriptor; v4l Double Teardown Panic Left Upstream") | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `storage-migration-and-rustup-followups` (GitHub #287, Storage and Install follow-ups)

Operator-run migration of legacy v1 storage envelopes to the AAD-bound v2 format, and a rustup bootstrap that never edits shell profiles (ADR 2026-10-01 "Operator-Run Migration of Legacy v1 Storage Envelopes"; ADR 2026-10-01 "rustup Bootstrap Leaves Shell Profiles Untouched").

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| SMI1 | `BiometricStore::migrate_legacy_templates` re-encrypts every legacy template through `enroll` (content preserved, mode `0600`, no temporary file left), never rewrites a bound template, and `migrate_template` reports `Missing` / `AlreadyCurrent` / `WouldMigrate` / `Migrated` | `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_bulk_migration_rewrites_only_legacy_templates`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_migrated_file_is_mode_0600_and_leaves_no_temporary_file`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_migrate_template_reports_each_outcome` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI2 | The migration is idempotent (a second run migrates nothing) and a dry run reports the legacy files without writing, for templates and evidence | `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_bulk_migration_is_idempotent`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_dry_run_reports_without_writing`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_is_idempotent_and_dry_run_writes_nothing` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI3 | A per-file failure (foreign UID, junk, renamed legacy snapshot, symlink) is reported with a reason, never aborts the other files, leaves the file byte-for-byte untouched, never follows a symlink, and reports carry no embedding value | `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_failure_of_one_file_does_not_abort_or_corrupt`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_symlinked_template_is_reported_and_never_followed`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_failure_reasons_never_contain_embedding_values`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_failure_does_not_abort_or_corrupt`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_never_follows_symlinks` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI4 | `EvidenceStore::migrate_legacy_snapshots` re-encrypts legacy snapshots in place (record unchanged, mode `0600`, no temporary file, daily counters untouched), creates nothing for a missing store, refuses a symlinked base directory; `MasterKey::load_existing` never creates a key and refuses symlinks | `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_rewrites_only_legacy_files`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_writes_0600_and_leaves_no_temporary_file`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_of_missing_store_is_empty_and_symlinked_root_refused`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_load_existing_key_never_creates_a_key` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI5 | `soos-enroll migrate` migrates templates and evidence then reports 0 migrated on the second run, `--dry-run` writes nothing, per-file failures are listed while the rest is migrated, and a missing evidence key skips evidence without creating a key | `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_migrates_templates_and_evidence_then_reports_zero`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_dry_run_changes_nothing`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_reports_per_file_failure_and_continues`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_without_evidence_key_skips_evidence_and_creates_nothing`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_opens_existing_evidence_key` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI6 | `migrate` is root-only (service and binary), its `--format json` summary is valid `serde_json` output without embedding values, and its flags parse (`--dry-run`, `--format`, `--evidence-dir`, `--evidence-key-file`) | `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_migrate_requires_root`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_binary_refuses_migrate_without_root`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_json_summary_is_valid_and_carries_no_embedding`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_parses_migrate_flags` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI7 | `scripts/install_rustup.sh` runs the verified `rustup-init` with `--no-modify-path` | `rustup_path_contract::test_rustup_installer_passes_no_modify_path` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI8 | A hermetic install (stub `curl` / `sha256sum` / `uname`, scratch HOME under `target/`) passes `--no-modify-path`, leaves every shell profile byte-for-byte unchanged, runs the installed `rustc` through the exported PATH and prints the `export PATH=...` line, with and without `CARGO_HOME` | `rustup_path_contract::test_rustup_installer_leaves_profiles_untouched_and_prints_path` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI10 | `soos-enroll migrate` never creates the master key or the biometrics directory: a missing key or directory reports templates as skipped (no failure), an existing key and directory are migrated; `biometric_store::MasterKey::load_existing` never creates a key or a parent directory and refuses symlinks | `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_without_master_key_skips_templates_and_creates_nothing`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_without_biometrics_dir_skips_templates_and_creates_nothing`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_opens_existing_template_store_and_migrates`, `migrate_tests::test_smi_cli_run_migration_requires_root`, `bulk_migration_tests::test_smi_load_existing_master_key_never_creates_a_key` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI11 | A migrated legacy snapshot's superseded inode is overwritten in place (same length, different bytes, observed through a hard link); a dry run never overwrites it | `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_migration_overwrites_legacy_ciphertext_best_effort`, `legacy_migration_tests::test_smi_snapshot_dry_run_never_overwrites_legacy_ciphertext` | ✅ Verified |
| SMI9 | The manual digest bump procedure of the pinned `rustup-init` digests and the PATH behaviour are documented in the script header and `Docs/CI_CD_AND_SECURITY.md` | `rustup_path_contract::test_rustup_digest_bump_procedure_is_documented` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `migration-lock-and-shutdown-bound` (GitHub #289, storage and daemon follow-ups of #287, `fix/p3fu2-storage-daemon`)

The biometric store serializes its writers on an advisory directory lock and a migration never rewrites a template that changed after it was read (ADR 2026-10-01 "Biometric Store Lock and Migration Identity Check"); `soos-enroll migrate` never creates the biometrics directory; `soos-daemon` process exit is bounded by the shutdown drain budget (ADR 2026-10-01 "Daemon Exit Bounded by the Shutdown Drain Budget"). Walkthrough 153.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| MLS1 | A `delete` or a re-enrollment of the same UID racing a real migration waits for the store lock: the deleted template is never resurrected and the newer enrollment is never overwritten | `concurrency_tests::test_mls_concurrent_delete_during_migrate_does_not_resurrect`, `concurrency_tests::test_mls_concurrent_enroll_during_migrate_is_not_overwritten` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `store-lock-ui-and-import-followups` (GitHub #291, storage and GUI follow-ups of #289, `fix/p3fu3-storage-gui`)

The GUI never waits for the template store lock on its UI thread; `soos-enroll import` without `--yes` checks and writes under one store lock; the `daemon.toml` notes `soos-enroll` prints come from the read it applies; `soos-daemon` sweeps the stores' orphaned temporary files at startup (ADR 2026-10-01 "Bounded Startup Sweep of Orphaned Store Temporary Files"); the store lock requires a local filesystem (`Docs/BIOMETRIC_STORE_CRATE.md`). Walkthrough 154.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| SGU1 | In direct (non-Polkit) mode `soos-gui` submits template saves and deletions to `StoreTaskRunner`: `submit` returns without waiting for a store lock held by another process, the outcome arrives through `poll`, the UI is woken up, one store task runs at a time, and `app.rs` calls neither `store.enroll` nor `store.delete` | `store_task_tests::test_sgu_enroll_submit_never_blocks_on_a_held_store_lock`, `store_task_tests::test_sgu_delete_submit_never_blocks_and_keeps_the_template_when_busy`, `store_task_tests::test_sgu_only_one_store_task_runs_at_a_time`, `store_task_tests::test_sgu_app_never_mutates_the_store_on_the_ui_thread` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU2 | A store lock timeout is reported as `StoreTaskError::Busy` with the message "another soos operation is using the template store; try again" and changes nothing; other store errors keep their message; a task's `Debug` output never prints embedding values | `store_task_tests::test_sgu_store_task_errors_map_lock_timeout_to_the_busy_message`, `store_task_tests::test_sgu_enroll_submit_never_blocks_on_a_held_store_lock`, `store_task_tests::test_sgu_store_task_debug_never_prints_embedding_values` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU3 | `BiometricStore::enroll_if_absent` refuses an enrolled UID with `AlreadyEnrolled` (UID only in the message) and leaves it byte for byte, checks under the store lock so a template that appears while it waits is never replaced, and has exactly one winner among concurrent callers; `soos-enroll import` without `--yes` writes through it, `--yes` still replaces | `enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_enroll_if_absent_writes_a_missing_template`, `enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_enroll_if_absent_refuses_an_enrolled_uid_and_keeps_it_byte_for_byte`, `enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_enroll_if_absent_checks_under_the_lock_against_a_concurrent_enroll`, `enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_concurrent_enroll_if_absent_has_exactly_one_winner`, `enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_already_enrolled_error_names_the_uid_only`, `import_enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_import_without_yes_never_replaces_a_concurrent_enrollment`, `import_enroll_if_absent_tests::test_sgu_import_with_yes_still_replaces_and_reports_it` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU4 | `BiometricStore::sweep_orphaned_temp_files` removes only `<uid>.tmp.<pid>.<suffix>` names with canonical numbers that are regular single-link files older than `TEMP_SWEEP_MIN_AGE`; templates, every decoy name, recent and future-dated files, symlinks (and their targets), directories, FIFOs and hard links survive; at most `MAX_TEMP_SWEEP_REMOVALS` per call; nothing is examined while the store lock is held (never waited for) | `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_removes_old_orphaned_template_temp_files`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_keeps_every_decoy_and_the_templates`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_keeps_recent_and_future_dated_temp_files`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_never_follows_or_removes_non_regular_entries`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_is_capped_per_call`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_touches_nothing_while_the_store_lock_is_held`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_sweep_minimum_age_and_cap_are_bounded` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU5 | `EvidenceStore::sweep_orphaned_temp_files` removes only the snapshot, migration and daily-counter temporary names inside real `YYYY-MM-DD` partitions; snapshots, counters, decoys, the base level, misnamed and symlinked partitions, recent, symlinked, directory and hard-linked entries survive; capped per call; skipped while the base-directory lock is held; a disabled or missing store is untouched and a symlinked base directory is refused | `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_removes_the_three_orphaned_temp_kinds`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_keeps_every_decoy_snapshots_and_counters`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_only_looks_inside_date_partitions`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_keeps_recent_non_regular_and_linked_entries`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_is_capped_per_call`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_touches_nothing_while_the_store_is_locked`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_of_a_disabled_or_missing_store_touches_nothing`, `temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_evidence_sweep_refuses_a_symlinked_base_directory` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU6 | `build_full_service_with_notes` resolves the camera once and reports that read's notes exactly once, before the device is validated: the applied device and the printed notes come from the same file; `soos-enroll` `main.rs` no longer reads `daemon.toml` separately through `camera_config_notes` | `full_service_config_notes_tests::test_sgu_full_service_reports_the_notes_of_the_applied_config`, `full_service_config_notes_tests::test_sgu_full_service_reports_empty_notes_for_a_clean_config`, `full_service_config_notes_tests::test_sgu_full_service_notes_a_missing_config_once`, `full_service_config_notes_tests::test_sgu_enroll_main_prints_the_notes_of_the_build_read` | ✅ Verified |
| SGU7 | `soos-daemon` sweeps both stores once at startup, right after opening them in `initialize_pipeline`, through `pipeline::sweep_orphaned_store_temp_files`; a failing sweep of one store does not prevent the other nor the startup | `store_temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_daemon_startup_sweep_cleans_both_stores_and_keeps_decoys`, `store_temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_daemon_startup_sweep_failure_is_not_fatal`, `store_temp_sweep_tests::test_sgu_initialize_pipeline_sweeps_once_after_opening_both_stores` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `storage-key-race-evidence-and-cli` (GitHub #303, #310, #312, review of 2026-10-02, `fix/storage-keys-evidence-cli`, walkthrough 167)

Key creation never replaces a published key; the `PasswordFailed` evidence write never runs on a Tokio worker and the evidence base-directory lock is waited for with a bound; the storage and CLI minor findings STO-NEW-2..10 are closed.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| SKE1 | `MasterKey::load_or_create` (biometric and evidence) publishes a new key with `link(2)` from an exclusive `0600` temporary file: 16 threads racing behind a `Barrier` on a missing path (25 rounds) all return the key that is on disk, and no temporary key file is left (GitHub #303) | `crates/biometric-store/tests/key_publication_race_tests.rs`, `crates/evidence-store/tests/key_publication_race_tests.rs`, `key_publication_race_tests::test_303_concurrent_load_or_create_returns_the_published_key`, `key_publication_race_tests::test_303_creation_leaves_no_temporary_file` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE2 | With the evidence base-directory `flock` held, a `PasswordFailed` event on a single-worker runtime: the event handler returns and a `Status` request on another connection is answered, both within `connection_timeout` (wall clock); the write is tracked in `ConnectionDispatcher::evidence_writes` (GitHub #310) | `password_failed_evidence_offload_tests::test_310_password_failed_evidence_write_never_blocks_a_worker` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE3 | The shutdown drain stays bounded: the blocked `PasswordFailed` write is abandoned by `BlockingTasks::drain` within its budget (`aborted == 1`); existing `PasswordFailed` snapshot tests wait for the tracked write before inspecting the store | `password_failed_evidence_offload_tests::test_310_password_failed_evidence_write_never_blocks_a_worker`, `pipeline_integration_tests::test_12_4_password_failed_event_captures_evidence_snapshot`, `peer_limits_tests::test_password_failed_events_are_rate_limited_per_peer_uid` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE4 | `rotate_retention` takes the base-directory lock through `O_DIRECTORY \| O_NOFOLLOW`, polls a contended lock for at most the store's lock timeout (`EVIDENCE_LOCK_TIMEOUT` = 5 s, `with_lock_timeout`) and then fails with `EvidenceStoreError::LockTimeout`; it succeeds once the lock is released and still refuses a symlinked base directory | `bounded_lock_tests::test_310_rotate_retention_times_out_on_a_held_lock`, `bounded_lock_tests::test_310_rotate_retention_succeeds_once_the_lock_is_released`, `bounded_lock_tests::test_310_rotate_retention_refuses_a_symlinked_base_directory`, `bounded_lock_tests::test_310_default_evidence_lock_timeout_is_bounded` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE5 | `migrate_legacy_snapshots` (dry run and real) uses the same bounded lock and fails with `LockTimeout` while the lock is held (STO-NEW-10) | `bounded_lock_tests::test_312_migrate_legacy_snapshots_times_out_on_a_held_lock` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE6 | `soos-enroll enroll` without `--yes` on a user not enrolled at the start of the capture writes through `enroll_if_absent`: a template created while the prompt is open is kept and the command fails with `AlreadyEnrolled`; a fresh enrollment and a confirmed replacement of a known enrollment still work (STO-NEW-2) | `enroll_concurrent_template_tests::test_312_enroll_without_yes_never_replaces_a_template_created_meanwhile`, `enroll_concurrent_template_tests::test_312_enroll_without_yes_on_a_fresh_user_still_enrolls`, `enroll_concurrent_template_tests::test_312_confirmed_replacement_of_a_known_enrollment_still_replaces` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE7 | `soos-daemon` loads or creates the evidence key only when `[pipeline.evidence] enabled` is set: disabled, neither the key nor its directory is created and an invalid key file is ignored; enabled, the key is created (STO-NEW-5) | `evidence_key_opt_in_tests::test_312_disabled_evidence_never_creates_the_evidence_key`, `evidence_key_opt_in_tests::test_312_disabled_evidence_ignores_an_invalid_evidence_key`, `evidence_key_opt_in_tests::test_312_enabled_evidence_still_creates_the_evidence_key` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE8 | A template read opens `O_NOFOLLOW \| O_NONBLOCK` and requires a regular file: a FIFO at the template path fails closed without blocking, a directory is refused (STO-NEW-6) | `template_fifo_tests::test_312_template_read_does_not_block_on_a_fifo`, `template_fifo_tests::test_312_template_directory_at_template_path_is_refused` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE9 | `soos-admin test-pam` exits `0` only for an accepted `Allow` (`PamTestReport::exit_code`): an `Allow` bound to another nonce and a `Deny` exit `1` (STO-NEW-3) | `test_pam_exit_deadline_tests::test_312_test_pam_exits_1_on_an_allow_bound_to_another_nonce`, `test_pam_exit_deadline_tests::test_312_test_pam_exits_0_only_on_an_accepted_allow` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE10 | `test-pam` applies one cumulative deadline started before a non-blocking `connect`, re-armed before every read and write: a response trickled over more than `timeout_ms` and a silent daemon are `AdminCliError::Timeout` within `timeout_ms` plus scheduling slack; a response completed late is `deadline_exceeded`, checked after the nonce and before freshness (STO-NEW-4) | `test_pam_exit_deadline_tests::test_312_test_pam_deadline_is_cumulative_across_reads`, `test_pam_exit_deadline_tests::test_312_test_pam_silent_daemon_times_out_within_the_deadline`, `test_pam_exit_deadline_tests::test_312_deadline_exceeded_rejection_has_a_stable_name`, `test_pam::tests::test_312_rejection_order_matches_pam_module` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE11 | `soos-admin gdm restore` refuses a backup that differs from the current PAM file without its soos rules (file and backup kept, the error names `--force`); `--force` restores it; an unchanged enable still restores (STO-NEW-7) | `gdm_restore_stale_backup_tests::test_312_gdm_restore_refuses_a_stale_backup`, `gdm_restore_stale_backup_tests::test_312_gdm_restore_force_restores_a_stale_backup`, `gdm_restore_stale_backup_tests::test_312_gdm_restore_of_an_unchanged_enable_still_succeeds`, `gdm_restore_stale_backup_tests::test_312_gdm_restore_force_flag_parses` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE12 | `soos-admin logs --file` opens `O_NONBLOCK` and requires a regular file (a FIFO is refused without blocking), keeps the tail in a ring buffer of at most `MAX_LOG_TAIL_LINES` lines (`-n 0` clamped), bounds each line to `MAX_LOG_LINE_BYTES` and tolerates invalid UTF-8 (STO-NEW-8) | `logs_bounded_file_tests::test_312_logs_file_refuses_a_fifo_without_blocking`, `logs_bounded_file_tests::test_312_logs_file_refuses_a_directory`, `logs_bounded_file_tests::test_312_logs_file_tail_is_bounded_by_the_ring_buffer`, `logs_bounded_file_tests::test_312_logs_file_bounds_each_line_and_tolerates_invalid_utf8` | ✅ Verified |
| SKE13 | Guided enrollment samples are `Zeroizing` buffers, the session `Debug` output prints only per-step counts, and `compute_composite_embedding` returns `Zeroizing<Vec<f32>>` (STO-NEW-9) | `guided_enrollment_zeroize_tests::test_312_guided_session_debug_never_prints_samples`, `guided_enrollment_zeroize_tests::test_312_guided_composite_is_returned_zeroizing` | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `mock-daemon-flake-and-store-poll-test` (GitHub #293 "Tests" items, `test/p3fu4-flaky-and-gui-poll`)

The Docker mock daemon is race-free for callers that poll for its socket path, its `expired` window closes long before any request a harness can build, and `StoreTaskRunner::poll` is tested through its lost-outcome branch. No existing assertion changed. Walkthrough 156.

| ID | Criterion | Evidence | Status |
|----|-----------|----------|--------|
| FGP1 | `tests/docker/mock_daemon.py` binds, sets the mode and group of, and listens on its socket under a private staging name and only then renames it to `--socket`, so a caller that connects as soon as the path exists is never refused (`ECONNREFUSED`); the socket path is never bound directly | Invariant test (`distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_publishes_its_socket_only_once_listening`); stress loop under 16 busy loops on 16 CPUs, 4 runs in parallel: `distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_malformed_modes_put_one_defect_on_the_wire` failed 6 times in 600 runs before (5 `ConnectionRefused`, 1 `expired` window) and 0 times in 600 runs after; the full `soos-invariants` binary failed 2 of 40 stressed runs before and 0 of 40 after (walkthrough 156 §8) | ✅ Verified |
| FGP2 | The `expired` mode closes its window `EXPIRED_AGE_NS` = 60 s before the send-time clock read, above the 10 s socket wait plus the 10 s read timeout of the invariant harnesses, so `expires < before` holds under load; the one-defect and stamp assertions of `distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_malformed_modes_put_one_defect_on_the_wire` and `pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_mock_daemon_stamps_from_the_monotonic_clock` are unchanged | Invariant tests (`distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_publishes_its_socket_only_once_listening`, `distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_malformed_modes_put_one_defect_on_the_wire`, `pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_mock_daemon_stamps_from_the_monotonic_clock`) | ✅ Verified |
| FGP3 | `StoreTaskRunner::poll` turns a finished worker that delivered no outcome into exactly one failed outcome of the submitted kind and UID (`StoreTaskError::Failed` with the lost-worker message), the runner stays busy until then and is free afterwards, and the failure is reported once | Unit tests (`worker_failure_tests::test_fgp_poll_reports_a_lost_delete_worker_as_one_failure`, `worker_failure_tests::test_fgp_poll_reports_a_lost_enroll_worker_as_one_failure`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `pam-response-expiry-protocol-followups` (GitHub #287 "Protocol / PAM" items and owner decisions of 2026-10-01, `fix/p3fu-pam-expiry`)

The PAM client enforces the daemon `Response` expiry against CLOCK_MONOTONIC, the clock `soos-daemon` stamps with (ADR 2026-10-01 "PAM Client Enforces Response Expiry"); untagged legacy client frames stay accepted (ADR 2026-10-01 "Untagged Legacy Client Frames Stay Accepted"); rows backed by the QFX5 Docker evidence are closed; `pcx_wire_routing_tests` is root-safe. Walkthrough 148.

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PRE1 | `Response::check_freshness(now, skew)` returns the first violated rule: `ClockUnavailable` (`now == 0`), `Unstamped` (a zero stamp), `Inverted` (`expires < issued`), `FutureDated` (`issued > now + skew`, saturating), `Expired` (`now >= expires`, the expiry instant included); it ignores the verdict and its error text renders no value | Contract tests (`response_freshness_tests::test_pre_fresh_response_is_accepted`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_response_is_expired_at_its_expiry_instant`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_long_expired_response_is_rejected`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_unstamped_response_is_rejected`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_inverted_stamps_are_rejected`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_future_dated_issue_beyond_skew_is_rejected`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_client_clock_failure_is_rejected`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_freshness_saturates_near_u64_max`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_freshness_is_independent_of_the_verdict`, `response_freshness_tests::test_pre_freshness_error_display_is_value_free`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE2 | For arbitrary `issued`, `expires`, `now` and skew the predicate never panics and accepts exactly the consistent, unexpired, not future-dated windows | Property test (`response_freshness_tests::prop_pre_freshness_accepts_only_consistent_unexpired_windows`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE3 | An `Allow` whose window is closed, unstamped (`0 / 0`) or inverted returns `PAM_IGNORE` from `pam_sm_authenticate` and `IpcError::StaleResponse` from the IPC client, never `PAM_SUCCESS` | Integration tests (`response_expiry_tests::test_pre_expired_allow_response_returns_ignore`, `response_expiry_tests::test_pre_expired_allow_response_is_rejected_by_the_ipc_client`, `response_expiry_tests::test_pre_unstamped_allow_response_returns_ignore`, `response_expiry_tests::test_pre_inverted_allow_response_returns_ignore`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE4 | An `Allow` issued further ahead of the client clock than `MAX_RESPONSE_FUTURE_SKEW_NS` (10 ms, `crates/protocol/src/types.rs`, re-exported by `crates/pam/src/ipc.rs`) returns `PAM_IGNORE` | Integration tests (`response_expiry_tests::test_pre_future_dated_allow_response_returns_ignore`, `response_expiry_tests::test_pre_future_skew_bound_is_ten_milliseconds`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE5 | A response stamped like `soos-daemon` (`issued = now`, `expires = issued + 2 s`, CLOCK_MONOTONIC) still authenticates, so the rejections are caused by the stamps alone; the daemon stamps from CLOCK_MONOTONIC with a 2 s window | Integration test (`response_expiry_tests::test_pre_fresh_allow_response_returns_success`); pre-existing daemon tests (`response_timestamp_tests::test_response_validity_window_is_two_seconds`, `response_timestamp_tests::test_wire_validation_rejection_is_stamped`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE6 | `tests/docker/mock_daemon.py` stamps from CLOCK_MONOTONIC at send time by default (`--stamps monotonic`, `expires = issued + 2 s`), `--mode expired` sends a closed window and `--stamps zero` the unstamped 0/0 form; every PAM matrix, distro and physical script passes `--stamps monotonic` explicitly, T15 covers the expired and the unstamped `Allow`, and every malformed mode still carries exactly one defect (valid stamps on every non-expired mode) | Invariant tests (`pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_mock_daemon_stamps_from_the_monotonic_clock`, `pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_every_mock_invocation_requests_monotonic_stamps`, `distro_matrix::test_mock_daemon_malformed_modes_put_one_defect_on_the_wire` *(migrated with owner approval 2026-10-01: decodes the varint stamps instead of pinning 41-byte frames)*, `distro_matrix::test_pam_matrix_exercises_every_mock_daemon_mode`); in-container PAM matrix run 2026-10-01 (walkthrough 148 §8) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE7 | No PAM or `soos-admin test-pam` test fixture sends a literal response stamp; the fixtures stamp from CLOCK_MONOTONIC through `crates/pam/tests/common/stamps.rs` (setup-only migration, no assertion changed) | Invariant test (`pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_pam_fixtures_never_send_literal_response_stamps`) | ✅ Verified |
| PRE8 | The expiry enforcement and the owner decision to keep accepting untagged legacy client frames are recorded (ADRs, `Docs/IPC_PROTOCOL.md`, `Docs/PAM_MODULE.md`) and the legacy-frame contract test is unchanged | Invariant tests (`pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_expiry_enforcement_is_documented`, `pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_legacy_untagged_frames_decision_is_recorded`); pre-existing `wire_discriminator_tests::test_204_legacy_untagged_status_request_still_served` | ✅ Verified |
| PRE9 | DDS6, QFU3, PK7 and DV3 cite the green QFX5 run and are verified (PCZ6 was already superseded, RFX6); `Docs/IPC_PROTOCOL.md` describes `StatusResponse` once and the PCZ6 supersession is stated once | Invariant tests (`pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_rows_backed_by_qfx5_cite_the_green_run`, `pam_response_expiry_contract::test_pre_duplicated_protocol_blocks_are_merged`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `diagnostics-parity-and-config-reader` (GitHub #289 "PAM / diagnostics" items and the `daemon.toml` reader follow-ups, `fix/p3fu2-diagnostics`)

`soos-admin test-pam` and `soos-gui` interpret a daemon `Response` with the predicates `pam_soos.so` uses, and one bounded, open-then-`fstat` `daemon.toml` camera reader serves `soos-admin`, `soos-enroll` and `soos-gui` (ADR 2026-10-01 "One Shared `daemon.toml` Camera Reader; Diagnostics Parity With `pam_soos.so`"). Walkthrough 151.

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| DGP1 | `soos-admin test-pam` binds the response to its nonce with `Response::matches_request`, before the freshness check like `pam_soos.so`: a fresh `Allow` or `Deny` whose `request_id` differs (one bit, or all zero) is reported as `PAM_IGNORE (response request_id does not match the request nonce; ...)` with the daemon verdict still shown, the nonce is never printed (table or JSON), and the echoed nonce stays `PAM_SUCCESS` | Integration tests (`test_pam_request_id_tests::test_dgp_test_pam_reports_request_id_mismatch_as_pam_ignore`, `test_pam_request_id_tests::test_dgp_test_pam_reports_zero_request_id_as_pam_ignore`, `test_pam_request_id_tests::test_dgp_test_pam_matching_request_id_is_pam_success`); invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_test_pam_binds_the_response_to_its_nonce`) | ✅ Verified |
| DGP2 | `soos-gui` applies `Response::check_freshness` with the shared `MAX_RESPONSE_FUTURE_SKEW_NS` and CLOCK_MONOTONIC to the only daemon `Response` it consumes (the `PreviewFrame` refusal): an expired, unstamped or future-dated refusal is `IpcPreviewError::Protocol`; fresh refusals keep `Unauthorized` / `RateLimited` / `Unavailable`; the GUI fake daemons stamp from CLOCK_MONOTONIC (`crates/gui/tests/common/stamps.rs`, setup-only migration) | Integration tests (`ipc_preview_freshness_tests::test_dgp_gui_expired_refusal_is_a_protocol_error`, `ipc_preview_freshness_tests::test_dgp_gui_unstamped_or_future_refusal_is_a_protocol_error`, `ipc_preview_freshness_tests::test_dgp_gui_fresh_refusals_keep_their_meaning`); invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_gui_applies_the_shared_freshness_rule`); pre-existing `ipc_camera_tests::*` and `ipc_camera_failure_tests::*` unchanged in their assertions | ✅ Verified |
| DGP3 | A stale `Response` is never shown as success by `soos-gui`: an expired (or fresh) `Allow` echoing the nonce is a protocol error, never an authorized preview | Integration test (`ipc_preview_freshness_tests::test_dgp_gui_stale_allow_is_never_success`) | ✅ Verified |
| DGP4 | One `daemon.toml` camera reader (`soos_camera_v4l::daemon_config::read_daemon_camera_config`, `DEFAULT_DAEMON_CONFIG_PATH`, `MAX_DAEMON_CONFIG_BYTES` = 1 MiB): `soos_admin_cli::daemon_config` re-exports its types, `soos-enroll` and `soos-gui` resolve through `resolve_camera_device_from_config_reported`, and no client parses the file itself | Integration tests (`daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_shared_reader_reads_both_camera_keys`, `camera_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_admin_daemon_config_is_the_shared_reader`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_valid_config_has_no_notes`); invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_single_daemon_config_reader`); pre-existing CVF1–CVF3, `camera_resolver_parity_tests::*`, `device_resolution_tests::*` and `import_tests::test_resolve_camera_device_from_daemon_config` unchanged | ✅ Verified |
| DGP5 | A single key of the wrong type falls back to its own default and is named (never quoted) in a warning while the other key still applies, for the shared reader and for `soos-enroll` / `soos-gui`; an unrecognized `sensor_preference` string is warned without its value. `soos-admin camera list` mirrors `soos-daemon` (which refuses to start) and reports a mistyped key as `Malformed` (CVF3) | Integration tests (`daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_wrongly_typed_camera_device_falls_back_alone`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_wrongly_typed_sensor_preference_falls_back_alone`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_unrecognized_preference_is_warned_without_its_value`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_wrongly_typed_device_keeps_the_preference`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_wrongly_typed_preference_keeps_the_device`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_camera_config_notes_match_the_resolver`); pre-existing `camera_config_tests::test_cvf_missing_or_invalid_daemon_config_falls_back_with_note` | ✅ Verified |
| DGP6 | A missing (dangling symbolic link included), unreadable, oversized (bounded read, exactly 1 MiB still accepted), malformed (TOML, UTF-8, `[pipeline]` not a table) or non-regular file is a `DaemonConfigError`; `soos-enroll` / `soos-gui` then use the soos-daemon defaults with one note naming the reason (an oversized file is no longer read whole and applied); the configuration path in every note is sanitized like the `soos-admin` note (`sanitize_display_text`) | Integration tests (`daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_file_level_failures_are_errors`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_unreadable_file_is_reported`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_dangling_symbolic_link_is_not_found`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_unusable_config_falls_back_with_note`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_notes_sanitize_the_config_path`) | ✅ Verified |
| DGP7 | The reader opens first (`O_NONBLOCK`, `O_CLOEXEC`), then checks the handle (`fstat`, regular file) and reads with the bound, with no path-based check before the open: a FIFO in place of the file, or a symbolic link to a FIFO or a device node, returns within 2 s as `NotARegularFile` (admin note and enrollment default included); a symbolic link to a regular file is followed exactly like `soos-daemon` follows it, so all clients read the file the daemon reads | Integration tests (`daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_fifo_in_place_of_the_file_returns_promptly`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_symbolic_link_is_followed_like_the_daemon`, `daemon_config_reader_tests::test_dgp_symbolic_link_to_a_fifo_or_device_returns_promptly`, `camera_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_camera_list_fifo_config_falls_back_with_note`, `camera_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_camera_list_follows_a_symlinked_config_like_the_daemon`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_fifo_config_returns_promptly`, `daemon_config_shared_reader_tests::test_dgp_enroll_follows_symlinked_config_like_the_daemon`); invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_reader_opens_first_then_checks_the_handle`) | ✅ Verified |
| DGP8 | The per-thread root helper of `pcx_wire_routing_tests` issues `SYS_setresuid32` on 32-bit x86 (and 32-bit ARM) and `SYS_setresuid` elsewhere (setup only, no assertion changed) | Invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_pcx_root_helper_selects_setresuid32_on_x86`); `pcx_wire_routing_tests::test_pcx_tagged_ambiguous_request_with_foreign_uid_hint_gets_a_response` green on `x86_64` (no `i686` toolchain was available to build the x86 branch) | ✅ Verified |
| DGP9 | The decision (reader placement, open-then-check, symbolic links followed, per-key rule and the admin mirror of the daemon, `test-pam` and GUI parity) is recorded in `AI/DECISIONS.md` and `Docs/CAMERA_V4L_CRATE.md` | Invariant test (`diagnostics_parity_contract::test_dgp_shared_reader_decision_is_documented`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `presence-auto-unlock` (GitHub #323, `feat/presence-auto-unlock`, walkthrough 175)

`soos-daemon` unlocks a locked local session through systemd-logind (`UnlockSession`) when the unchanged face pipeline verifies the session owner, without a keypress and without PAM; enabled by default, global per-UID rate limit 40 / 60 s for every face request, `pam_faillock` and account / password expiry respected (ADR 2026-10-02 "Presence Auto-Unlock Through logind", ARCHITECTURE invariant 6, `Docs/DAEMON.md` §6, `Docs/DISTRIBUTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` §5.5). Contract: `AI/tester_contract_presence_unlock.md` (210 tests).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PAU1 | `[presence]` defaults `enabled = true`, `scan_interval_ms = 2000`, `lock_grace_ms = 3000`; absent file, empty file and empty table give the same `PresenceConfig`; `0`, `999`, `60001` are startup errors naming the key (also with `enabled = false`); `1000` and `60000` accepted; rate-limit interplay warnings | Integration tests (`presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_defaults_and_bounds_match_spec`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_absent_empty_file_and_empty_table_give_the_same_presence_config`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_keys_are_parsed`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_out_of_range_presence_intervals_are_startup_errors`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_zero_interval_is_rejected_even_when_disabled`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_interval_bounds_are_accepted`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_wrong_type_is_a_startup_error`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_validate_names_the_key_and_is_called_by_daemon_validate`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_warns_when_max_attempts_leaves_no_room_for_scans`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_warns_when_scans_will_be_throttled`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_defaults_and_disabled_feature_do_not_warn`, `presence_config_tests::test_pau_presence_rate_limit_warning_is_not_an_error`) | ✅ Verified |
