## Component: `presence-review-followups` (GitHub #325, review follow-ups of PR #324, `fix/presence-review-followups`, walkthrough 176)

Follow-ups of the presence auto-unlock review: fresh attempt stamp, reachable connect bound, `/etc/pam.conf` and a superset PAM line scan, one account check in flight, no logind traffic without templates, and the diagnosis of a load-only admin-cli flake (ADR 2026-10-02 "Presence Auto-Unlock Through logind", amendment #325; spec `AI/architect_spec_presence_followups.md` rev 3).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| PFU1 | The rate-limit attempt of a presence scan is stamped with a clock read taken after the policy write lock is acquired and right before `record_attempt_with_reserve`; the same value marks the scan start; a clock error or a reading older than the step-3 one skips the tick with `ClockUnavailable` (no attempt, no camera wake, no inference, no unlock) | Integration tests (`presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_attempt_is_stamped_with_a_fresh_clock_read`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_reserve_is_evaluated_at_the_fresh_timestamp`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_clock_failure_before_the_attempt_skips_without_attempt_or_scan`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_clock_regression_before_the_attempt_skips_the_tick`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_attempt_is_stamped_after_the_policy_lock_is_acquired`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU2 | `PresenceLogind::connect()` is awaited before every snapshot under `DBUS_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS` (1000 ms), outside `DBUS_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS` (500 ms): a connect slower than the call bound but within the connect bound proceeds, a hung connect ends the tick within the connect bound, any connect failure is `LogindUnavailable` with backoff before any snapshot call; only `ZbusLogind::connect` builds a bus connection; both bounds are documented in `presence/mod.rs` and `Docs/DAEMON.md` §6 | Integration tests (`presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_connect_slower_than_the_call_bound_is_still_awaited`, `presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_hung_connect_is_bounded_by_the_connect_timeout`, `presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_connect_failure_skips_before_the_snapshot`, `presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_connect_failure_restarts_the_grace`, `presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_connect_precedes_every_snapshot_and_is_skipped_without_templates`); invariant tests (`presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_only_connect_opens_a_bus_connection`, `presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_worker_bounds_connect_outside_the_call_bound`, `presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_logind_bounds_are_documented`, `presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_daemon_docs_describe_the_followups`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU3 | `/etc/pam.conf` (`DEFAULT_PAM_CONF`, injectable through `with_pam_conf`): present while no PAM directory is a directory ⇒ `Undeterminable`; present next to a PAM directory ⇒ scanned like a stack file (option, non-regular, unreadable, oversized or non-UTF-8 ⇒ `Undeterminable`; comment-only or option-free ⇒ usable); absent ⇒ unchanged behaviour; PAM directories recognised through symlinks; UID 0 refused first; never written | Integration tests (`presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_default_pam_conf_is_etc_pam_conf`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_absent_pam_conf_keeps_the_account_usable`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_pam_conf_without_any_pam_directory_is_undeterminable`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_non_directory_pam_paths_do_not_hide_pam_conf`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_any_pam_directory_switches_to_the_scan`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_pam_conf_next_to_pam_d_is_scanned_for_faillock_options`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_unscannable_pam_conf_is_undeterminable`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_pam_conf_path_under_a_regular_file_is_undeterminable`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_symlinked_pam_conf_is_followed`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_root_is_refused_before_pam_conf`, `presence_followups_pam_conf_tests::test_pfu_guard_never_writes_pam_conf`); invariant test (`presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_account_guard_documents_over_detection_and_pam_conf`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU4 | The PAM line scan is a superset of libpam: any policy-option substring after the first `pam_faillock.so` of a logical line is detected whatever the whitespace (Unicode included), brackets (unterminated included) or a `\` before a `#` comment; both round-1 counter-examples are detected; an `include` / `substack` / `@include` (ASCII case-insensitive) followed by any later token holding `/` is undeterminable while plain-name includes stay usable; the deliberate over-detection and the name-only module match are documented in `account.rs`; options before the module, other modules and comments stay undetected | Integration tests (`presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_detects_options_across_unicode_whitespace`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_backslash_before_comment_continues_the_line`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_unterminated_bracket_after_unicode_space_is_detected`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_line_after_a_commented_continuation_is_detected`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_include_of_a_path_is_detected`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_include_path_after_other_tokens_is_detected`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_searches_after_the_first_module_occurrence`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_plain_name_include_stays_usable`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_superset_keeps_negatives_undetected`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_pam_scan_matches_the_module_by_suffix`); invariant test (`presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_account_guard_documents_over_detection_and_pam_conf`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU5 | At most one account check is in flight: while a check blocks past `ACCOUNT_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS`, the next tick refuses the candidate (`AccountRefused`) at once without entering the guard a second time; the guard is entered again only after the first check ended | Integration test (`presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_second_account_check_never_runs_while_one_is_in_flight`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU6 | While the store holds no template (bounded, non-decrypting `BiometricStore::has_enrolled_template`, `MAX_ENROLLMENT_PROBE_ENTRIES` = 4096), a tick makes no D-Bus call (no connect, no snapshot) and skips with `NotEnrolled`; a store error skips with `TemplateStoreError` likewise; the probe never decrypts, ignores symlinks, directories, temporary and non-canonical names, is bounded (4096 ⇒ `false`, 4097 ⇒ error, missing directory ⇒ error) and read-only; it runs every tick (new enrollment picked up on the next tick) and skipping clears the lock tracker | Integration tests (`presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_no_logind_traffic_while_nobody_is_enrolled`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_new_enrollment_is_picked_up_on_the_next_tick`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_removed_enrollment_stops_polling_and_restarts_the_grace`, `presence_followups_tests::test_pfu_store_listing_error_skips_before_logind`, `presence_followups_connect_tests::test_pfu_connect_precedes_every_snapshot_and_is_skipped_without_templates`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_bound_is_4096_entries`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_is_false_for_an_empty_store`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_is_true_with_one_template_and_false_after_delete`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_ignores_entries_that_are_not_template_files`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_does_not_decrypt`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_is_bounded_by_max_entries`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_missing_directory_is_an_error`, `enrollment_probe_tests::test_pfu_probe_is_read_only`); invariant test (`presence_followups_contract::test_pfu_worker_probes_the_store_before_any_dbus_call`) | ✅ Verified |
| PFU7 | Diagnosis of the load-only flake of the 0 ms clamp test recorded (the cumulative deadline started before `connect` is the #231 / #312 PAM-parity contract, no production change); owner-approved assertion migration (option a): the clamp test accepts `Ok` or `Err(AdminCliError::Timeout)` only, the captured `deadline_monotonic_ns` assertion is unchanged and always runs (5 s receive bound); the 250 ms and oversized-timeout tests still require success | Integration test (`cli_deadline_json_tests::test_simulate_pam_auth_zero_timeout_is_clamped_to_pam_minimum`); walkthrough 176 | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `upgrade-procedure` (GitHub #327, `chore/upgrade-procedure`, walkthrough 177)

Documented and tested upgrade path of local installs: `scripts/install.sh` re-run, `.deb`, Arch and RPM upgrades keep the master key, the enrolled templates, `/etc/soos/daemon.toml` and the PAM activation state, and leave the daemon enabled and healthy (spec `AI/architect_spec_upgrade_procedure.md`).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| UPG1 | A re-run of `scripts/install.sh` over a live install keeps `/var/lib/soos/master.key`, an enrolled template (`/var/lib/soos/biometrics/4242.cbor.enc`), `/etc/soos/daemon.toml`, `/etc/pam.d`, `/var/lib/pam` and the PAM snapshot byte-identical, key mode `600` and `biometrics/` mode `700`, both owned by root (the group is re-owned to `soos` by systemd at each start) | Docker harness `tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh` part 6 (stage `upgrade`, CI job `systemd-unit`); invariant test (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_docker_harnesses_cover_upgrade`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG2 | After the re-run the unit is still enabled and an active daemon is restarted on the new binary (new `MainPID`, `/proc/<pid>/exe` is `/usr/libexec/soos/soos-daemon`, not `(deleted)`, byte-identical to the release build) and reports `is_healthy` — plain re-run and `--start` | Docker harness `tests/docker/systemd_unit_acceptance_test.sh` part 6 with models (`--models host`, CI `--models download`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG3 | `install.sh` records `is-enabled` / `is-active` before step 8, restarts an active unit (`systemctl restart`) before the readiness wait even without `--start`, and its rollback never disables a unit that was enabled before the run | Invariant tests (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_install_restarts_an_active_unit`, `upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_install_rollback_keeps_a_previously_enabled_unit`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG4 | `dpkg -i` of a version-bumped `.deb` over the installed package keeps key, template, `daemon.toml`, `/etc/pam.d` and `/var/lib/pam` byte-identical, with soos enabled and with soos disabled by the administrator | Docker harness `tests/docker/test_packages.sh` (Debian branch, CI job `package-deploy`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG5 | The `.deb` `postinst` never re-enables a profile disabled with `pam-auth-update --disable` (it only refreshes with `pam-auth-update --package`), and still runs `pam-auth-update --package --enable soos soos-notify` on a first install, after `dpkg -r` and when both profiles are enabled | Invariant tests (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_postinst_keeps_admin_disabled_profiles`, `upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_postinst_enables_profiles_on_first_install`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG6 | `pacman -U` and `rpm -U --replacepkgs` over the installed package keep key, template, `daemon.toml` and `/etc/pam.d` byte-identical | Docker harness `tests/docker/test_packages.sh` (Arch and RPM branches, CI job `distro-deploy` on push to `main`; run locally in `soos-distro-val-arch` and `soos-distro-val-fedora` on 2026-10-03) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG7 | README `## Upgrade`: commands per method (.deb, Arch, RPM, `install.sh --build`), `systemctl restart soos-daemon`, `soos-admin status`, `soos-enroll list`, re-enroll only for a foreign template | Invariant test (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_readme_documents_upgrade_per_method`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG8 | `Docs/PACKAGING_AND_PROVISIONING.md` §9: procedure, what is preserved and not preserved per method, switch from an `install.sh` install to a package | Invariant test (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_packaging_doc_documents_preservation_and_switch`) | ✅ Verified |
| UPG9 | The Docker harnesses keep the upgrade cases (systemd stage `upgrade`, `verify_upgrade_preserves_state` in the three package branches, version-bumped `.deb`) | Invariant test (`upgrade_procedure_contract::test_upg_docker_harnesses_cover_upgrade`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `install-readiness-presence-warmup` (GitHub #329, `fix/install-readiness-and-presence-warmup`, walkthrough 178)

Install readiness polls the daemon health within its bound, `install.sh --build` refuses a cargo target directory the build user cannot write, and a presence scan that wakes the camera never evaluates auto-exposure frames (ADR 2026-10-03 "Presence Wake Settle and Health-Polled Install Readiness"; spec `AI/architect_spec_install_presence_warmup.md` rev 3).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| IWP1 | `scripts/wait_daemon_ready.sh` re-queries `soos-admin status` every 0.5 s while it exits non-zero and succeeds at the first attempt that exits 0, within one `--timeout` bound shared with the socket wait | Invariant test (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_readiness_polls_until_status_is_healthy`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP2 | Only the final attempt's stdout is printed (one JSON document): the healthy report on success; on timeout the last report once, then a stderr error naming the bound and `journalctl`, exit 1 | Invariant tests (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_readiness_prints_only_the_final_status`, `install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_readiness_times_out_after_polling_with_last_status`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP3 | A hanging `soos-admin status` is killed per attempt (`timeout --kill-after=1 5`); the wait stays bounded and a killed attempt prints nothing | Invariant test (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_readiness_attempt_is_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP4 | Missing manifest (immediate), non-socket path (no query), usage errors (exit 2) and `install.sh`'s exit 70 mapping are unchanged | Invariant tests (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_install_keeps_exit_70_on_readiness_failure`, `installer_templates_contract::test_wait_daemon_ready_succeeds_when_socket_and_status_answer`, `installer_templates_contract::test_wait_daemon_ready_fails_closed_and_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP5 | `install.sh --build` over a target directory holding entries not owned by the build user fails in the read-only preflight (exit 2, `nothing was modified`) with `sudo chown -R <user>: <target>`, before `runuser`, `cargo` or any staged file; also under `--dry-run` | Invariant tests (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_build_refuses_foreign_owned_target_dir`, `install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_dry_run_build_reports_foreign_owned_target_dir`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP6 | A target directory owned by the build user but holding a non-writable directory fails the same way with `chmod -R u+w <target>` | Invariant test (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_build_refuses_non_writable_target_dir`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP7 | An owned target, an absent target, an unresolvable build user and a root build keep today's behaviour (the build runs; the frozen `--build` strings are unchanged) | Invariant tests (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_build_proceeds_with_an_owned_target_dir`, `packaging_ownership_contract::test_install_build_as_root_drops_to_invoking_user_or_refuses`, `arch_faillock_ci_contract::test_install_build_exports_the_prefix_bindir`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP8 | A presence scan that wakes the camera evaluates no capture (no PAD call, no inference) before `PRESENCE_WAKE_SETTLE_MS` after the wake, and unlocks when wake-time captures look like a spoof but settled captures are live (the 2026-10-03 trace) | Integration tests (`presence_wake_settle_tests::test_iwp_woken_scan_skips_captures_before_the_settle`, `presence_wake_settle_tests::test_iwp_woken_scan_unlocks_after_spoof_looking_wake_frames`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP9 | A spoof after the settle still vetoes a woken scan (no unlock, attempt spent); a constant spoof never unlocks a woken scan | Integration tests (`presence_wake_settle_tests::test_iwp_spoof_after_the_settle_still_vetoes`, `presence_wake_settle_tests::test_iwp_constant_spoof_never_unlocks_a_woken_scan`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP10 | A scan that finds the camera streaming (not woken) applies no settle | Integration test (`presence_wake_settle_tests::test_iwp_scan_of_a_streaming_camera_has_no_settle`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP11 | PAM path unchanged: a `compute`d window has `not_before_ns = 0` and `run_face_consensus` behaves as before; the dispatcher never references the settle; `PRESENCE_WAKE_SETTLE_MS = 1000` lives in `presence/mod.rs` only; `DAEMON_DEFAULT_WARMUP_FRAMES` stays 0 | Integration tests (`presence_wake_settle_consensus_tests::test_iwp_settle_constant_value`, `presence_wake_settle_consensus_tests::test_iwp_not_before_zero_is_the_pam_consensus`, `warmup_default_tests::test_daemon_default_warmup_frames_constant_is_zero`), invariant test (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_settle_is_presence_only`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP12 | `run_face_consensus` under `RequestDeadline::with_not_before`: captures stamped before the bound are never evaluated; a bound past the deadline gives `Pending` with 0 captures and 0 inferences (fail closed) | Integration tests (`presence_wake_settle_consensus_tests::test_iwp_not_before_skips_earlier_captures`, `presence_wake_settle_consensus_tests::test_iwp_not_before_past_the_deadline_fails_closed`) | ✅ Verified |
| IWP13 | `Docs/PACKAGING_AND_PROVISIONING.md` documents the health poll and the `chown` fix, `Docs/DAEMON.md` the presence wake settle, `AI/DECISIONS.md` the ADR of GitHub #329 | Invariant test (`install_presence_warmup_contract::test_iwp_docs_and_adr_describe_the_changes`) | ✅ Verified |

## Component: `install-gdm-followups` (GitHub #331, `fix/install-gdm-followups`, walkthrough 179)

GDM reuses a primary `pam_soos.so` rule already reached by its delegated stack instead of a second managed block, the presence settle is keyed on the camera stream start, and the install/readiness/status follow-ups of PR #330 are closed (ADR 2026-10-05 "GDM Reuses a Shared Primary soos Rule; Presence Settle Keyed on the Camera Stream Start; Install Readiness Follow-ups"; spec `AI/architect_spec_install_gdm_followups.md` incl. Revision 1).

| # | Criterion | Test Method | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| IGF1 | `install.sh --build` over a target directory holding a read-only file fails in the read-only preflight (exit 2) with `holds a file … cannot write` and `chmod -R u+w <target>`, before `cargo`/`runuser`; also under `--dry-run` | Invariant tests (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_build_refuses_read_only_file_in_target_dir`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_dry_run_build_reports_read_only_file_in_target_dir`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF2 | A user-owned unreadable directory (0000, 0300) is refused with `cannot read` and `chmod -R u+rwX <target>` instead of the former `sudo chown -R` advice, as a non-root runner and under fake root | Invariant tests (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_build_refuses_unreadable_dir_mode_0000_with_chmod_advice`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_build_refuses_unreadable_dir_mode_0300_with_chmod_advice`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_fake_root_build_refuses_unreadable_dir_with_chmod_advice`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF3 | `wait_daemon_ready.sh` header, `--help`, `README.md` and `Docs/PACKAGING_AND_PROVISIONING.md` document the real worst case `--timeout` + 7.5 s (37.5 s by default); a hanging admin with `--timeout 1` ends within that bound | Invariant tests (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_documents_real_worst_case_bound`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_hanging_admin_respects_documented_bound`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF4 | On timeout the helper prints the last non-empty `soos-admin status` stderr (`Last '…' error:`), bounded to 1024 characters with control characters printed as `?`; stdout unchanged | Invariant tests (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_timeout_reports_last_status_stderr`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_stderr_tail_replaces_control_characters`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_stderr_tail_is_bounded`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF5 | An `--admin` that is not executable (exit 126) or missing (exit 127) fails at once (exit 1, `Cannot run '<admin>'`, no JSON) instead of waiting for the whole timeout | Invariant tests (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_fails_fast_on_non_executable_admin`, `install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_readiness_fails_fast_on_missing_admin`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF6 | `presence_settle_window` returns `max(woke ? start + settle : 0, stream + settle)` with exact boundaries (stream age 1000 ms → no bound, 999 ms → 1 ms, 0 → 1000 ms), clamps a future stream stamp, treats `Some(0)` as `None`, rounds the wait up and saturates without panic; `PRESENCE_WAKE_SETTLE_MS` stays 1000 | Integration tests (`presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_settle_window_*`, 15 tests, e.g. `presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_settle_window_stream_age_999_ms_waits_1_ms`, `presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_settle_window_saturates_without_panic`, `presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_settle_window_properties_over_a_grid`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF7 | A presence scan of a camera whose stream started 300 ms earlier evaluates no capture before the settle ends and still unlocks with k = 3; a stream older than the settle gets no settle | Integration tests (`presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_scan_settles_a_stream_started_shortly_before`, `presence_stream_settle_tests::test_igf_scan_of_a_long_running_stream_has_no_settle`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF8 | `CameraManager::stream_started_mono_ns` defaults to `None`; the V4L supervisor stamps the stream set-up no later than the first frame, withdraws the stamp on suspend and renews it on resume; the mock setter round-trips regardless of readiness | Unit tests (`supervisor_tests::test_igf_stream_start_stamp_precedes_the_first_frame`, `supervisor_tests::test_igf_stream_start_stamp_is_withdrawn_on_suspend_and_renewed_on_resume`, `supervisor_tests::test_igf_stream_start_stamp_is_none_before_any_stream`), integration tests (`stream_start_stamp_tests::test_igf_default_stream_started_mono_ns_is_none`, `stream_start_stamp_tests::test_igf_mock_stream_started_defaults_to_none`, `stream_start_stamp_tests::test_igf_mock_stream_started_setter_round_trips_regardless_of_readiness`) | ✅ Verified |
| IGF9 | The stream-start settle is presence only: the dispatcher and consensus never name it, only `crates/daemon/src/presence/` reads `stream_started_mono_ns`, `DAEMON_DEFAULT_WARMUP_FRAMES` stays 0 | Invariant test (`install_gdm_followups_contract::test_igf_stream_start_settle_is_presence_only`) | ✅ Verified |
